Options for sites with their own design (bsw, julieedwards-info, txasm) on the shared site header, prose, tabs, sortable list, disclosure and time, with no change to how they look. Shared code is behavior; each site's look is its tokens and slots. Every new token defaults to today's value.

- **Chart builder reads its CSV with Prelum's `csvColumns`** (`@drdustinedwards/site-helpers/source`, 0.49.0), so the optional peer is now `>=0.49.0` and `csvColumns` is no longer exported from `capsomer/behavior/chart-builder`; import it from Prelum. Two local helpers with names Prelum also exports do different jobs and are renamed: `lineText` in the email part (now `plainLine`) and `sectionOf` in checklist-run (now `stepSection`). No token, class or HTML change.
- **The showcase site enforces Trusted Types.** site-runtime 0.132.0 (dev dependency) sets the capsomer registry entry's `trustedTypes` to `true`, so `_headers` and the Worker send `require-trusted-types-for 'script'` in the enforced Content-Security-Policy. No token, class or HTML contract changes.
- **Site header: tokens for a site's own bar.** `--header-max-inline-size` (default `100%`; bsw's 72rem column keeps the bar's contents inside it while the band runs full width; `--header-inset` is the computed side), `--header-link-weight` (400) and `--header-link-current-weight` (500), `--header-edge` (the bottom edge, default `--header-line`), `--header-translucent-mix` (85%) and `--header-blur` (12px) for `data-translucent`, and for the scrolled look `--header-scrolled-edge` (default `--header-edge`), `--header-scrolled-shadow` (`none`) and `--header-scrolled-mix` (default `--header-translucent-mix`). The height stays `--header-h`, set on `:root`.
- **Site header: spacing and icon tokens.** `--header-link-pad` (0), `--header-links-gap` (20px), `--header-link-gap` (`--space-2`) and `--header-chev-size` (12px) for the section links (the icon controls and the tools' gap are 0.99.1's `--header-icon-*` and `--header-tools-gap`). React `menuIcon` sets the menu button's glyph. The phone sheet: `--header-sheet-label-ink`, `--header-sheet-label-fs`, `--header-sheet-label-weight`, `--header-sheet-label-case`, `--header-sheet-label-tracking`, `--header-sheet-row-fs` and `--header-sheet-row-h`.
- **Site header: a `<picture>` logo keeps its place.** A mark in a `<picture class="contents">` no longer puts its `<source>`s in the logo's row, where each took a gap before the image (`.cap-site-header-logo source { display: none }`).
- **Prose: list and text tokens.** `--prose-list-indent` (1.5em) and `--prose-item-indent` (none) for lists; `--prose-wrap` (`pretty`), `--prose-numerals` (`oldstyle-nums`), `--prose-heading-tracking` (-0.015em), `--prose-heading-leading` (1.15) and `--prose-size-adjust` (`from-font`).
- **Tabs: weight and pill corners.** `--cap-tab-weight` (500), `--cap-tabs-tray-radius` and `--cap-tab-mark-radius` (both `--radius-full`).
- **Site header: `data-scrolled`.** The behavior (`enhance()`, and the React header when it hydrates) sets it once the page scrolls past 8px and removes it at the top. New exports `wireScrolled(header)` and `SCROLLED_AFTER_PX`.
- **Site header: `data-collapse="lg"`** (React `collapse="lg"`) moves the phone's row and sheet to under 1024px. The row's rules now sit in one container query (`@container cap-site-header-row`), the header named that container under 768px, or under 1024px with the attribute; the bottom bar stays under 768px.
- **Site header: slots.** `className` on an extra (an icon control, or the button extra in the bar and in the sheet), for the site's own look; `logo.name`, the logo link's accessible name where it says more than `label`.
- **Site header: no-script sheet.** With `hydrate={false}` the menu button carries `commandfor` and `command="show-modal"`, so the sheet opens before or without script where invoker commands exist; `enhance()` takes the click over (and cancels the command) and is the fallback elsewhere.
- **Site header: no empty parts.** `sheetIcons="auto"` (`data-sheet-icons="auto"` on the header) drops the sheet's 24px icon column where no row has an icon; by default every sheet keeps it, as before. The React header renders no `.cap-site-header-account` slot when there is neither an account nor `signIn` (it was hidden by `:empty` before, so nothing looks different).
- **Prose: tokens.** `--prose-heading-measure` (30ch), `--prose-heading-gap` (0.5em), `--prose-link-ink`, `--prose-link-hover-ink` and `--prose-link-hover-decoration` (the link system's by default). `css/prose.css` now also sets the prose context's face, size and leading and a text link's look, from the same tokens as `base.css`, so a site that cannot load `base.css` (Tailwind) reaches them; with `base.css` the values are the same.
- **Tabs: tokens.** `--cap-tab-ink` (the chosen tab's label, default `--text`), `--cap-tab-tone` (on a pill list, the chosen tab's tone, for `--cap-tab-ink`), `--cap-tab-min-inline-size` (default `--target`). tabs.md says the tab list must be a direct child of `.cap-tabs`.
- **Sortable list: a site's own rows.** Props `steps` (`"quiet"` by default, `"bordered"`, or `"labeled"` with the words Up and Down), `grip` (on by default; off, no drag handle), `itemClassName(item, state)`, `renderControls(item, state)` (after the steps and the move-to select, before Remove) and, on a board, `laneClassName`. Tokens: `--sortable-gap`, `--sortable-item-pad`, `--sortable-item-gap`, `--sortable-item-radius`, `--sortable-item-align`, `--sortable-card-pad`, `--sortable-card-radius`, `--sortable-item-border`, `--sortable-item-bg`, `--sortable-step-ink`, `--sortable-remove-ink`, `--sortable-remove-hover-ink`, `--sortable-remove-fs`, `--sortable-remove-weight`, `--sortable-lane-pad`, `--sortable-lane-border`, `--sortable-lane-radius`, `--sortable-lane-bg`.
- **Disclosure: the compact card's spacing and a triangle marker.** Tokens `--disclosure-pad`, `--disclosure-summary-pad`, `--disclosure-summary-min-h`, `--disclosure-body-pad`, `--disclosure-body-gap`; `data-marker="triangle"` (React `marker="triangle"`), a triangle at the start in place of the chevron or plus.
- **Time: `formatDate` styles `"weekday"`** ("Saturday, September 12") **and `"time"`** ("3:04 PM", with `zoneName` "3:04 PM UTC").
- **Empty state: the title is balanced** (`text-wrap: balance` on `.cap-empty-title`), as the state is; base.css's `pretty` on a `p` had overridden what it inherited.
- **`enhance(root)` of tabs and the site header takes `Document | Element`** (was `ParentNode`), so an element passes under the Workers types.

No token or class was removed or renamed. HTML contract additions only: `data-scrolled` and `data-collapse` on the header, `data-sheet-icons` on the header, `commandfor` and `command` on a no-hydration menu button, `data-marker="triangle"` on a disclosure; the account slot is left out when it would be empty, and a sortable item has no grip with `grip={false}`.

## 0.101.0

- **Series nav and related links: the furniture under a post is Capsomer's** (moved from dustinedwards.info, 2026-11-11). New `components/series-nav/` (`nav.cap-series` with `.cap-series-title`, `ol.cap-series-parts` and the manual's signs as `.cap-series-steps`; React `SeriesNav`, and `seriesPlace()` in `capsomer/behavior/series-nav`): where a post sits in its series, every part with this one marked `aria-current="page"`, and the parts either side as signs. New `components/related-links/` (`.cap-related` with `data-kind="internal|external|mentions"`, `.cap-related-title`, `ul.cap-related-list`, `.cap-related-name`, `.cap-related-excerpt`, `time.cap-related-date`; React `RelatedLinks`, and `httpHref()`, `sitePath()` and `longDate()` in `capsomer/behavior/related-links`): Further reading, Linked from and Mentions in one look; a path on this site is a router link. A mention's address is re-parsed and only http and https become a link, with `rel="nofollow ugc noopener noreferrer"`. The manual's signs get a React wrapper, `Signs` in `capsomer/react/manual`. No token or class was removed or renamed.

## 0.100.0

Batch 4 of the shared-components audit (C24 to C38, without C37): the parts the sites and the Portal built for themselves, now in Capsomer. Nothing is removed or renamed; every HTML contract change below is an addition.

- **Admin shell** (C24): `isCollapseKey`, `currentEntryId` (the longest match; an entry's `match` takes prefixes or RegExps), `ACCESS_LOGOUT`. The shell binds `[` itself (`collapseKey`), and `pathname` sets the current entry and the page. New `AdminErrorView`; `data-width="narrow|page"` on the page.
- **Error page** (C30): `actions`, `gone` (a 410 is an Empty with one way out) and `hero`. New `SignedOutPage`.
- **Panel and read state** (C25, C26): a panel with no title is a bare surface; new `meta`, `moreHref`, `reading` (Reading again, `aria-busy`) and `href`; new `TablePanel`. New read-state component (`ReadState`, `ReadAgain`, `SourceState`) and `Missing`. Bulk bar: selection helpers and `useBulkSelection`.
- **New components** (C27, C28, C29): delta, source line, status mark with `ResultPage`, mode banner (demo, view as), consent (`PermissionList`, `ConsentScreen`, `ConsentRefusal`), setup checklist. The row list gets a static row, read and not opened.
- **Sortable groups** (C31): covered by 0.92.0's lists that share a `data-group` (`SortableBoard`, `moveBetween`, `keyMove`), so this release adds no second groups component.
- **Menus, popover, toolbar** (C32): FormMenu takes an outer `form` and `size="sm"`. New `openAt(popup, anchor)` opens a popover at a virtual anchor (a text selection), and `place` takes any box. `attachToolbar(bar, items, { keepFocus })` keeps an editor's caret and selection; it also works where native `focusgroup` runs the bar.
- **Command menu** (C33): `useCommandHost` (an idle host and `runWhenReady`), `navCommands`, recents, snippets with their matches marked, Ask, and full screen on a phone.
- **Uptime strip** (C34): `fromRing`, `padRing`, `uptimeOf` and a React `UptimeLegend`.
- **Disclosure** (C35): `data-variant="inline"` (no box, a rule above and below, the title in the link color: a post's "Show the data", a step's calculator) and `data-marker="sign"` (a mono plus and minus that do not move the title). Every section, group and expand region is open on paper; `data-print="hide"` leaves a screen-only section off. New expand toggle (`.cap-expand-toggle`, `data-cap="expand"`, `data-open-label`/`data-closed-label`; React `useExpand`, `ExpandToggle`) and table detail row (`.cap-expand-row`, `tr.cap-expand-detail`; React `ExpandRows`). `enhance()` also wires the toggles (`enhanceExpand`).
- **Pagination** (C36): `parsePage` (whole digits from 1, else page 1), `pageEnvelope(page, perPage, total)` (the page kept in range, offset, range, `hasPrev`/`hasNext`; `"all"` for one page), `pageHref(path, params, page)` (empty parameters and page 1 left out), `pageRelLinks` for the head's prev and next, and `countText` ("Showing 20 of 312 mentions"). React: `renderLink` for a router link (`PageLinkProps`), and `form="steps"` (Previous, "Page 3 of 16", Next at every width; with `pageCount={0}` and `hasNext`, "Page 3" for a total that moves). The pure functions are re-exported from `capsomer/react/pagination`.
- **From the Portal** (C38): new verdict (`Verdict`: "All good" with what it covers, or the problems by name and "and 2 more"; `unknown` is never all good), log view (`LogView`: raw lines in mono that wrap, counts in tabular figures, a note when the source cut the list, a stated None), status matrix (`StatusMatrix` and `MatrixKey`: rows against columns, each cell a glyph named with its row, column, status and why; the row headers stick) and config form (`ConfigForm`, `useConfigForm`, `useEditor`: focus in the first field, a hint rather than a disabled submit, Preview and Cancel, Esc cancels, a Danger area; one editor per list with a fresh draft per row). The row list gets a `reply` slot and `RowReply` (focus in the box, an announced hint, Ctrl or Cmd+Enter sends outside an IME composition, Esc cancels).
- Fixes found on the way: the popover specimens' open states no longer stop when the virtual-anchor script cannot find its button.

Not in this release: HTML-only forms of the command menu's new extras, and pairing site-runtime's mcp-login consent style with the consent component.

## 0.99.1

- **A page that hydrates after `enhance()` no longer fails hydration for a dark-theme visitor.** On a header rendered with `hydrate={false}`, `enhance()` renames the theme button and its tip to the theme showing ("Switch to light theme"), so when such a page hydrated later (germomics' hydrate on intent) React found text that differed from the server's and threw error #418. The button and the tip's text now carry `suppressHydrationWarning` while `hydrate` is false.
- **New: `capsomer/react/player-context`** (`PlayerProvider`, `usePlayer`). Importing the provider from `capsomer/react/player` in an app's root put the whole player (the dock, its menus and its sheet, about 50 KB gzip) into every page's first load once the dock was loaded lazily, since the bundler keeps the barrel and all it re-exports together; the root imports the provider from here and loads `PlayerDock` with `lazy()` when a track is loaded. `capsomer/react/player` still exports both.
- **The site header takes a site's own icon controls, order and divider.** New custom properties, each defaulting to today's look: `--header-icon-size` (36px), `--header-icon-glyph` (18px), `--header-icon-stroke` (1.6) and `--header-icon-bg` (none) for every icon control and the theme button's glyph; `--header-tools-gap` for the tools and the extras; `--header-sep: none` drops the divider before the tools; `--header-cta-order: -1` puts the button extra before the icon extras and `--header-theme-order: -1` the theme first among the tools; and the phone's menu button takes `--header-menu-size`, `--header-menu-glyph`, `--header-menu-line` and `--header-menu-bg`. From germomics, whose header keeps its 34px filled circles, 16px glyphs and Subscribe-first order on Capsomer's header.
- **Removed: `toParagraphs` from `capsomer/player`.** The transcript groups its cues with Prelum's `toParagraphs` (`@drdustinedwards/site-helpers/vtt`, 0.47.0), the same rule (a paragraph ends at a sentence's end after 45 words, or at a pause over 2.5 seconds), so the optional peer `@drdustinedwards/site-helpers` is now `>=0.47.0`. Import `toParagraphs` from Prelum instead.
- No token or class was removed or renamed.

## 0.99.0

Shared components from the network audit: parts each site wrote for itself, now in Capsomer so every site behaves the same.

- **New: copy** (`components/copy`, `capsomer/behavior/copy`, `capsomer/react/copy`). A button that copies another element's text, or shares a link, with one `role="status"` bubble beside it for Copied and Copy failed. Invoker Commands (`command="--copy"` or `"--share"`, `commandfor`) with a click fallback, never disabled while its message shows, a no-break space so VoiceOver reads a repeat, and a cancelled share says nothing. `.cap-copyable` reveals the button over repeated blocks without hiding it from the keyboard. React: `CopyButton`, `useCopy`. Built to Modern Web Guidance `copy-button`; it replaces the copy code in germomics, Foxhound, dustinedwards.info and the Capsid Portal as they adopt it.
- **New utility `.cap-tap-target`** (`css/utilities.css`): a hit area at least `--target` in each direction that can still grow. It replaces germomics' own `tap-target`.
- **The media library, the detail panel and the posts list copy through it.** Removed from `capsomer/behavior/media`: `copyText`, `COPIED_MS` and `COPY_FAILED_MS` (import them from `capsomer/behavior/copy`). The `execCommand("copy")` fallback is gone: sites are served over HTTPS, and a refused write now says it failed and selects the text, as before. A repeated detail-panel status is announced again.
- **New: card** (`components/card`, `capsomer/card.css`). One piece of content or one choice: an `<article class="cap-card">` whose picture, heading, text and footer are direct children, placed by element (no wrapper classes). A whole card that goes one place is `<a><article>`; a card with its own actions stretches its heading link (`.cap-card-link`); a choice is `<label class="cap-card">` around a radio or checkbox. `data-ratio`, `data-layout="side"` (a container query on the card), and `.cap-card-grid` with `data-align="rows"` (subgrid). Built to Modern Web Guidance `card`.
- **New: carousel** (`components/carousel`, `capsomer/behavior/carousel`, `capsomer/react/carousel`). A native scroll-snap track with Previous, Next, one marker per slide and a polite "Slide N of M" after a scroll settles. Loops with three slides or more by parking an end slide with CSS `order` (`sibling-count()`), never cloning; finite otherwise. `data-per-view`, `data-peek`, `data-axis="block"`. Built to Modern Web Guidance `carousel`, from bsw's testimonials.
- **New: marquee** (`components/marquee`, `capsomer/react/marquee`). A seamless drifting row of marks with a visible Pause checkbox that works without script (WCAG 2.2.2), paused by hover and focus, still and wrapped under reduced motion; the second copy is `aria-hidden` and `inert`. From bsw's logo rows.
- **New: reading progress** (`components/reading-progress`). A bar at the top of the window filling through one `[data-cap-reading]` article, CSS only (a view timeline), decoration: no bar where scroll-driven animations are missing or less motion is asked for. Built to Modern Web Guidance `scroll-progress-indicator`.
- **New: scroll area** (`components/scroll-area`). A fixed-height box that scrolls, its thin scrollbar at 3:1 (WCAG 1.4.11), full contrast under `prefers-contrast: more`, legacy pseudo-elements only under `@supports not (scrollbar-color: auto)`. Built to Modern Web Guidance `adapt-scrollbar-to-contrast-preferences`.
- **New: reveal** (`components/reveal`). `.cap-reveal` and `.cap-reveal-stack` fade sections in as they enter, CSS only (a view timeline). Content is never hidden without it, and never moves under reduced motion.
- **Site header: clear at rest.** `data-rest="clear"` (React `rest="clear"`) with a first child `<span class="cap-site-header-ground" aria-hidden="true">`: no ground or line at the top of the page, the band once stuck, by a `scroll-state(stuck: top)` query with no scroll listener. Where that query is missing the band always shows. Built to Modern Web Guidance `state-aware-sticky-headers`.
- **New: hooks** (`capsomer/react/hooks`, `capsomer/behavior/hooks`). `useHydrated`, `ClientOnly`, `useMedia`, `usePrefersReducedMotion`, `useBrowserZone`, `useRemembered` (localStorage with a page-long fallback, every reader follows, `null` forgets) and `useNow`, all through `useSyncExternalStore` with a server snapshot so hydration matches; `cn` for class names. They replace the copies in Foxing, Carrel, germomics and the Capsid Portal.
- **New: React picture** (`capsomer/react/picture`). site-runtime's `pictureProps` as a `<picture class="cap-picture">` (display: contents) with its sources, or the `<img>` alone; `priority`, `eager`. Replaces the local `Picture` in six sites.
- **New: `routerRenderLink`** (`capsomer/react/link`). The `renderLink` every Capsomer part takes, as React Router's `Link` with Capsomer's prefetch, so no site writes its own adapter.
- **New: icons** (`components/icons`, `capsomer/behavior/icons`, `capsomer/react/icons`). The network's icons on one 24-unit grid: UI icons stroked in the text's color, and the Facebook, X, Instagram and LinkedIn marks filled. `Icon` (React) and `iconSvg` (DOM, `createElementNS`, no markup string) are `aria-hidden` unless given a label; `--icon-size`, `data-flip` for right to left. They replace the inline SVGs in txasm's admin menu, Foxing's account panel and header, julieedwards.info's header and the germomics and bsw footers.
- **New layout utilities** (`css/utilities.css`): `.cap-stack`, `.cap-cluster`, `.cap-spread` and `.cap-grid` (as many columns of at least `--grid-min` as fit, at most `data-cols` 2, 3 or 4, with no media query), a gap from the space scale with `data-gap="1"`..`"9"` that a nested layout never inherits, `data-align`, `data-justify`, `.cap-push` and `.cap-measure`. They replace the stack, cluster, spread, row and grid classes in Foxing, Carrel, txasm and julieedwards.info.
- **Combobox: options from a server, and free text.** `search(query, signal)` lists what a server returns as the person types, after `searchDelay` (200 ms) and from `minSearchLength` (1); a newer keystroke aborts the older request, loading reads "Searching", a failure says so and never says no match, and the chosen label stays. New `ComboboxFreeText` (Base UI Autocomplete): the typed text is the value, suggestions from `items` or `search`, and `onPick` fills what comes with a suggestion. An option may carry a muted `hint`. `ComboboxFreeText` replaces txasm's institution autocomplete.
- **Carousel:** the slides' width no longer widens a phone's page (one `minmax(0, 1fr)` column).
- **React picture** names the ratio from the file's width and height in the image's style, as the sites' own copies did (no LazyLoadImage issue in Chrome).
- **Command menu: live results without React.** `createCommandMenu(commands, { search, searchDelay })` and `attach(dialog, run, { search })` take the React menu's `search(query, signal)`: asked 180 ms after the last key, the one before aborted (and on close), results after the matching commands, the list `aria-busy` and "Searching…" until they come. `run(option, found)` gets a live result's command. It replaces the live search Foxing wrote around `attach` (search-palette.ts) and is what dustinedwards.info's palette moves onto.
- **Player transcript: Copy with link copies through the copy part,** so a refused clipboard now says Copy failed.
- **Save toggle keeps its list through `capsomer/behavior/hooks`** (`readStored`, `writeStored`, `subscribeStored`): where storage is blocked the button still toggles and the list lasts the page. The key and the stored JSON are unchanged.
- **Tabs remember through `capsomer/behavior/hooks`.** The remembered tab sets keep their choice with the hooks' `readStored`, `writeStored` and `subscribeStored` instead of their own copies; the storage key and behavior are unchanged.

## 0.98.0

- **Batch 8 of the shared-components audit: specialized components** (clusters C73 to C85). Each was extracted from a site that had its own copy; the sites move onto this release and delete theirs. Every new component has a doc page, a states page and its specs.
- **Dictionary entry** (from dustinedwards.info's named software pages). New `.cap-dictionary-entry`: a headword with its syllables, IPA and respelling, part of speech and plural, numbered senses with subject labels, and the etymology between two rules. The speaker is a link to the clip with no script and a button with it (`aria-pressed` while playing), and a clip that cannot load or play brings the link back. `capsomer/behavior/dictionary-entry` (`enhance`, `wireListen`, `listenLabel`) and `DictionaryEntry` in React. No token or class was removed or renamed.
  - `DictionaryEntry` sets `data-cap-ready` in an effect, not in its server HTML, so a page that renders it on the server and never hydrates it (an islands page) calls `enhance()` and the speaker becomes a button.
- **Calculator** (from dustinedwards.info's lab calculators). New `.cap-calculator`: a form drawn from a field list beside a result drawn from a plain view tree (`resultView`, `ViewNode`, tags whitelisted), rendered on the server from the address so it works with no script, and recomputed on each input by the same `run` function the site passes in. The answer sits in a status region and the address follows the values unless `data-embedded`. `capsomer/behavior/calculator` (`enhance(root, runs)`, `attach`, `initialValues`, `textParts`) and `Calculator` in React. No token or class was removed or renamed.
  - The form is in the UI face (`--ui`) inside prose too. `enhance(root)` takes `Element | Document | DocumentFragment`, so a form passes under `@cloudflare/workers-types`.
- **Scale control** (from dustinedwards.info's procedure scaler). New `.cap-scale-control`: a quantity with Field's steppers, an optional unit Select and a button, as a plain GET that rescales the page it sits on. `ScaleControl` in React. No token or class was removed or renamed.
  - `step` may be `"any"` (1.5 servings passes the browser's check; the steppers step by 1). The control is in the UI face inside prose too, and hidden in print.
- **Annotated figure** (from dustinedwards.info's Plate I). New `figure.cap-annotated-figure` and `.cap-annotated-key`: a drawing with numbered callouts on leader lines, in a wide and a narrow layout chosen by the figure's width, and a key of ruled boxes. A callout and its key entry light together (`data-lit`) under a mouse and pin on a tap, and leaders can draw themselves in (`data-draw`, none under reduced motion). `capsomer/behavior/annotated-figure` adds the geometry helpers (`sideCallout`, `scalePoint`, `scalePathData`, `viewBox`), and React has `AnnotatedFigure`, `AnnotatedDrawing` and `AnnotatedKey`. No token or class was removed or renamed.
  - The labels are `--fs-detail` in both layouts, in place of a fixed 12px. `enhance(root)` takes `Element | Document | DocumentFragment`.
- **QR code** (from dustinedwards.info's procedure sheet). New `.cap-qr`: an SVG drawn from the module matrix the site's encoder returns (`qrPath`), black on white with a quiet zone in every theme and under forced colors, named "QR code for {url}", with the address as text under it. Capsomer carries no encoder. `capsomer/behavior/qr` and `Qr` in React. No token or class was removed or renamed.
  - The caption is in the UI face inside prose too.
- **Author list** (new, `capsomer/author-list.css`, `capsomer/react/author-list`, `capsomer/behavior/author-list`), from dustinedwards.info's publications. A byline with the owner's name marked by weight and color. `collapse` folds a long list into a native `details`, "A, B, ..., Owner and 5 more", so the owner is always in view. Names may link, in the full list only. No token or class was removed or renamed.
- **Cited by** (new, `capsomer/cited-by.css`, `capsomer/react/cited-by`), from dustinedwards.info's paper page. `CitedByCount`: "Cited by 12" with its index and read date, drawn only at 1 or more. `CitedBy`: the citing works under "Cited by N of M", titles linked through doi.org. No token or class was removed or renamed.
- **Record facts** (new, `capsomer/record-facts.css`, `capsomer/react/record-facts`), from dustinedwards.info's paper rail. A record's labeled facts as a description list, drawn value first with its label under it, identifiers in mono. A fact with no value is left out. No token or class was removed or renamed.
- **Draft notice** (new, `capsomer/draft-notice.css`, `capsomer/react/draft-notice`), from six dustinedwards.info routes. "Draft: Only you can see this page.", with "Draft" in a dashed box on the warning tint. `show={false}` draws nothing. Who may see a draft is decided on the server (site-runtime's `draftGate`). No token or class was removed or renamed.
- **JSON-LD** (new, `capsomer/react/json-ld`), from twelve dustinedwards.info routes. `<JsonLd data={node} />` writes one `application/ld+json` block, escaped by Prelum's `jsonLdText` and set through the `capsomer` Trusted Types policy. React's own client creation of a `script` element still needs a `default` policy under enforced Trusted Types (see its page). No token or class was removed or renamed.
- **Trusted HTML: `RichText` and a doc page** (`capsomer/react/trusted-html`, new `capsomer/trusted-html.css`), from txasm's 28 `dangerouslySetInnerHTML` of stored, sanitized HTML. It renders through the `capsomer` policy and takes `as`, `className`, `fallback` for empty HTML, and `measure` (`data-measure="text"`, `--measure`) in place of an inline max-width. It never renders a `style`. `trusted-html.ts`'s exports are unchanged. No token or class was removed or renamed.
- **Link: `capsomer/link-file.css`**, split out of link.css, which imports it: the `file` kind's down arrow, the rule that draws an after mark, and `.cap-link-meta`. A site loads it alone to style a component's file link and meta without marks on every other link. A site that loads link.css changes nothing. No token or class was removed or renamed.
- **Plain form: `capsomer/react/plain-form`** (new module): `PlainForm` and the `FormComponent` type, moved out of `capsomer/react/content` with no re-export left there, so a component that takes a router form loads nothing else. Change `import type { FormComponent } from "capsomer/react/content"` to `"capsomer/react/plain-form"`. `FormComponent` is narrowed to what React Router's `Form` and `fetcher.Form` take (`action` a string, `encType` one of the three form encodings), so they pass as `Form={Form}` with no cast. Media, posts list, mentions list and media library use it. No token or class was removed or renamed.
- **Field.** `size="short"` writes `data-size="short"` on the `.cap-field`, for a layout that sets field widths (the inline add form). `NumberField` takes `step="any"`; its steppers and Page Up and Page Down step by 1 there, and a stepped value keeps its own decimals (1.5 steps to 2.5). Field's and Select's `enhance(root)` take `Element | Document | DocumentFragment` (a form typed under `@cloudflare/workers-types` was not a `ParentNode`); editor workspace, draft compare, moderation queue and dictionary entry do too. No token or class was removed or renamed.
- Schedule (`capsomer/schedule.css`, `capsomer/behavior/schedule`, `capsomer/react/schedule`), new: a program by day and time slot, with sessions that overlap in time as named parallel tracks that fit side by side or stack, and a day nav of links. `groupByDay`, `dayRange`, `dayKeys`, `pickDay`, `dayLabel` and `slotLabel` are pure and take the meeting's zone. Extracted from txasm's public program, program builder and printed program. No token or class was removed or renamed.
  - `year` on `Schedule` and `ScheduleDayNav` says each day with its year ("Saturday, March 6, 2027"), for a past meeting's program. Off by default.
- Generated file (`capsomer/generated-file.css`, `capsomer/behavior/generated-file`, `capsomer/react/generated-file`), new: a panel for a file the server makes. It says Not made yet, Generating, Ready, Out of date or Failed with its reason, offers the file with its format and size, and has the Generate or Regenerate button. The lifecycle is the pure `fileState`, and `mayStart` is the server's 5 minute lock rule. Extracted from txasm's program document and book PDF panels. No token or class was removed or renamed.
  - `generate={false}` leaves out the Generate form, for a file another step makes (a book PDF made on publish), and the stale and failed sentences then ask for nothing. A record's `status` may be `"pending"`, read as not made. The panel loads `capsomer/link-file.css` (or `capsomer/link.css`) for its file link's mark and meta.
- Document archive (`capsomer/document-archive.css`, `capsomer/behavior/document-archive`, `capsomer/react/document-archive`), new: past documents by category as row-list rows, and a document's page with its download, the app's viewer, and Previous and Next that show the neighbor's title. Includes `adjacent` and `groupByCategory`. Extracted from txasm's archive routes. No token or class was removed or renamed.
  - `.cap-docview-title`, the document page's h1, has the page head title's size and face, so a site needs no rule of its own. The download's meta needs `capsomer/link-file.css` (or `capsomer/link.css`).
- **Approval queue** (new): things your own side drafted, waiting for a person to approve them one by one, drawn on the moderation queue's markup with a generic `ApprovalItem`. Each item has its status, its context, its findings and its editable text, and its decisions are one form. With script, `j` and `k` move, `a` and `r` approve and reject, and a one-way decision asks first (from Carrel's social queue). The moderation queue's stylesheet gains `.cap-mq-findings`, `.cap-mq-edit` and `.cap-mq-decide`. No token or class was removed or renamed.
- **Writing progress** (new): today's words against the target (the meter), the streak, a deadline's pace, the last seven days as an Enarratio bar chart and the writer's goal form, with the pure goal, streak and untyped-tally math in `capsomer/behavior/writing-progress` (from Carrel's book page). No token or class was removed or renamed.
- **Inline add form** (new): one line at the foot of a list that starts a new thing by name. A field or two and one button, posting a form, wrapping in a narrow column, with `data-size="short"` for a select (from Carrel's `.app-inline-form`). No token or class was removed or renamed.
  - A short field is `<Field size="short">` (Field's new `size`).
- **Label color list** (new): a person's own ordered labels, each a word with a `--series-1` to `--series-8` dot. Includes the list, a value as the list names it (shown as written when it is not on the list), the editor's rows, and `cleanLabels`/`labelFor`/`editRows` (from Carrel's status lists). No token or class was removed or renamed.
  - Split: `LabelColorEditor` moved to `capsomer/react/label-color-editor`, so a page that shows labels (`ColorLabelPill`, `LabelColorList` from `capsomer/react/label-color-list`) loads no Field or Select.
- **Not-found report** (new): the paths a site was asked for and does not have, most hits first, with referrers, last seen, a 7/30-day window, an action per row, and probes counted but not listed. One component for Carrel's 404s page and the Capsid Portal's missing pages. No token or class was removed or renamed.
- **AI job buttons** (new): small buttons that each post one AI job, the answer as an alert with a link to the draft, and the same job on the other engine one button away. It is router-agnostic: pass a fetcher's `Form`, `data` and state (from Carrel's `AiWriteButtons`). No token or class was removed or renamed.
- **AI engine settings** (new): which engine runs each AI job. Shows what each engine can do now, one table of each job's engine and API model saved with the monthly cap, and the month's spend by job, plus `settingsFromForm` for the action (from Carrel's AI engine page; the engine logic stays in site-runtime). No token or class was removed or renamed.
  - `models` takes a partial map, as site-runtime's settings reader returns it.
- **Frontmatter fields** (new): a file's frontmatter as one labeled box per key (line, text, list "Separated by commas.", one per line, checkbox) typed from its value or the app's `FieldSpec`s, with the raw text one disclosure away naming the keys only it holds; pure `typeFront`/`setBox` on Prelum's frontmatter writer, so untouched keys come back byte for byte (from Carrel's FrontmatterFields and HeaderFields). No token or class was removed or renamed.
- **Chart builder** (new): the insert-a-chart dialog, from CSV pasted or chosen, type, x and y columns, title, finding and caption, with the app's check, an offered drafted finding, a drawing in the chart frame and the site's render in a sandboxed frame; Prelum's `:::chart` block by default, the syntax the app's `write` (from Carrel's ChartInsert). No token or class was removed or renamed.
- **Editor workspace** (new): Write, Split and Preview remembered in this browser, two panes side by side from a 48rem workspace, the site's preview in an empty-sandbox frame refreshed once typing pauses (`usePausedStamp`, `previewUrl`), `useSaveShortcut` and `useTypingRecede` (`html[data-cap-typing]`, `data-cap-recede`), with a framework-free `enhance`, `onSaveKey` and `typingRecede` (from Carrel's editor). No token or class was removed or renamed.
- **Schedule dialog** (new): a `datetime-local` field in the Dialog named by the browser's time zone, the chosen time said back with the Time component, a past time refused beside the field, and an ISO time sent on confirm; pure `localValue(at, zone)` and `checkSchedule` (from Carrel's ScheduleDialog). No token or class was removed or renamed.
- **Share preview: `SerpWidthMeter`**, a Meter of a search title's or description's pixel width against the cut, on Prelum's `serpWidth`, so the editor and `prelum-audit` agree and a server render shows the number; `measure` takes an app's own (from Carrel's WidthMeter). No token or class was removed or renamed.
- **Draft compare: `unifiedPatch(name, before, after)` and `hasChanges(patch)`**, a unified patch in jsdiff's `createTwoFilesPatch` shape from the component's own line diff, so an app no longer needs the `diff` library for DraftPatch (from Carrel's lib/patch.ts). No token or class was removed or renamed.

## 0.97.0

- **The page helpers need no test runner: `capsomer/testing/page`.** `countCssRules`, `settled`, `paintedContrast` (and its `Reading`), `focused`, `nextPost`, `ringContrast` and `rowOverflow` each run one function in the page, so they move to their own module, typed for any page with `evaluate` (`PageLike`: Playwright's or Puppeteer's). A site whose browser checks run on Puppeteer imports them without installing Playwright or axe; `capsomer/testing` still exports every one.
- **Catalog: the search waits while an IME composes a word.** The search box fetched results on every `input` event, and an IME (Japanese, Chinese and Korean input, and many phone keyboards) sends those while a word is still being composed, so the catalog fetched half-typed syllables. It now skips an input event whose `isComposing` is true and searches on `compositionend` (UI Events, Composition Events). Found in the second shared-code audit, 2026-11-11. No token or class was removed or renamed.

## 0.96.0

Options so a site keeps its own look on the shared parts (Foxhound, moving its stat cards, badges and blog lists onto Capsomer without a visible change).

- **Static stat tile: look tokens, and `ok` colors the figure.** New custom properties, each defaulting to the family's look: `--tile-figure-face`, `--tile-figure-weight`, `--tile-label-face`, `--tile-label-size`, `--tile-label-weight`, `--tile-label-case`, `--tile-label-tracking`, `--tile-icon-flow`, `--tile-icon-align`, `--tile-icon-size`, `--tile-gap`, `--tile-detail-space`, `--tile-pad`, `--tile-radius`, `--tile-shadow` (with 0.92.0's `--tile-figure-size`), and for a grid row `--tiles-columns` and `--tiles-gap`. A static tile with `data-tone="ok"` now draws its figure in `--ok`, as `warn` and `crit` do; a static tile has no word beside the figure, so its label and note say what the color means. Link and button tiles are unchanged.
- **Badge: `variant="tag"` and `glyph={false}`.** A tag is the tone's tint with an edge in the word's own color (or a hairline label with no tone, or a family's accent with `data-family`), set in the site's tag face: `--tag-face`, `--tag-size`, `--tag-weight`, `--tag-case`, `--tag-tracking`, `--tag-edge`. `glyph={false}` leaves the glyph out of a toned badge, for a site whose badges are words alone; the word must then say the state. Type change: `PillProps.glyph` is `GlyphName | false`, and `BadgeVariant` gains `"tag"`.
- **Prose blocks: list tokens.** `--prose-bullets` and `--prose-numbers` set the list-style of a plain and a numbered list (disc, circle and decimal by default), and `--prose-item-space` the space after each item (0.3em).

No token or class was removed or renamed.

## 0.95.1

- **Rich editor:** an image inserted from the editor uses the markdown editor's `defaultImageMarkdown`. The editor had its own copy, which kept a line break in the alt text and so broke the Markdown image (as Carrel fixed in its copy after 0.95.0 was cut). No token, class or HTML contract changed.

## 0.95.0

- **Rich editor (new, `components/rich-editor`)**, from Carrel's formatted editor: Tiptap 3 (MIT) over Markdown that stays byte for byte. Unchanged blocks are written as the bytes they were read from; only a changed or new block is spelled again, and checked by parsing it. Front matter, code, tables, directives, math, footnotes and images sit in labeled source blocks. It keeps the markdown editor's toolbar, keys, link palette, block menu and image step. `capsomer/react/rich-editor` (`RichEditor`), `capsomer/behavior/rich-editor` (the round trip and schema, for a server), `capsomer/rich-editor.css`. A pasted or dropped HTML fragment is parsed in an inert document under the `capsomer` Trusted Types policy and read by the schema; ProseMirror's own paste handed it to the page's default policy, which escapes markup, so formatted pastes arrived as tags. New command `capsomer-roundtrip <folder> [--plain]` checks a site's content round trips.
- **Collab editor (new, `components/collab-editor`)**, from Carrel's file rooms: a shared Yjs copy over y-partyserver, each person's caret in their series color, Editing or Suggesting with tracked changes (who, round, when), comment threads anchored to passages, the room bar, and the suggestions and comments panels. `capsomer/react/collab-editor` (`useRoomEditing`, `useRichRoom`, `useRoom`, `RoomBar`, `SuggestionsPanel`, `CommentsPanel`), `capsomer/behavior/collab-editor` (load, patch, save and settle the copy, for the room's server), `capsomer/collab-editor.css`. The room's server, who may enter and storage stay in the app.
- The rich editor's link palette and image alt field ignore the Enter that confirms an IME conversion (`isImeEnter`, from 0.90.0).
- **Markdown editor:** `GLYPH` (its toolbar icons) is exported for the rich editor. **Trusted HTML:** new `inertDocument(html)`.
- New optional peers, at Carrel's versions: `@tiptap/core`, `@tiptap/extension-collaboration`, `@tiptap/extension-list`, `@tiptap/extensions`, `@tiptap/pm`, `@tiptap/starter-kit` (^3.31.4), `@tiptap/y-tiptap` (^3.3.0; 3.0.x is deprecated upstream because its position migrations grow shared documents, so every client in a room needs 3.1.0 or later), `@handlewithcare/prosemirror-suggest-changes` (^0.1.8), `yjs`, `y-protocols`, `y-partyserver`, and the micromark and mdast packages the round trip reads with. `micromark-extension-math` gets katex 0.19.1 by override (its own range, ^0.16, has GHSA-238p-pmpm-9mq7; the round trip reads math syntax only and never renders it). Dev: site-runtime v0.114.0 (`startPage` in the specimens). No token or class was removed or renamed.

## 0.94.0

- **Seventeen page and app widgets move into Capsomer** (the shared-code audit's item 31; Dustin, 2026-11-11: a piece of UI that is a component belongs in Capsomer, even when only one site uses it so far). Each is a component with its CSS, notes, specimen and tests, extracted from the site that had it; the sites switch to them and delete their copies next.
  - `range-slider` (two-handle range, from Foxhound's payments filter): `.cap-range-slider` and its parts, each handle a field's `input.cap-range`, `capsomer/behavior/range-slider` (`rangeFill`, `clampPair`, `formatValue`, the `cap:range-commit` event), React `RangeSlider`.
  - `toc` (a post's contents, sidebar from 64rem and inline below, from Foxhound's blog): `.cap-toc`, React `Toc`.
  - `share-row` (share links plus a copy button, from Foxhound's blog): `.cap-share`, `shareHref`, React `ShareRow`.
  - `route-splash` (a slow navigation's loading screen, from Foxhound's app shell): `.cap-route-splash`, `shouldShowRouteSplash`, React `RouteSplash`, now `role="status"`.
  - `count-up` (a number that counts up once in view, from Foxhound's audit): `capsomer/behavior/count-up`, `.cap-count-up`, React `useCountUp`.
  - `timeline` (numbered steps whose line fills as they scroll in, from BioScience Writers): `ol.cap-timeline` and its parts, React `Timeline`; the fill is a transform, with no measuring.
  - `pdf-viewer` (PDF.js pages with keyboard paging and zoom, from txasm): `.cap-pdf` and its parts, React `PdfViewer`.
  - `checklist-run` (a page of steps worked through once, with timers, notes, one step at a time and a record, from dustinedwards.info's run mode): `.cap-run-*`, `capsomer/behavior/checklist-run` and the pure `capsomer/behavior/checklist-run-model`. The scale swap now copies every `[data-run-swap]` region by name and place, through `trusted-html`.
  - `offline-keep` (keep a page for offline reading, from dustinedwards.info): `.cap-keep`, `capsomer/behavior/offline-keep`, and the worker `capsomer/offline-sw.js` (store `kept-v1` unless `?cache=` names another).
  - `error-ad` (the error page's vintage ad slot, from dustinedwards.info's utility ads): `.cap-ad` and its parts, the model (`adProblems`, `burstPoints`, `scopeArt`, `chooseAd`), React `ErrorAd`, and the fonts Bangers and Alfa Slab One (OFL 1.1) in `fonts/`.
  - `web-push` (a device list that turns phone alerts on and off, from the Capsid Portal): `.cap-push` and its parts, `subscribePush`, React `PushDevices`, and the worker `capsomer/push-sw.js`.
  - `scope-chip` (a one-select filter form that submits on change, from Carrel): `form.cap-scope`, React `ScopeChip`.
  - `danger-zone` (the risky controls set apart, from the Capsid Portal): `.cap-danger`, React `DangerZone`.
  - `freshness` (when data was updated, with a ring for the next refresh, from the Capsid Portal): `.cap-fresh`, `shareLeft`, React `Freshness` and `FreshRing`.
  - `save-toggle` (save a post in this browser, from germomics): `button.cap-chip.cap-save` (a toggle chip with a bookmark), React `SaveToggle`.
  - `subscribe-links` (listen and subscribe links, from germomics): `.cap-subscribe`, each link a `cap-chip`, React `SubscribeLinks`.
  - `split` (text beside a picture, flipping sides, from dustinedwards.info's media split): `.cap-split`, React `Split`.
  - The package ships `components/*/*-sw.js`.
- **The single slider's thumb takes a 44px touch target** on a coarse pointer (the dot stays 20px inside it), and its hover ring shows only where a pointer hovers.
- No token or class was removed or renamed, and no existing HTML contract changed.

## 0.93.0

From the sites, batch 9: an inspector, React Router bindings, a work queue with Later, a role grid and a Compare that works before the script runs.

- **Inspector: a new component** (`capsomer/react/inspector`, `capsomer/inspector.css`), from Carrel's editor. Everything about a text that is not the text, in tabs beside the writing on a wide screen and in a sheet from the bottom on a phone; the head (saving, publishing) stays in the page; a tab with nothing to show is left out; `initialTab` opens a tab (and the sheet) from the address; `wideQuery` matches the page's own grid. New classes: `.cap-inspector`, `.cap-inspector-tabs`, `.cap-inspector-panel`, `.cap-inspector-open`, `.cap-inspector-sheet`.
- **React Router bindings in one place** (`capsomer/react/router`, `capsomer/behavior/router`): `routerRenderForm` for any part's `renderForm`, `RouterConfirmModal` (the router's Form, and Cancel that keeps the scroll position), `useRouterRowForm` (each row its own fetcher), `revalidateUnlessOnly` and `onlyParamsChanged` (skip a loader when only a display parameter changed; from dustinedwards.info's media library). `routerRenderLink` stays in `capsomer/react/link`.
- **Attention list: a person's work queue with Later** (`NeedsList`, from Carrel's Home). Blocking, then Waiting for you, in one row list; each row's main action and Later, which sets it aside until 8 am tomorrow in the person's zone, moves focus to the next row and offers Undo; the Later view with Bring back; `l` on a row, obeying `data-cap-single-keys`; the keys line. New types and helpers in `capsomer/behavior/attention-list`: `Need`, `NeedUrgency`, `needsShown`, `needCount`, `focusNeighbor`, and the inbox feed's `Inbox`, `InboxApp`, `InboxItem`, `InboxSeverity`, `severityOf`, `worstSeverity`, `severityTone` (from Capsid's inbox). New classes: `.cap-need-row`, `.cap-need-group`, `.cap-need-head`, `.cap-need-count`, `.cap-need-keys`, `.cap-need-key`. A need's title is a full target on a touch screen.
- **Row list: `RowForm` and `usePlainRowForm`**, the shape a row action's form takes (React Router's is `useRouterRowForm`).
- **Time: `knownZone(zone)` and `laterUntil(now, zone, hour)`** (8 am the next day in the person's zone, right across a summer-time change; from Carrel).
- **History list: Compare works before the script runs.** The pick boxes belong to a GET form in the bar (`form` attribute), and a submit button stands in for the Compare link until the script is live, posting `?compare=<id>&compare=<id>`. HTML contract change: the bar gains `form` and `button[data-cap-part="compare-submit"]`, the boxes gain `form`, and the link may be delivered `hidden` (the module shows it). New: `comparePicks(params, at)` reads either address older first, `defaultPicked` keeps a compare view's pair ticked, `messageSummary(message, path)` turns a commit message into a line's summary (Carrel's `revisionLabel`), and `changeText(before, after)` says an audit row's change in words (dustinedwards.info's lab, which called a removal "Changed" and missed a dropped field).
- **People and roles: the role grid** (`RoleGrid`, from Carrel's People page): people by scopes, a role select in each cell that saves on change, Disable with Undo, a per-row Save before the script runs, the Owner's row spanning the scopes. New helpers: `personFromParam`, `manageLead`, `listText`, `pluralTitle`, `roleSavedText`, `personName` (from dustinedwards.info's lab). New classes: `.cap-role-grid`, `.cap-role-grid-wrap`, `.cap-role-person`, `.cap-role-email`, `.cap-role-pills`, `.cap-role-actions`.
- **The lightbox and site header specs take over dustinedwards.info's browser cases** for Capsomer's own behavior (a modal viewer, focus on open, the original image; a panel's toggle and `aria-current`), and the test helpers gain `countCssRules(page)`, which counts nested rules, so a states page missing its stylesheet fails.

No token or class was removed or renamed.

- **The showcase deploy's live gate runs again.** Every devkit workflow call moves from v0.27.0 to v0.33.0, whose worker-deploy sets `DEVKIT_DIR` for the verify command; v0.27.0 never set it, so `verify:live` failed with "DEVKIT_DIR: unbound variable". No change to the package.

## 0.92.0

From the network shared-code audit, items 22 to 30 (Dustin's rule of 2026-11-11: any UI that is a component lives in Capsomer). Every site moves onto these parts and deletes its own copy.

- **The player carries the persistent audio player and the inline audio button.** `PlayerProvider` keeps one `<audio>` playing across client-side navigation, with a queue, play and pause, next and previous, seek, skip back 15 and forward 30 seconds, a rate from 0.5 to 3 and the volume and mute remembered (`playerStorage(prefix)`), a sleep timer by minutes or at the end of the track, buffering and error states, a resume point per track that is never in its last five seconds, and the whole Media Session surface (metadata with `chapterInfo` and an artwork fallback, position state, every action handler, `playbackState`). `PlayerDock` measures its height into `--cap-player-dock-h` (the body and the scroll are padded by it), is `inert` while the track's own surface is on screen, and moves its settings into the dialog's bottom sheet under 48rem. `PlayerSurface` (waveform, scrubber with chapter ticks, transport, settings), `PlayerTranscript` (parsed cues, the active cue from the native TextTrack, a click seeks, search, Copy with link), `ChapterList`, `KeyMoments`, `Hosts`, `PlayTrackButton`, `Waveform`, `WaveformPreview`. The inline audio button plays a short clip inside running text and is a link to the clip with no script: `span.cap-player-clip[data-cap="player"][data-kind="clip"]` holding `a.cap-player-clip-link` and `button.cap-player-clip-button`, wired by `enhance()` in `capsomer/behavior/player` or rendered by `PlayClip`. New `capsomer/behavior/player` (plain TS: storage, Media Session, peaks, transcript paragraphs, the press before React: `startTrack`, `loadTrack`, `STARTED_ATTRIBUTE`, `AUDIO_SELECTOR`). New classes under `cap-player-` (dock, surface, sheet, scrubber, wave, transcript, chapters, moments, hosts, clip and their parts; see player.md) and custom properties `--cap-player-face`, `--cap-player-meta-face`, `--cap-player-btn`, `--cap-player-btn-lg`, `--cap-player-dock-h`. The embed, video and audio markup is unchanged.
- **The site header works on pages that don't hydrate, its menu rows can post, and every site keeps the theme the same way.** `hydrate={false}` renders the header for `enhance()` to wire; every panel button has `popovertarget`, so a panel opens with no script. A row in a panel or the sheet can post a form (`action`, `method`, `fields`, rendered as `form.cap-site-header-form`) or run `onClick`; `account.signOutAction` and `signOutFields` make Sign out a form post, and `guardForms` sends a form once. Down or Up on a panel's button opens it on its first or last item; Back and Forward close every panel and the sheet; a panel that hover opened no longer moves focus when the pointer leaves. `panelSize: "wide"` (`data-size="wide"`, `--header-panel-w`, 40rem) and `panelClassName` for a mega-menu; `phone: "bar"` keeps a button extra in the phone's top row. New props `navLabel`, `logo.className`, `sheetLinksLabel`, `themeCookie`, `onThemeSave`, `themeInitial`, `themeAction`; new exports `initialsOf`, `guardForms`, `wireThemeKey`. Type change: `HeaderGroup.links` is `HeaderItem[]`, so a row's `href` may be absent. The admin shell passes `signOutAction`, `signOutFields`, `hydrate` and the theme props through.
- **Theme switch: one way to keep the choice.** `chooseTheme` paints, saves in this browser, writes the site's cookie (`data-cap-cookie`, or `cookie` in React) and hands the choice to the site's `onSave`. With a cookie or an `onSave`, `syncServedTheme` first copies the server's `data-theme` into this browser. `themeScript(key, { cookie })` reads the server's `data-theme`, then the cookie, then localStorage; with no options it is the old script byte for byte, so CSP hashes hold. New in theme-server: `themeAction(request, { cookie, secure, save })` (303 back, 204 for JSON, 400 for a bad choice, all no-store), `readThemeChoice`, `themeReturnTo`. The `cap-theme` event's detail gains `chosen`.
- **Prose blocks cover every site's post look and furniture.** A site sets its reading look with `--prose-*` tokens on `data-context="prose"` (ink, strong, heading face, ink, weight and space, h2 and h3 sizes, flow, marker, quote, code and pre, radii, table rule). Lists keep their markers. New article choices `data-headings="parts"`, `data-tables="manual"`, `data-details="plus"` and `"ruled"`; the details marker is drawn. Inline-SVG diagrams (`.blog-diagram`, `.blog-diagram-block`, `.blog-diagram-credit` and their part and tone classes) take their look from `--diagram-*` tokens. Print wraps code, keeps headings with what follows, splits no figure, table, code block or callout, prints outside link addresses and opens every details.
- **Tabs gain remembered sets, a draggable pill and page tabs.** `data-progressive` turns server-sent panel links into tabs; `data-remember="<key>"` (and `data-remember-guess="platform"`) keeps the choice on the device for every set with that key, on this page and in other windows. `data-variant="pill"` with `data-tone` per tab, dragged, clicked or moved by the arrows (`--cap-tabs-tray`, `--cap-tab-mark`, `--cap-tab-mark-edge`, `--cap-tab-mark-shadow`). React: `Tabs remember`, `variant="pill"`, `TabsTrigger tone`, and `PageTabs` (`?tab=` links that keep the page's other parameters). New class `.cap-tabs-panel-label`. The keyboard script is unchanged.
- **Stat tile, status, sortable list, time, message and disclosure.** A static stat tile (`data-variant="static"`, `StaticStatTile`, `.cap-tile-delta`, `.cap-tiles[data-layout="grid"]`); the interactive tile rules now apply only to `.cap-tile:is(a, button)`. Status gains the `muted` tone, more glyphs (queued, unknown, blocked, question, pr, report, proposal, site-down), `variant="accent"` and `.cap-pill-dot`. Sortable lists move rows between lists (`data-group`, `SortableBoard`), add and remove rows (`useRows`, `onAdd`, `onRemove`, `min`, `max`, `cap:sortable-remove`), and take `layout="stack"`, `altKeys`, `readOnly`, `busy`, `renderHead` and `empty`; the single-list React render has no wrapper. Time gains `formatDate`, `dateRange`, `utcInputValue`, `fromUtcInputValue`, `timecode`, `duration`, `span`, `until` and `relative(..., { granularity })`, `format="date"`, and `Time` props `tip` and `empty`. Message gains `useActionResult`, `<ActionResult>` and `actionResultView` for a form action's result. Disclosure gains `variant="card" size="sm"`. The menu's radio group takes `closeOnClick`.
- No token or class was removed or renamed.

## 0.91.1

- **A chart no longer redraws 16 px tall.** With Enarratio 0.12 the figure holds the toolbar's 16 by 16 icons before the chart, and `ChartFrame` took its redraw height from the first `viewBox` it found, so every redrawn chart collapsed (seen in the Capsid Portal on 0.90.0). It now skips `enarratio-icon` drawings (`chartViewBox` in `components/chart-frame/chart-frame.ts`).

## 0.91.0

- **New: the draft compare's merge mode** (`DraftMerge`, `mergePlan`, `merged`; from Carrel's merge view). Two texts that both moved on from one version, block by block, each difference kept from either side or both, starting on the side that changed it when the base is known. Its diff is the compare's own, so it needs no diff library. New classes: `.cap-merge` and its parts (`-intro`, `-list`, `-same`, `-choice`, `-sides`, `-side`, `-text`, `-empty`, `-mark`, `-actions`). No token or class was removed or renamed.
- **New: rich text field** (`capsomer/react/rich-text-field`, `capsomer/behavior/rich-text-field`, `capsomer/rich-text-field.css`; from txasm's abstract editor). A short text with a few inline tags and nothing else, a formatting toolbar (one tab stop) whose buttons toggle and show where the caret is, and `sanitizeRichText`, which the server runs on every save. The box is built from DOM nodes, never from a markup string, so it needs no Trusted Types policy. New classes: `.cap-rich-text`, `.cap-rich-text-bar`, `.cap-rich-text-box`, `.cap-rich-text-count`. No token or class was removed or renamed.

## 0.90.0

Fixes from a review against Chrome's Modern Web Guidance v1 (all 208 guides, 2026-11-11). Every site gets them by bumping Capsomer.

- **Enter while typing Japanese, Chinese or Korean no longer submits.** The confirming Enter of an IME conversion acted at once in the confirm dialog's typed word, the markdown editor's link and alt fields, the media tag field, the site header's search, and the command menu. New `isImeEnter(event)` in `capsomer/behavior/shortcuts` (`isComposing`, plus `keyCode` 229 for Safari); those fields and the listbox, tree, row list, draft compare, media grid and shortcuts now use it. Sites with their own Enter handlers should use it too.
- **Find-in-page reaches text in an inactive tab.** Where the browser has it (Chrome, Edge, Firefox 148), an inactive panel is `hidden="until-found"` once enhanced; a match chooses its tab, and the panel is no Tab stop while hidden. A disabled tab's panel stays plainly hidden. Safari is unchanged. HTML contract unchanged: markup still ships `hidden`. Base reset: `[hidden]` no longer forces `display: none` on `hidden="until-found"`.
- **Dialog.**
  - The page behind no longer locks with `overflow: hidden` on the root, which removed the scrollbar. The backdrop now contains the scroll (`overflow: hidden` and `overscroll-behavior: contain` on `::backdrop`; Chrome 144, Firefox 150).
  - Light dismiss is the browser's where it has `closedby`: `any`, `closerequest` for locked and alert dialogs, `none` while busy. An author's own `closedby` is kept, and Safari keeps the scripted backdrop click.
  - A button opener may carry `commandfor` and `command="show-modal"`, so it opens the dialog before script runs. A closer with `command="close"` is handled by the script while it runs.
  - On a phone the footer buttons stack in source order (`column`, was `column-reverse`), so Tab order matches what you see. The main action is now last, nearest the thumb.
  - A sheet's drag header takes only its drag axis (`touch-action: pan-x` on a bottom sheet, `pan-y` on a side sheet; was `none`).
- **No heading inside a `summary`.** JAWS and TalkBack drop it. The "accordion titles in headings" pattern and its specimen are removed (the CSS rule that styled `summary > h2…h6` is gone). Use a group row in a heading for sections that need one. No site used the old pattern.
- **Long lists render lazily.** Feed days after the second, media tiles after the 24th and rows after the 30th get `content-visibility: auto` with an estimated height (`contain-intrinsic-size: auto`) and `overflow-clip-margin` for focus rings.
- **Rows that scroll sideways fade only where there is more.** These are tab lists, the anchor bar, the letterhead's phone links row (replacing its fixed end fade) and wide prose tables. It uses a mask driven by a scroll timeline, and needs no script. Where scroll timelines are missing there is no fade.
- **Upload progress is a native `<progress class="cap-meter-bar" max="100" value="…">`** (media tile and React media grid), styled as the meter's track. HTML contract change: the media uploading block's `span.cap-meter-bar[role=progressbar]` with `span.cap-meter-fill` is now `progress.cap-meter-bar`.
- **The toolbar uses native `focusgroup`** where the browser has it (Chrome 150). The roving script is skipped there and stays elsewhere. Bars with their own item set (markdown editor, tag group) keep the script. Tabs keep their script, because native `tablist` focusgroup does not skip disabled tabs.
- **Moving rows keeps their state.** The tree, the media grid sort and the sortable list use `moveBefore` where the browser has it (new `moveNode` in `capsomer/behavior/sortable-list`). A pointer drag follows only the pointer that started it, and captures it.
- **A cut-off sortable label or history name scrolls to its end** while its row is hovered or has keyboard focus (CSS only, not under reduced motion). The text is now in an inner `<span>`, an HTML contract change for `.cap-sortable-label` and `.cap-hist-name` (without the span nothing moves).
- **Video and audio wait until near the screen.** `Video` and `Audio` set `loading="lazy"` by default, and pass `loading="eager"` for a player at the top of a page.
- **Reduced motion is handled per animation.** The global `animation-duration: 0.01ms !important` and `transition-duration: 0s !important` are removed from `base.css`; only `scroll-behavior` stays. Every Capsomer animation already sat inside `prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference` except the system map's flow, which now does. A site with its own unguarded animation must guard it.
- **The header link underline grows by `scale`**, not `inline-size`.
- **Type.**
  - The header, field, admin shell and manual font sizes are in rem, not px, so they follow the reader's text size.
  - Inline code in prose matches the prose's x-height with `font-size-adjust: from-font`. Browsers without it keep 0.85em.
  - Paragraphs, list items, `dd`, blockquotes and captions get `text-wrap: pretty` everywhere.
- **A textarea in an input group keeps its drag handle** where the browser cannot grow it with its text (`resize: none` only under `@supports (field-sizing: content)`).
- No token or class was removed or renamed. The removed specimen section was `#s-accordion-headings`.

## 0.89.0

- **Chart frame: the chart's toolbar is Enarratio's** (Enarratio 0.12.0, its record 0035; Dustin approved 2026-11-11). Enarratio now draws one toolbar above the chart, with Sound, Range and a Download menu ("Download CSV", then the drawing's files and Copy embed), each an icon and a word on one row that never wraps, phones included. Its data table is always visually hidden, for screen readers and AI agents, so "Show data" is gone and no table opens under a chart to push the page down. Capsomer's own Download menu is removed: `components/chart-frame/chart-downloads.ts` and `groupDownloads()` are deleted, along with the class `span.cap-chart-downloads` and the grouping in `capsomer/behavior/chart-enhance` and React's `ChartFrame`. A page with charts no longer needs `capsomer/menu.css`. `css/enarratio-tokens.css` sizes the toolbar on Capsomer's tokens (new `--enarratio-control-height`, `--enarratio-padding-control`, `--enarratio-radius-control`, `--enarratio-gap-controls`, `--enarratio-gap-icon`, `--enarratio-padding-panel`, `--enarratio-panel-shadow`), and `css/enarratio.css` takes the link system's file mark and tint off "Download CSV" in the menu. Removed rules: `.enarratio-export` in `css/base.css` and `css/enarratio.css`, and in `chart-frame.css` the "Show data" summary, the CSV link's target, the open range's row and the scrolling data table. The stat tile's and usage meter's tables (`dataTableBlock`) are hidden the same way, with no tab stop. The enarratio peer range is `^0.12.0`.

## 0.88.1

- **Buttons change under the pointer in every variant.** The hover, pressed and open states sat above the variants in `button.css`, and each variant sets `--cap-btn-bg` with the same specificity, so a primary, secondary or danger button kept its resting fill on hover and press (Foxhound's amber calls to action never moved; found in the Recova parity audit, 2026-11-10). The states now come after the variants, so each variant's own `--cap-btn-hover` and `--cap-btn-press` apply. The default button and the disabled look are unchanged. No token or class was removed or renamed.
- **Foxhound's 404 title is set in DM Sans.** The 404 title reads `--poster` (Jost), and Foxhound loads no Jost, so its title fell back to Futura or Century Gothic. The foxhound site tokens now set `--poster` to DM Sans, like its headings. No token or class was removed or renamed.

## 0.88.0

- **Chart frame: the downloads are one Download menu** (Carrel Desk redesign, approved 2026-11-10). Once Enarratio's enhancement layer has added Download SVG, Download PNG, Download tactile SVG and Copy embed beside a figure's Download CSV link, the frame moves all five into a Download menu (Capsomer's form menu, `details.cap-menu-form` in the new `span.cap-chart-downloads`) where the link was, in React's `ChartFrame` and in `capsomer/behavior/chart-enhance`. With no script the CSV link is unchanged. A page with charts now also loads `capsomer/menu.css` for the menu's look (without it the menu is a plain disclosure). No token or class was removed or renamed.
- **Media library: Upload is a dialog that asks each file's alt text** (Carrel Desk redesign, approved 2026-11-10). The upload form no longer sits open above the grid: Upload ends the toolbar and opens a dialog. With script and `submit` it takes several files, and each has its own Alt text field and Decorative box; it posts one `upload` per file and says the whole as one result, keeping a refused file in the dialog with the site's reason. Import from a web address moved into the same dialog. With no script, Upload links to `?upload=1` and the dialog is rendered open in the page with the one-file form. HTML contract change: `.cap-media-lib-upload` and the import disclosure are rendered only inside `dialog.cap-media-upload` (and the picker keeps its form in place); the query gains `upload` (`readMediaView` reads `upload=1`, `withMedia` closes it on any other change). New: `.cap-media-lib-upload-open`, `.cap-media-upload` and its parts, and `useIntents().show(result)` for a result a list made from several posts. No token or class was removed or renamed.
- **A status message sits above the phone's tab bar.** On a phone with the app's tab bar, the sticky status region (`.cap-message`) lay over the bar and covered its links (axe target-size, WCAG 2.5.8). Its bottom offset now clears the bar. No token or class was removed or renamed.

## 0.87.0

- **Letterhead: a site with one name centers it and sets it at 22px.** On the headquarters site (dustinedwards.info) and its admin, the name was the second line of a two-line block under an empty spacer line, so it sat low and at 20px. The spacer is gone, the name is centered on the row beside the mark, and it is 22px (the longest admin name, "Dustin Edwards Lab bench", still clears the links). Sites with an agency line over a site name are unchanged. No token or class was removed or renamed.

## 0.86.0

- **The family page is 1440px wide** (Dustin, 2026-11-08: "extend out the width of the header"). `--page-max` goes from 1280px to 1440px (a 1408px column), so the letterhead, content and footer use more of a wide window. At 1280px and narrower nothing changes. Running text keeps `--measure`.
- **The letterhead sits 8px below the top of the window, not 16px,** on a public page and an admin from 768px, so the band moves up and the cover under it has more of the first screen. `--header-h` is 80px (one row, from 1080px) or 128px (two rows), 8px less. No token or class was removed or renamed.

## 0.85.0

- **Layout grid: the label is opt-in.** `.cap-layout-grid` no longer draws "12 columns, 24 px baseline" ("4 columns" on a phone) at its foot; it is words beside a decoration. HTML contract change: add `data-labeled` to a `.cap-layout-grid` that should keep the label (a colophon or a page about the grid). No token or class was removed or renamed. Apps that want the old look everywhere add the attribute.
- **Docs and comments: the NASA Graphics Standards Manual is dated 1975,** the year of the manual (the Standards Manual reissue is titled "1975 NASA Graphics Standards Manual"), not 1976. No token, class or markup changed.

## 0.84.1

- The `enarratio` peer range adds `^0.10.0`, so a site on Enarratio 0.10.0 installs Capsomer without a peer conflict (npm ERESOLVE). No token, class or HTML change.
- The component pages' script no longer sizes an example frame before the example has marked itself shown. On a slow load the frame jumped to a 360 px fallback and back, shifting the page (CLS 0.3 live).

## 0.84.0

- **Component pages reserve each example frame's height.** The frames resized as they loaded, so a page shifted (CLS 0.1 to 0.4 on Anchor bar, Combobox and Catalog; web.dev/articles/cls says to reserve the space). `scripts/frame-heights.mjs` measures every frame at four page widths and writes `site/frame-heights.json` and `site/frame-heights.css`; each frame carries `data-h1` to `data-h4` and the page's CSS sets its height from them. The page's script only grows a frame past the reserved height. No token, class or component markup changed.
- **`img[width][height]` takes `aspect-ratio` from its attributes in `base.css`.** Chrome's DevTools flags a lazy image whose height is `auto` and whose ratio is only the attribute-derived `auto w / h` (`LazyLoadImageIssue`); an explicit ratio clears it. The rule is `:where(...)`, so any host `aspect-ratio` wins, and it sits in `@supports` for typed `attr()`. No token or class changes.

## 0.83.1

- **Breadcrumb: long names wrap in full instead of being cut after two lines.** The two-line clamp still lost the end of a long name under a reader's own text spacing (WCAG 1.4.12). `.cap-breadcrumb-link` and `.cap-breadcrumb-page` are `display: block` with no `overflow: hidden` or line clamp. No token or class changes.
- **Docs: the screen-reader line.** Every component doc says no manual NVDA or VoiceOver run is planned (decision of 2026-11-07) and that the automated browser checks and a front-end review stand in, instead of "Last checked by hand: not yet". No token, class or markup changed.

## 0.83.1

- **Confirm page: the form no longer carries `role="group"`.** `group` is not an allowed role for `form` (ARIA in HTML; axe `aria-allowed-role`), and it replaced the form landmark's role. The `form.cap-confirm-page` keeps its `aria-labelledby` and `aria-describedby`, so it is a named `form` landmark. HTML contract change: remove `role="group"` from `form.cap-confirm-page`. React: `ConfirmPage` and `ConfirmModal` no longer pass `role`, and `ConfirmFormProps` loses its `role` field (a `renderForm` that spreads the props needs no change; one that reads `props.role` should stop).
- **History list: the phone specimen's hidden heading is an `h2`, not an `h3`,** so the specimen page no longer skips a level (axe `heading-order`). Specimen only; no component change.

## 0.83.0

- **Install contract: `@drdustinedwards/site-helpers` is an optional peer dependency, no longer a dependency.** Only the `capsomer` command (`states`, `site-data`) and this repository's own site import it. A git dependency inside a git dependency is refused by npm 12 (`allow-git`, EALLOWGIT) and left the lockfiles of apps stale. An app that runs the `capsomer` command declares `@drdustinedwards/site-helpers` itself (>=0.40.0), as it does `@dustinedwards/site-runtime`. No token, class or HTML changes.
- **Lightbox doc: the Close button's size is stated as the dialog block's.** The page said 44 px; it is the icon button's own size. Docs only.

## 0.82.0

- **Family footer: the Workspace link reads "Lab Bench"**, capital B, as its accessible name does ("Lab Bench, the members' workspace (requires sign-in)"). Text only; no class, token or HTML structure changes.

- **Family footer: a Legal column, and a Features link.** Privacy, Terms and Accessibility move out of the Site group into a new Legal group (`<nav aria-labelledby="cap-family-footer-legal">`, heading "Legal"), as GOV.UK's footer groups its policy links (privacy, accessibility statement, terms). The Site group is now Contact, Site map, Features and Colophon. Features links dustinedwards.info's `/features`, a page in code that is always published, so it is always listed. The footer's columns go from three to four at 48rem and wider. New export `legalLinks()` and `LEGAL_HEADING`; `familyLinks()` lists the Legal links after the Site links; `siteLinks()` no longer returns Privacy, Terms or Accessibility, so a site that read them from it reads `legalLinks()`.

## 0.81.0

- **Lightbox: `capsomer/behavior/lightbox` and `capsomer/lightbox.css`.** A link to a full-size image (`a.image-link`) or a diagram image (`img.diagram-image`) inside `data-cap="lightbox"` opens in the shared dialog and shrinks back into place. It moves here from Prelum's `enhance()` so every site has one viewer. Additive; no class, token or HTML contract changes to existing components.

## 0.80.0

- **Family footer: no hover tint behind the wordmark or the social icons.** `.cap-family-footer a:hover` no longer sets `background-color: var(--accent-soft)`; the word links and site tiles already reset it, so only the wordmark and the social icons changed. Their hover color stays. No class, token or HTML contract changes.

- **Chart enhance uses Enarratio's page code.** `capsomer/behavior/chart-enhance` was a copy of Enarratio 0.9's `enhanceCharts` and `watchCharts`; it now calls them with the frame selector. Same exports and behavior. `enarratio` is 0.9.1 for development. No class, token or HTML contract changes.

- **Site header (React): the section links' `nav` is named "Main", not "Site"**, so it no longer shares a name with the footer's "Site" `nav` (axe `landmark-unique`; a screen reader's landmark list showed two navigations called Site). The `aria-label` changes from `Site` to `Main`. No class or token changes.

- **Removed export: `escapeHtml` from `capsomer/email` and `capsomer/system-map`.** It is site-runtime's (`@dustinedwards/site-runtime/html`), which a site already has; import it from there. Same five entities, same output. No class, token or HTML contract changes.

## 0.79.3

- **No script hands a string to an injection sink.** A page that enforces Trusted Types (`require-trusted-types-for 'script'`) refuses `innerHTML`, `srcdoc` and `DOMParser` given a plain string, and Chrome logs each as a CSP issue in DevTools. Capsomer's scripts now build DOM (`createElement`, `textContent`, `replaceChildren`) wherever they can: the site header's search results, the catalog's swap of regions, filters and sort options. The remaining markup, which a visitor cannot write (icon SVG children, the catalog's own server HTML, a chart's or a comparison's SVG), goes through one policy named `capsomer` in the new `components/trusted-html/trusted-html.ts` (`trustedHtml`, `setStaticHtml`, `markup`, `copyChildren`); a page whose `trusted-types` list omits the name gets plain strings, as before. No HTML contract, class or token changes.

## 0.79.2

- **Chip: a label longer than its row wraps inside the chip.** `.cap-chip` loses `white-space: nowrap` and gains `max-width: 100%`, `overflow-wrap: anywhere` and 2px block padding, so a long subject chip (Foxing /books) no longer forces sideways scroll at 320px. Short chips look the same. No class or token changes.
- **Tabs: choosing a tab no longer calls `scrollIntoView`.** It scrolled the page and set the browser's Tab starting point to the tab, so on a page with an initial tab set the first Tab press skipped the skip link and header. Only the tab list now scrolls, to show the chosen tab. No class, token or HTML contract changes.
- **Site header: the search form's landmark name is the search label, not "Site"**, so it no longer shares a name with the sections `nav` (axe `landmark-unique`; screen readers listed two landmarks called Site). The form's `aria-label` changes from `Site` to the search label. No class or token changes.

## 0.79.1

- **`confirmSubmit` accepts a React mouse event** (its `nativeEvent` was typed too narrowly, so passing a button's `onClick` event failed to typecheck). Types only; no class, token or HTML contract changes.

## 0.79.0

- **Renamed export: `kindOf` in `capsomer/behavior/message` is now `messageKind`**, so devkit's overlap check no longer finds the name defined twice. Same arguments and result.
- **Chart frame no longer imports `escapeHtml` from Enarratio** (it uses Capsomer's own, the one family-footer and site-header use), so Enarratio can stop exporting it. Same output.

- **Prelum 0.35.1** (was 0.33.0), so sites can take Prelum 0.35.1 without an npm ERESOLVE over Mermaid CLI. No code change.
- **Peer range: `enarratio` now accepts `^0.8.0` and `^0.9.0`** as well as `^0.5.0` to `^0.7.0`. No code change.
- **Confirm dialog: `confirmSubmit`** (`capsomer/behavior/confirm-dialog`), the dialog in place of `window.confirm` on a form that posts: it holds a form's submit, asks, and sends the form again with the same button once the action is chosen. Additive; no class, token or HTML contract changes.
- **Email: `escapeHtml` is site-runtime's.** `capsomer/email` re-exports `escapeHtml` from `@dustinedwards/site-runtime/html` instead of keeping a copy; same five entities, same output. site-runtime is already a peer of every site that uses Capsomer's email and footer. No class, token or HTML contract changes.

## 0.78.0

- **Kit form: `KitForm` and `useKitSubmit`** (`capsomer/react/kit-form`). The form and the submit a React Router app hands the posts, media and mentions lists. Carrel and dustinedwards.info each had their own copy; they use these. Additive (`react-router`, already an optional peer, is needed to import it); no class, token or HTML contract changes.
- **Error page: a 403 has its own words.** `errorWords` gives a 403 "You cannot open this page" and says the account lacks access, instead of the generic "This page cannot be shown (403)". Every other status keeps its words. No class, token or HTML contract changes.
