- **Development: two browser-test races fixed** (no package change). The tabs, breadcrumb and pagination specs read `page.url()` straight after Enter on a hash link, before Playwright had heard of the navigation; they wait with `toHaveURL`. The dialog's drag-to-close test read the sheet header's box during the sheet's 200 ms slide in, so under load its press landed below the header and started no drag; each drag now waits for the sheet's animations to finish and measures the header then.
- **Showcase site: shared maintenance page and a real 404.** The showcase site's Worker (site-runtime 0.87.0) answers the shared 503 maintenance page while its `MAINTENANCE` var is on, and its `/404` answers 404, not 200. Not in the package files.
- **CI: one check job per pull request** (devkit v0.26.1's pr.yml in `checks.yml`): the trailer, shared-code, OSV-Scanner, workflow audit, no-bloat and reviewer checks run as `checks / Repo checks`; `review.yml`, `workflow-audit.yml` and `no-bloat.yml` are gone, the page weight report is `ci.yml`'s `weight` job, dependencies runs daily only, and the code map and Scorecard run nightly. Not in the package files.
- **Development: Oxlint** (devkit's shared config, `oxlint.config.ts`, `npm run lint`, a Lint step in CI; not in the package files). Fixes from its first run: hydration flags read `useSyncExternalStore`, so a part drawn after hydration starts in its scripted form; state that follows a prop resets in the same render instead of an effect after paint; latest-value refs are written in layout effects; `Intents` gains `setRoot`, a callback ref for the list's root (additive); avatar initials take a word's first grapheme (`Intl.Segmenter`), so a flag or an accented capital stays whole. No class, token or HTML contract changes.

## 0.77.0

- **Mentions list: `mentionsDataOfKit` and `kitMentionsSearch`** (`capsomer/behavior/mentions-list`), with `KitMentionsData` and `KitMentionRow` typed by shape. They turn site-api's `loadMentions` answer into the list's `data` and read the kit's query from the list's address, as `mediaDataOfKit` does for the library. Carrel had them as its own; they move here so dustinedwards.info's admin draws the same page. Additive; no class, token or HTML contract changes.
- Tabs: a malformed link fragment (like `#%`) no longer throws during setup and leaves every later tab set on the page dead; the fragment falls back to its raw text, as the link component already does. No class, token or HTML contract changes.
- Popover: a tooltip removed from the page while open no longer swallows the next Esc, so a dialog around it closes by keyboard again. The Popover API hides a removed popover without a toggle event, so Esc now forgets any recorded popup that is no longer open. No class, token or HTML contract changes.
- **System map wires** (`.cap-system-map-wires`): the layer is sized by the map in CSS (`inline-size` and `block-size` 100%) where the script set a pixel width. After a resize the old drawing stood one frame at its old width and widened the page, so a phone could flash a sideways scrollbar.
- **Development: quicker CI browser tests** (no package change). Tests, not files, are shared over the workers (`fullyParallel`), so the sweep no longer holds one worker for nearly the whole run; CI records no traces; only Chromium's headless shell is installed. A pull request runs the specs and sweep entries of the components it touches and of every component that shows them (`scripts/ci-specs.mjs`, `CAPSOMER_SWEEP`); a shared change, a version bump and every push to main run the whole suite.
- Site: the front page's network board is drawn in CSS, seven columns of alternating squares per site, where it was an SVG of 168 rects. It looks the same and still lights and squeezes a site in trouble. The board is decoration, not a chart (it plots no quantity, and the bar under it says each name and state in words), so it stays aria-hidden and out of Enarratio; as an SVG it read to Prelum's chart audit as a chart drawn outside Enarratio (area 10, rule F). No class, token or HTML contract changes in the package.

## 0.76.0

Wayfinding links line up with their lamp, icons and arrows, and the header's chevrons turn up when their panel opens (Dustin, 2026-11-06).

- **Changed HTML: a workspace login's word is now `<span class="cap-family-footer-label">`** (`familyFooterHtml`), between its icon and its lock. Its box is trimmed to the capitals (`text-box: trim-both cap alphabetic`), so the row centers on the letters.
- **Footer links and next links** (`.cap-family-footer-links a`, `a[data-link="next"]`): `display: inline-block` with the same trim, padded back out to `--target`, where they were `inline-flex` around the line box. Overpass's ascent (0.883em) stands far above its capitals (0.7em), so the line box's middle sat 0.1em under the letters' and the panel lamp, the login icons and locks, and the outbound arrow all hung about 1.5px low against the words. A site's own footer links (`center`) take the fix with no markup change.
- **Header chevrons** (`.cap-site-header-chev`): turn 180 degrees while their button is `aria-expanded="true"`, as the combobox's do, with the turn animated unless motion is reduced.

## 0.75.1

No token, class or HTML change. The package depends on Prelum (`@drdustinedwards/site-helpers`) 0.33.0; the 0.75.0 tag still pinned 0.32.1, so an app that took it carried the old Prelum beside its own. An app on this tag resolves one Prelum.

## 0.75.0

Lighter Inter and Overpass files, so dustinedwards.info's /systems is back under its 2.5 s LCP limit (Dustin, 2026-11-05). On the check's simulated slow phone it went from a steady 2.17 s before 0.73.0 to about 2.5 s (2.6 s in CI), because the preloaded Inter was 22 KB heavier than the face it replaced and Overpass added 27 KB; with both cuts it measured 2.20 s.

- **Renamed file: `fonts/inter-latin-opsz-normal.woff2` is now `fonts/inter-latin-wght-normal.woff2`.** Inter's optical size axis is pinned at 14, its text cut (68,948 to 45,696 bytes); the weight axis stays 300 to 800. Titles no longer switch to the display cut at large sizes. A site that imports or preloads the file by name uses the new name.
- **Overpass** (`fonts/overpass-latin-wght-normal.woff2`) keeps plain English characters, the no-break space, the middle dot, dashes, curly quotes, the bullet, the ellipsis and arrows, with kerning, ligatures and contextual forms (27,036 to 14,824 bytes). Its `@font-face` `unicode-range` names exactly those, so any other letter (an accented title in a breadcrumb) draws in the Overpass fallback.
- `scripts/narrow-font.py` makes every cut from the Fontsource 5.3.0 files: it narrows or pins axes and keeps a set of characters. The fallback metrics, measured again, are unchanged.

## 0.74.4

- **System map headings** (`.cap-system-map-column-name`, `-group-name`, `-place-name`, `-card-name`, `-part-name`, `-flow-name`): `--weight-heading` (Inter Medium), where they were 600, so the map's headings read as the type plan's headings on Capsid, dustinedwards.info and the showcase.
- **Cue card titles** (`.cap-cue-title`): `--weight-heading`, where they were 700.

## 0.74.3

- **Panel and dialog titles** (`.cap-panel` header `h2`/`h3`, `.cap-dialog-title`): `--weight-heading` (Inter Medium), where they were 600 and 800, so they read as the headings the type plan sets in Medium. Smaller UI labels (alert, flag and tray titles) keep 600.

## 0.74.2

The type plan's weights where they had slipped (verifier, 2026-11-04).

- **Breadcrumb links** (`.cap-direction a`): Overpass Bold, as the sign is. base.css's text-link weight (`--link-weight`, 600) set the ways a step lighter than the page you are on, on every site.
- **Headings by default** (`css/base.css`): `h1` takes `--weight-title` (Inter Light) and `h2` to `h4` `--weight-heading` (Inter Medium), where the browser drew them bold. Carrel, Prelum's specimen and Enarratio's gallery titles were 700 for want of this. A site's or component's own weight still wins.
- **Showcase site**: page titles take `--weight-title` (Inter Light) and headings `--weight-heading` (Inter Medium), in place of 800 and 700. The cover band's lettering and the figures stay heavy; they are display type, not titles.

## 0.74.1

Smaller font files, for faster pages: dustinedwards.info's /systems went from 2.31 s to 2.60 s Lighthouse LCP (mobile profile, CI) with 0.73.0's larger Inter and the new Overpass, past the 2.5 s limit.

- **Inter** (`fonts/inter-latin-opsz-normal.woff2`): the weight axis is cut to 300 to 800, the weights the network sets (72,920 to 68,948 bytes). Its `@font-face` weight range is now `300 800`. The opsz axis and every kept weight are unchanged.
- **Overpass** (`fonts/overpass-latin-wght-normal.woff2`): the weight axis is cut to 600 to 700, the two weights wayfinding sets (39,380 to 27,036 bytes). Its `@font-face` weight range is now `600 700`; a 400 request draws 600.
- `scripts/narrow-font.py` makes both cuts from the Fontsource 5.3.0 files.
- **Changed token: `--faa-arrow`** is now a `polygon()` clip path, not a `data:` SVG mask image: a page whose Content-Security-Policy says `img-src 'self'` (the Capsid explorer) refused the image, so the direction strip lost its arrows. The direction strip draws it with `clip-path`; a site that used `mask: var(--faa-arrow)` uses `clip-path: var(--faa-arrow)` on a 1em by 0.72em box.
- `allowScripts` denies esbuild's and workerd's install scripts for npm 12, which asks about each one; both work without them (their binaries come from platform packages). No class, token or HTML contract changes.

## 0.74.0

Decoration for the manual theme (Dustin, 2026-11-04): the footer band becomes a cheatline, and two new parts. No token or class is removed or renamed.

- **Changed look: `.cap-livery` is a cheatline.** Three stripes, 22, 8 and 3 px with 6 and 4 px gaps (43 px, was 49), in `--ramp-3`, `--ramp-2` and `--ramp-1` in place of `--on`, so the foot of every manual page shares its tones with the site's sequential charts. The HTML contract is unchanged; `[data-livery]` on a card keeps the family color.
- **New: `.cap-hold`**, the runway holding position marking (FAA AIM 2-3-5) as a divider in the page's ink, for the end of a post before other sites' responses. Use it on an `hr`.
- **New: `ul.cap-annunciator`**, a caution and warning panel: `li[data-state="normal" | "caution" | "warning" | "off"]` holding a name and `span.cap-annunciator-state`, on the dark sign panel in both themes, lit amber (`--warn`) or red (`--crit`). Legends use `--drafting` where the site loads it, else `--mono`.
- **Changed look: family footer site tiles** no longer take the hover tint; the mark's outline alone answers the pointer.
- Site: the front page has a network board under its figures, after Bridget Riley's Movement in Squares (1961): one checkerboard with a stretch per network site in its family color and a name bar under each. A site whose /health says warn or fail lights amber or red and its columns squeeze. The site's Worker answers `/network.json` from each site's /health, held a minute at the edge.

## 0.73.0

The network's type (Dustin approved the seven-face set, 2026-11-04): Inter replaces Schibsted Grotesk, and four faces join for jobs the UI face should not do. Sites pin the tag; the faces download only where a page uses them.

- **Removed: Schibsted Grotesk.** `fonts/schibsted-grotesk-latin-wght-normal.woff2`, its OFL file and its `MEASURED` entry and fallback (`"Schibsted Grotesk Fallback"`) are gone. **Changed tokens:** `--ui` and `--display` are now `"Inter", "Inter Fallback", "Segoe UI", "Helvetica Neue", Arial, sans-serif`, Inter with its optical size axis (Fontsource 5.3.0's latin opsz file, 73 KB), so large type takes Inter's display cut by itself. Inter is the face closest to the 1976 NASA manual's Helvetica of the free faces measured (type study). The capsid site's `--prose` follows `--ui`. Enarratio's theme (`capsomer/enarratio-theme`, `enarratio.css`) names Inter. `UI_STACK` in `tokens/palette-core.mjs` changes with it.
- **New faces in `fonts.css`, each with a size-matched fallback:** Overpass (`--wayfinding`, the free Highway Gothic that FAA airport signs use, AC 150/5345-44L Appendix A), Jost (`--poster`, the free Futura, for share cards, title images, banners, flyers and a 404 title, never text under an Inter heading), Capsomer Drafting (`--drafting`, labels in line drawings and words on lit panels) and DSEG7 Classic (`--readout`: upright 400 for instrument readouts, italic 300 for countdowns). Licenses in `fonts/OFL-*.txt`.
- **Capsomer Drafting** is Routed Gothic (Darren Embry, OFL 1.1), Leroy and Gorton lettering, with the digit 1 redrawn with a flag so "HTLV-1" can't read as "HTLV-I". "Routed Gothic" is a Reserved Font Name, so the modified font has its own name (OFL section 3). `scripts/drafting-font.py` builds it from the pinned source.
- **New tokens:** `--weight-title` (300: Inter Light for titles and names set at 24px and up, after the manual's Helvetica Light), `--weight-heading` (500: Inter Medium for headings), `--wayfinding`, `--poster`, `--drafting`, `--readout`, `--faa-arrow` (the FAA sign arrow as a mask image, with a shaft as thick as Overpass Bold's stem, AC Fig. A-5 Note 1) and `--faa-arrow-out` (the footer's outbound arrow). Family colors: `--sign-here` and `--on-sign-here` (the current page in a direction strip, checked at 7:1), and `--lit-bulb` (a lit countdown on the dark sign, the KSC countdown clock's warm bulbs, checked at 7:1).
- **Changed token:** `--figures` is `tabular-nums` (was `proportional-nums`). Inter's tabular comma and period are narrow (0.269 em), so a tile's figure keeps its width as it changes and has no gap at the comma.
- **Direction strip (`.cap-direction`)** reads as airport direction signs: Overpass Bold, white FAA arrows between the steps (they were small triangles in the family color), and the current page as a location sign (`--on-sign-here` text and a 2px inset outline on `--sign-here`, 3px corners; it was filled in `--on`). Links get every link's hover, a white 18% tint on the dark band. On a phone the current page stays visually hidden, as before. No HTML contract change.
- **Wayfinding links use Overpass:** the manual's signs (`a.cap-sign`) at 700 (they were Inter 500), `a[data-link="next"]` and `.cap-go[data-go="back"]` at 700, and the family footer's link columns (`.cap-family-footer-links a`) at 600. The footer's outbound arrow is 1.25em with the FAA shaft and head (`--faa-arrow-out`). Links in text, menus and buttons keep their look.
- **Headings at Medium:** `.cap-page-head-title` (22px, was 800) and `.cap-part-title` (was 700) use `--weight-heading`. Letterhead and footer names keep their weight; they are logotypes.
- **The 404 title is a Jost poster title:** `.cap-error-page[data-status="404"] h1.cap-empty-title` is set in `--poster` at 600, 2rem to 2.75rem. Other errors keep the UI face.
- **New: `.cap-form-boxes[data-variant="panel"]`**, readings as an Apollo DSKY panel: the dark sign in both themes, legends in Capsomer Drafting capitals and values lit in DSEG7 in the family's light color (`--on-sign-here`) with a soft glow, so each site's panel is its own hue. For numbers only, since DSEG7 has no letters.
- `css/fallback-fonts.css` keeps `"Inter Fallback"` for sites that host their own Inter (txasm), and adds nothing for the new faces, whose fallbacks are in `fonts.css`.
- Site: the home page shows all seven faces, the theme builder offers Inter in place of Schibsted Grotesk (the `@fontsource/inter` static files are gone; it uses Capsomer's own Inter), and the home page preloads Inter.

## 0.72.0

Email gains the blocks a product's own mail needs, and Banner takes block content. No token or class is removed or renamed.

- **Email: new blocks** `subheading`, `rows` and `pre`, and a paragraph can hold lines and bold runs (`string | (string | { bold })[][]`). A string paragraph renders as before. **Changed HTML contract:** when a template has no `heading` block, the brand name in the header is an `h1` (it was a plain div), inheriting its font and color. New markup: `h2` for a subheading, a presentation table for rows, `div.e-pre > pre` for preformatted text; `alert` accepts `pre` and `details` text, and `notice` bodies accept the same text. The dark scheme adds `.e-pre` (ground background, line border).
- **Banner: `body` prop** (React) for block content, rendered in `div.cap-banner-text` inside `.cap-banner-body`, with block rules for `p`, `ul` and `ol` in that div. **Changed behavior:** the React Dismiss moves focus to the main region when focus was inside the banner (the script already did; `focusMain` is now exported from `capsomer/behavior/banner`). `children` is now optional.
- Site: the email states page has a digest sample at 320 and 600 px.

## 0.70.0

- **The hexagons draw larger.** Each group's name stands in the empty cell before its run on the outer ring, so the comb loses the ring it kept for names, and the comb may be as tall as the screen less 8rem (it was the lesser of the screen less 12rem and 52rem). In a 900 px tall window a cell goes from about 72 px wide to about 95. No class, token or HTML contract changes.

## 0.69.0

- **The hexagons' lit lines say which way each tie runs.** Their dashes move from the system that uses toward the system used, a tie both ways stays solid, and the detail line adds "Uses … Used by …" in words. Reduced motion stills the dashes. The network's detail line keeps four lines of room, so lighting no longer shifts the drawing. **Changed HTML contract:** a lit `path.cap-system-map-wire` in the comb carries `data-both` where the tie runs both ways, and the network's detail line ends with `span.cap-system-map-detail-ways` while a system is lit.
- **Removed: the `capsomer/behaviour/*` export**, the British-spelling alias of `capsomer/behavior/*`. Import `capsomer/behavior/<name>`.
- **Removed: `Badge` from `capsomer/react/status`**, the alias of `Pill`. Use `Pill`; it takes the same props.

## 0.68.0

The family's faces load with `font-display: optional` in place of `swap`. No class, token or HTML contract changes.

- **`fonts.css`: Schibsted Grotesk and Martian Mono no longer swap in after the first paint** (D5). With `swap`, a page painted in the size-matched fallback and then redrew in the face; the two are within about 1% in width, but that was still enough to move a line break and the page under it (germomics' influenza heading 0.060, the Portal's export row 66 to 90 px). With `optional`, the browser waits about 100 ms for the face, and a visit it misses stays in the fallback, so text never moves once drawn (web.dev, "Best practices for fonts"; Lighthouse's font-display audit passes it). **What a site does:** preload the face its pages draw first (site-runtime's `preloads()`), so the first visit gets it in time; the cache serves later visits. A site that doesn't preload shows its first visit in the fallback.
- **`fallback-fonts.css`: Space Grotesk's fallback has bold rules** (on Arial Bold, measured from the variable font instanced at 600 and 700: Space Grotesk keeps nearly one width at every weight). Its single regular rule set a 600 heading in synthetic bold about 1.5% too wide; it now sets it within a letter.
- The fallback-font docs (README, `tokens/fallback-fonts.mjs`) describe the fallback as the face a late visit is drawn in, not only a bridge to a swap.
- **Site only: a component page no longer moves as it loads** (D5; live 0.24 on chart-frame/, 0.15 on stat-tile/ at 320 px). Long example code is written closed under View code, and Copy page is written into the title row, by the page itself instead of its script, so the code box and the title row are their final size from the first paint. Without scripts the code stays open and both buttons are hidden. The package is unchanged.
- **Site only: an old root address such as `/#/components/button` no longer forwards to `/admin/`**, and `npm run site:release` is gone (it was `npm run site`; the deploy runs that). The package is unchanged.

## 0.67.2

React's `ChartFrame` gives a primitive's figure no `fit`, so a heat strip or timeline that spreads `fit` into its draw keeps its own height. No new or changed exports, and no class, token or HTML contract changes.

- **React's `ChartFrame` no longer fits a primitive to the first drawing's height.** `shownHeight` (so `fit`) is empty for a figure Enarratio marks `data-enarratio-static` (a heat strip, timeline, sparkline, uptime strip or progress ring), and the draw keeps its own height. 0.66.0 scaled every drawing's height with its width, which is a chart's shape but not a primitive's: a heat strip drawn 640 by 20 and shown in a 300 px column would have been redrawn 300 by 9.4, not 300 by 20, and a timeline ignores `height` (its lanes set it) and shows its list, not its SVG, under 30rem. A primitive's draw can now take `fit` like a chart's, `(width, fit) => heatStrip({ ...options, width, ...fit })`, and gets its own height. No class, token or HTML contract changes.

## 0.67.1

- **The system map draws whole under a Content Security Policy that forbids inline styles.** 0.67.0 set the hexagons' text sizes and the Inside column's lane count in `style` attributes, which such a policy blocks. **Changed HTML contract:** the Inside column carries `data-places="1|2|3"` in place of `style="--cap-places: n"`, and `svg.cap-system-map-comb` no longer carries a `style`; its text sizes are in system-map.css.

## 0.67.0

The system map is a connections map (Dustin, 2026-11-03: "i want the actual connections map"). The 0.65.0 cards stay; the rule that no lines are drawn goes, and every tie is a wire.

- **System map, level 1: a layered drawing.** Three lanes side by side: the shared packages in the middle, and each block of the rest (a group of what people use, and the core) on the side that leaves the fewest wires crossing the middle with the sides most even. The barycenter heuristic orders each block's cards, and each lane's blocks, so wires cross least. The script draws a wire for every tie between the cards where they landed: one curve between neighboring lanes, through a gap between cards when a wire skips a lane, bowed out to the side within a lane, and spread down each card's side in the order of where they go. Pointing at or focusing a card lights its wires (accent, heavier, on top) and fades the rest. The whole network is in view with no scrolling, on a phone too (Dustin, 2026-11-03): a card is its short name alone (the model's `short`, kept by `modelMapData` as `short` on a system), and what it is shows in a detail line above the drawing when it is pointed at or focused, while a screen reader reads it in the card.
- **System map, level 1: hexagons first, the plain map a tab away** (Dustin, 2026-11-03: "Hexagons as default"). The network opens on a honeycomb: the core in the middle, the shared packages on a ring with a cell between each, what people use on an outer ring in its groups, and the comb's empty cells drawn faded. Every tie is a line that runs only through empty cells, never under another system: the shortest path on the hex grid, with turns costing more and cells already carrying lines costing less, so lines run straight and bundle into tracks, drawn with rounded corners. Where each system stands is searched (simulated annealing over the groups' order, the gaps between them, each group's order and both rings' turn, from a few seeds, keeping the placement whose routed lines cross least): on the live model that cut the lines' total length by 16%, their crossings by 35% and their corners by 21% against the barycenter placement alone. The group names stand in the empty ring outside. The drawing is SVG from the server (`hexLayout`, pure), so the lines are there with no script; the script lights a cell's lines and its ties' cells as on the plain map. The two views are tabs (components/tabs). Narrower than 44rem the hexagons' names would fall under 12 px, so the plain map shows alone.
- **System map, level 2: wired lanes.** Used by, Runs, Stores, Outside and Uses side by side (60rem and wider), with a wire for each line: part to part, part to the other system, and the system as a whole from its Inside heading. A part shows its name and what it runs on; what it does and its lines in words are in the detail line when it is pointed at or focused (each part is a tab stop), and for screen readers in the part. Pointing at or focusing a tie or part lights its wires; a flow's step lights the wires between its parts. A tie is one row, its verbs beside its name. Narrower, the columns stack as before and the words carry the ties.
- **Changed HTML contract.** Both levels: new `p.cap-system-map-detail`. Level 1: `.cap-system-map-groups` is gone; the network is `.cap-system-map-network` of `.cap-system-map-graph` of `.cap-system-map-lane[data-lane]`, holding the same groups and bands. A card carries `data-wire` and `data-wires`, shows the short name, and its words move into `span.cap-system-map-card-about` (new, clipped; new `.cap-system-map-card-full`): `.cap-system-map-card-text` and `.cap-system-map-card-meta` are now spans in it. A part's `.cap-system-map-part-text` and `.cap-system-map-part-lines` move into `span.cap-system-map-part-about` (new, clipped; new `.cap-system-map-part-full`), and a part carries `tabindex="0"`. Level 2: ties, parts and the Inside heading's new `span` carry `data-wire` and `data-wires`; the Inside column carries `--cap-places`; the root carries `data-held`. New with script: `svg.cap-system-map-wires`, `.cap-system-map-wire`, `.cap-system-map-port`. The band's accent top border is gone. Level 1 also has `div.cap-tabs.cap-system-map-views` (new) holding a tab list (`.cap-system-map-switch`, new, hidden until the script runs) and two panels: `div.cap-system-map-hex` (new) with `svg.cap-system-map-comb` (`.cap-system-map-comb-cells`, `.cap-system-map-comb-lines`, `.cap-system-map-comb-group`, and a link `.cap-system-map-cell[data-id][data-ring]` per system with `.cap-system-map-cell-name`, all new), then `.cap-system-map-network`. **Removed class:** `cap-system-map-groups`. **New:** `hexLayout`, `networkLayout`, `tiePairs`, the types `HexCell` and `HexLayout`, and the types `Lane`, `NetworkBlock` and `NetworkLane`.

## 0.66.0

Charts and fonts stop moving the page when they arrive: React's `ChartFrame` redraws at the height its first drawing showed, and each measured fallback font has a bold face per weight range. New exports: `shownHeight` and the type `ChartFit` (`capsomer/react`'s chart frame), and the type `FontWidths` (`capsomer/fonts`); `fallbackFontFace` takes `widths`, `MEASURED` entries carry `widths`, `LOCAL` entries carry `bold`, and `capsomer fallback-font` takes `--weight`. `ChartFrame`'s `draw` gets a second argument, `fit`. No class, token or HTML contract changes; a fallback rule's `local()` names and weight ranges change in `fonts.css` and `fallback-fonts.css`.

- **Bold text in a fallback font is as wide as the real face, so headings no longer rewrap when the font arrives.** A fallback rule was one regular face, so bold text in it was the browser's synthetic bold. That makes the strokes thicker but keeps the regular widths: Chromium set a 40 px line of Liberation Sans 715.9 px wide at 400 and also at a synthesized 700, while the real bold took 766.8 px. Schibsted Grotesk is 5.1% wider at 700 than at 400, so the fallback set dustinedwards.info's /cv h1 at 320 px on 2 lines (110 px tall) and the face on 3 (150 px), a CLS of 0.112 when the font came late. Now `"Schibsted Grotesk Fallback"` is three `@font-face` rules on one name. Up to weight 549 it uses Arial; from 550 to 649 it uses Arial Bold scaled to Schibsted Grotesk at 600; from 650 to 1000 it uses Arial Bold scaled to Schibsted Grotesk at 700. The /cv h1 is now 3 lines and 150 px in both, and the CLS is 0.0007. In Playwright's Chromium, at 16 px and 40 px, the fallback's width moved from 3.4% narrower than the face at 700 and 1.5% narrower at 600 to 0.9% and 1.0% wider, the same as at 400 (1.9%). `css/fallback-fonts.css` does the same for DM Sans, Source Serif 4, Montserrat, Open Sans, Inter and Fraunces. Fraunces's fallback is now within 0.3% of the face at 400, 600 and 700, where it had been 12% narrower. Martian Mono, DM Mono, JetBrains Mono and Space Grotesk keep one rule: their width does not change with weight. The widths were measured from the real files at 600 and 700 with @capsizecss/unpack (variable files instanced with fontTools first). The bold local faces' widths are Capsize 4.4.0's 700 entries; Liberation Sans Bold measures the same 983. Methods: web.dev, "Optimize Cumulative Layout Shift" (web fonts: keep the size difference between fallback and web font small with `size-adjust`, `ascent-override`, `descent-override` and `line-gap-override`); web.dev, "CSS size-adjust for @font-face" (size-adjust applies to the one face its rule imports); MDN, `size-adjust` and `ascent-override`.
- **The metric twins are named by their full names:** `local("Arimo Regular")`, `local("Cousine Regular")`, `local("Tinos Regular")` and `local("Gelasio Regular")` replace the bare family names. `local()` matches a full or PostScript name, and these fonts' full regular names include "Regular", so on a system with only the twin (ChromeOS's Croscore fonts, or Gelasio standing in for Georgia) the regular fallback never applied.
- **`capsomer/fonts`:** `fallbackFontFace` takes `widths` (`{ 600: xWidthAvg, 700: xWidthAvg }`, the new type `FontWidths`) and returns the rules for each weight range. `MEASURED` entries carry `widths`, and `LOCAL` entries carry `bold`. Called without `widths`, it returns the same single rule as before. `capsomer fallback-font` takes `--weight 700=<file>` for each heavier weight. No class or HTML contract changes, and no token changes. A site that copies `fonts.css` or `fallback-fonts.css`, or generates its rules from `MEASURED`, gets the fix when it updates.
- **React's `ChartFrame` redraws at the height its first drawing showed**, so a chart in a column narrower than its `width` no longer grows when the redraw swaps in after hydration and pushes the page down (Carrel's progress chart at 320 px: 141 px to 212 px). `draw` now gets a second argument, `fit` (`{ height }`, the first drawing's height at the frame's width, scaled down and never up, as `enarratio/redraw` does for pages drawn without React; empty on the first draw); spread it into the chart's options, `(width, fit) => barChart({ ...options, width, ...fit })`. A draw that takes only `width` still type-checks and keeps its own height, as before. New exports: `shownHeight` and the type `ChartFit`. No class, token or HTML contract changes.
- **The showcase draws with Enarratio 0.7.3** (the devDependency, from 0.7.0; the peer range `^0.5.0 || ^0.6.0 || ^0.7.0` is unchanged). Its charts no longer move the page when the enhancement layer loads: the redraw keeps the server drawing's height, a legend entry has its button's box before it is a button, and the figure holds the controls the layer adds, inert until it arrives. `css/enarratio.css` needed no change: it restyles no legend entry or control row, and the pending downloads carry `.enarratio-export`, so they get the same 44 px touch target as the working ones. A page that uses React's `ChartFrame` with `enhance={false}`, or enhance options that turn controls off, draws its figure with Enarratio's `controls: false`. No class, token or HTML contract changes.

## 0.65.0

The system map is cards, not a capsid drawing (Dustin, 2026-11-03: yes to the card-and-drill-down design). It replaces answer 3 of capsid/rulings/system-explorer-2026-10-10.md (the hexagon drawing) and the line routing of the seat rulings on #57 and #64; answer 9 (one component for both homes) and the one-tap rule of 2026-10-18 still hold.

- **System map, level 1: the network as cards.** Every system is a card (its name as a link stretched over the card, what it is for, its address, and how many parts it has and systems it uses and is used by), standing in a column with its group, then a band of the shared packages and a band of the core. No lines are drawn: pointing at a card or focusing it lights the cards it uses and is used by, each saying how in words, and mutes the rest by color. A tap opens the system.
- **System map, level 2: one system.** `systemMapHtml(data, { system })` shows Used by, Inside and Uses: each tie is the other system with its verbs as text, and the parts stand under Runs, Stores and Outside, each with what it runs on, what it does and its own lines in words. Flows over its parts are ordered lists, which Start, Next and Back step with script, lighting each step's parts. `only: "inside"` shows the parts and flows alone, and `flow: { id, step }` delivers a step lit. The columns stack where the map is narrower than 52rem (a container query). A line from a part to another system's part counts as the two systems' tie.
- **Changed HTML contract, replaced whole.** New: `data-level` and, at level 2, `data-system` on the root; `.cap-system-map-network`, `.cap-system-map-groups`, `.cap-system-map-band`, `.cap-system-map-group-count`, `.cap-system-map-cards`, `.cap-system-map-card` (and its `-name`, `-text`, `-meta`, `-domain`, `-private`, `-count` and `-tie`), `data-lit` on the root and `data-tie` on a card; `.cap-system-map-columns`, `.cap-system-map-column`, `.cap-system-map-column-name`, `.cap-system-map-ties`, `.cap-system-map-tie` (`-name`, `-verbs`), `.cap-system-map-place` (`-name`), `.cap-system-map-parts`, `.cap-system-map-part` (`-name`, `-kind`, `-text`, `-lines`), `.cap-system-map-none`, `.cap-system-map-steps`, `.cap-system-map-flow-controls` and `.cap-system-map-flow-status`. Kept: `.cap-system-map`, `-head`, `-title`, `-caption`, `-group`, `-group-name`, `-flows`, `-flow`, `-flow-name`, `-flow-text`, the root's `data-flow` and `data-step`, and the event `cap:system-map-flow`. **Removed classes:** `cap-system-map-views`, `-stage`, `-layer`, `-tile`, `-name`, `-tag`, `-edge`, `-group-label`, `-box`, `-box-name`, `-box-kind`, `-wire`, `-boundary`, `-inside-title`, `-inside-name`, `-inside-text`, `-token`, `-rim`, `-rim-name`, `-detail` (and its `-name`, `-text`, `-actions`), `-list`, `-item-name`, `-item-tag`, `-item-text`, `-item-domain` and `-item-link`. **Removed attributes:** `data-view`, `data-selected`, `data-entered`, `data-zoom`, `data-circles`, `data-insides`, `data-insides-ready`, and every tile, edge, box and wire attribute. **Removed events:** `cap:system-map-select`, `cap:system-map-enter`, `cap:system-map-leave`.
- **Removed: the export `capsomer/system-map-circles`** (`circleLayers`, `sceneOf`, `layoutScene`, `layoutKey`, `faultsOf`) and the files `system-map-circles.ts`, `system-map-circles-search.ts` and `system-map-inside.ts`. **Removed functions and constants:** `layoutMap`, `ringCells`, `seamRoute`, `hexPoints`, `spatialNext`, `linksOf`, `connectionText`, `insideScene`, `playableFlows`, `portsOf`, `rectInset`, `rectOutset`, `segmentHitsRect`, `segmentHitsBox`, `segmentFaults`, `segmentCross`, `segDist`, `segAngle`, `sideBySide`, `pointSegDist`, `tokenRoute`, `routeFrames`, `R`, `ROUTE_CLEAR`, `LINE_SEP`, `JUNCTION_END`, `SHORT_LINK` and `BEND_CLEAR`; the options `circles` and `openLabel`; the fields `short` and `tag` of a system; the controller's `select`, `enter`, `setView`. **New:** `tiesOf`, `flowsOver`, `ownerOf`, `placeOf`, `verbOf`, `count`, `VERB`, the options `system`, `only` and `flow`, the class `cap-system-map-link` on each system's name link (a card's and a tie's whole box is its target), a container's `technology` and `description` and the model's flows (each step on the ends of the line it rides; the type `ModelFlow`) kept by `modelMapData`, and the controller's `light(id)`. `controller(root)` takes no label.

## 0.64.0

The direction strip's arrow is a solid triangle, and the changes below, which were waiting under Unreleased.

- **`.cap-direction` marks each step with a solid triangle, not the "→" character.** The `li + li::before` separator is a 0.5em by 0.6em shape (`clip-path`) in the family's color (`--on`), so it is the same on every platform. No class, token or HTML contract changes.

- **A scroll region's focus ring sits inside its edge.** `base.css` gives `[role="region"][tabindex="0"]:focus-visible` an inset outline (`outline-offset: calc(var(--focus-width) * -1)`), the table's and dialog's rule, so a code box or wide table inside a box that clips its overflow still shows its whole ring. Prelum's code and table regions get it too. No class, token or HTML contract changes.
- **Showcase site: every code box can be reached and scrolled with the keyboard** (WCAG 2.1.1, area 9). The install code on the front page, each component page's Installation tabs and examples, the theme builder's output and the doc pages' code are named regions (`tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Code: …"`, Prelum's pattern) written by one helper, `codeRegion` in `site/markdown.ts`. The Installation and theme builder tab panels drop their own `tabindex`, since the code inside is now the stop (WAI-ARIA APG, tabs), and a closed example leaves the tab order until View code opens it. The accessibility statement no longer lists the limitation.

## 0.63.1

- **Prelum v0.32.1 as the dependency**, whose peer accepts Enarratio 0.7.0. 0.63.0 still depended on Prelum v0.31.4 (peer `^0.5.0 || ^0.6.0`), so an app with Enarratio 0.7.0 and no Prelum of its own (Capsid's dashboard) installed Capsomer with an invalid peer. No class, token or HTML contract changes.

## 0.63.0

Capsomer accepts Enarratio 0.7.0 (charts, area 10).

- **Enarratio 0.7.0 as the peer** (`^0.5.0 || ^0.6.0 || ^0.7.0`), and the showcase draws with it: maps (`enarratio/map`), the tactile SVG download and the forced-colors legend swatches. The chart frame's markup and behavior are unchanged. No class, token or HTML contract changes.
- **Showcase site: a breadcrumb list only where a trail shows.** A component page marks up Capsomer > Components > Name, which the visitor sees as Components > Name; Make, Accessibility and Search carry none. Prelum v0.31.4. No class, token or HTML contract changes.

## 0.62.0

Nothing scrolls sideways on a 320 px phone (WCAG 1.4.10 Reflow, area 9), breadcrumbs on a phone are one line, and displayed math keeps its spacing.

- **Long words break instead of widening the page.** `body` sets `overflow-wrap: break-word`, so an address written out as a link (github.com/DrDustinEdwards/prelum on Prelum's and Enarratio's 404 pages and Enarratio's /astro) wraps at the column's edge. `break-word` leaves every box's min-content size alone, so no table, grid or flex row lays out differently. No class, token or HTML contract changes.
- **System map: the view buttons wrap.** `.cap-system-map-views` wraps, since Plain map and Back to all systems side by side were 322 px, which pushed every Capsid system and flow page 26 px past a 320 px screen. No class or HTML contract changes.
- **Breadcrumbs on a phone are one line** (changed default). Up to 40rem wide, `.cap-breadcrumb` and the manual's direction sign (`.cap-direction`, the trail on Capsid's explorer and dustinedwards.info) keep the trail on one line: the current page (`[aria-current]`) leaves the line, since the page's heading names it, and stays in the list for a screen reader; a trail still too long scrolls sideways inside itself instead of wrapping. This follows Baymard's mobile breadcrumb testing, GOV.UK's breadcrumbs and NN/g. It replaces the wrapping trail, which on Capsid's /tools pages broke a tool's long name mid-word over three lines and ran the sign off a 320 px screen. No class or HTML contract changes.
- **Displayed math is not clipped under text spacing** (WCAG 1.4.12, area 9). Prose blocks give `.katex-display` half an em above and below instead of a quarter: with the spacing 1.4.12 asks a page to allow, KaTeX's struts no longer hold the glyphs and the formula's bottom edge was cut off (devkit-a11y's live run on Prelum's specimen, 77 of 80 px shown). No class, token or HTML contract changes.
- CI: devkit v0.21.0 in every workflow call and in `package.json`, and OpenSSF Scorecard (its scorecard.yml). The release runs devkit's release.yml with `build: true` (the same built release commit as before), which also attests the release's `npm pack` tarball and attaches it to the release. No package changes.

## 0.61.0

The markdown editor asks for alt text before it uploads an image, and the media library stores nothing undescribed (area 11).

- **Markdown editor: an image is described before it is uploaded** (AI and MCP, area 11; changed contract). The describe step now comes first: a pasted, dropped or chosen image shows a local preview with the alt field, Insert image (once there is alt text) and Insert as decorative, and only then is the file sent. `onUpload(file)` is now `onUpload(file, image, signal)`, where `image` is `{ alt, decorative }` (new type `ImageDescription`; `alt` is `""` when decorative) and `signal` aborts if the person cancels during the upload; `imageMarkdown` also gets `decorative`. An app sends `alt` and `decorative` with the file, so a server that refuses an undescribed upload (site-api 0.19 and later) accepts it, and a cancelled image is never uploaded. A failed upload is an alert inside the step, which keeps the alt text for a retry, instead of closing it. The toolbar hint reads "Asks for alt text, then uploads the file". No class or token changes; `.cap-md-upload-error` now sits inside `.cap-md-upload-fields`.
- **Media library: nothing is uploaded undescribed** (area 11; changed HTML contract). The upload and import forms' Alt text is `required`, and each gains a second submit, Upload as decorative and Import as decorative (`name="decorative" value="on" formnovalidate`, the picker's pattern), in a new `.cap-media-lib-upload-actions` row; with script, choosing decorative clears any typed alt, since site-api 0.19+ refuses a file that is both and one that is neither. The help no longer says a picture can be stored without alt text. A crop saved as a new file sends the alt text or decorative choice of the file it was cut from, which 0.19+ servers otherwise refused. New class only.

## 0.60.0

Each site's footer links its own accessibility statement, and the showcase serves one (links and accessibility, area 9).

- **The family footer can link the site's own statement.** `familyFooterHtml({ statement: "/accessibility" })` points the Site group's Accessibility link at the site's own page instead of dustinedwards.info's, and shows it even where `published` leaves the family's page out. A statement speaks for one site (W3C WAI, "Developing an Accessibility Statement"). Left out, nothing changes. New option only; no class or HTML contract changes.
- **The showcase's statement** at `/accessibility/`: Prelum's `accessibilityStatement` (0.30, `capsomer: true`, `links: "marked"`), the open findings of devkit-a11y's 2026-11-01 run as its known limitations, linked from the footer and listed in the sitemap. Prelum (`@drdustinedwards/site-helpers`) is pinned at v0.30.1 for it.
- **Pagination no longer styles the page column.** `.cap-page` names both the family page column (`css/utilities.css`) and a pagination item, so a page that loaded `capsomer/pagination.css` and wrapped its content in `.cap-page` became one unwrapping inline-flex line (dustinedwards.info's /research/phages scrolled 5,000 px sideways on a phone, failing WCAG 1.4.10 Reflow). The item's rules now apply only inside `.cap-pagination`, at the same specificity (`:where`). No class or HTML contract changes.

## 0.59.0

The footer's Lab bench link moves from `/lab` to `/lab-bench`, embeds keep each player's shape (area 4), and the shared charts accept Enarratio 0.6 and redraw on the states page (area 10).

- **Enarratio 0.6 accepted** (shared charts, area 10). The optional `enarratio` peer is `^0.5.0 || ^0.6.0`, so a site on Enarratio 0.6.0 installs Capsomer without an override; the showcase builds against 0.6.0, so its enhanced charts gain Enarratio's Play as sound. No class, token or HTML contract changes.
- **Chart frame states page: every bar chart redraws to its column** (shared charts, area 10). The narrow column, the open table and the ten family charts carry Enarratio's redraw spec like the bar, line and area specimens, so at 320 px they keep 12 px text instead of scaling to 9.5 px or scrolling (the live chart check's twelve C12 findings). Specimen markup only; no class, token or HTML contract changes.
- **Embeds keep each player's shape** (writing and rendering, area 4). The player (`capsomer/player.css`) now draws Prelum 0.28's embed card (`.embed-poster`, `.embed-play`, `.embed-label` inside `a.embed-link`) with the same rules as `.cap-player-facade`, and prose blocks size it as the player it becomes, so nothing moves on the click: a video 16:9, a TikTok 9:16, a Google map 4:3, an X, Bluesky or Instagram post 4:5 at most 34rem wide, and a song or a podcast a strip at its player's height (Spotify 152 px for a track or an episode and 352 px for a list, SoundCloud 166, Apple Podcasts 175 for an episode and 450 for a show, Amazon Music 352, the height in its Share > Embed code). The player's iframe takes the same size. A video, map or post with no poster is held at the same size and names the service and title at its center, as click-to-load placeholders do. A link stored before Prelum 0.28 stays the centered card. New class rules only.
- **Footer: the Lab bench link is `/lab-bench`.** `WORKSPACE_LINKS` points the Lab bench entry at `/lab-bench`, where dustinedwards.info now serves the lab members' workspace, so `/lab` is free for other uses. The path is one exported constant, `LAB_BENCH_PATH` (`capsomer/behavior/family-footer`), which dustinedwards.info imports so the footer and its routes cannot disagree. The link's label, name and icon are unchanged. A changed link target on one footer entry; no token or class is removed or renamed and no HTML contract changes.

## 0.58.0

Link style as tokens, so a site outside the network sets its own.

- **Link style is five tokens (group `link`).** `--link-weight`, `--link-decoration`, `--link-hover-bg`, `--link-pressed-bg` and `--link-tint-pad`, read by `css/base.css` where it had literals. The defaults are the values it had, so no site changes. A site outside the network sets its own in its `SITES` entry (components/link/link.md, "A site's own links"). Added tokens only; no class or HTML contract changes.

## 0.57.0

The error page says when the database is down (area 8), and the email-change mails are one template (area 3), images and media (area 6).

- **Error page: the database down** (area 8, I3). `errorWords` gives a 503 whose statusText names the database (`Database unavailable`, what site-runtime 0.62.0's `databaseDown(error)` throws when D1 is down or reset) "The site's data is unavailable" with a sentence that says to try again in a few minutes, and Try again; any other 503 is "This page is unavailable for now". A site gets it by rethrowing D1's transient errors through `databaseDown` in its loaders. New specimen `database`. The region's `cap-error-page` class is now named as the marker devkit's ops-audit reads for N6 (the shared error page); no class or HTML contract changes.
- **New email template: `emailChange`** (`capsomer/email`; sign-in and access, area 3, gap 14). The two mails of an email change, which Foxing, Foxhound and TXASM each wrote locally: `step: "current"` (with `newEmail`) asks the current address to approve, `step: "new"` asks the new address to confirm. It says when the link expires and never that it works once, since Better Auth's change links work until they expire. It takes `copy` like the others. States page: two new frames. No class, token or HTML contract changes.
- **Images and media** (images and media, area 6; the verifier's gaps of 2026-10-31, sections 5, 7 and 9).
  - **A decorative pick is written `![""](url)`**, not `![](url)`, by the editor's default image Markdown and by the copy snippets (`copySnippets` takes `decorative`). Prelum 0.28 refuses `![](url)` as missing alt text.
  - **The picker no longer suggests alt text from the file name** (B2). The alt step said "Suggested: launch hero" for `launch-hero.jpg`, and so did the media inspector; a file's name says nothing of what the picture shows. The step now asks for alt text or Insert as decorative, and where the site's model can suggest (`offers.suggestions`) it links Suggest alt text (`?pick=<id>&suggest=1`) and shows the model's words with Use it, which fills the field for the person to read and edit. A host shows that suggestion by loading the kit with `inspect` set to the picked file when the address has `pick` and `suggest`; `data.suggestions` is then read when `data.inspected` is the picked file. The upload form no longer says alt text can wait. Removed export: `suggestedAlt` (from `capsomer/behavior/media`); a document's link text in the copy snippets is still made from its name.
  - **New component: picture** (`capsomer/picture.css`). `img.has-lqip` (the class Prelum already writes) shows the stored placeholder and main color from the image's own style attribute until its pixels paint, on any page, not only in a post: `style="background-image:url(&quot;data:…&quot;);background-color:#rrggbb"`. `.cap-motion` wraps an animated image with a still (`img.cap-motion-still`, lazy) and a Pause checkbox (`label.cap-motion-toggle > input.cap-motion-pause`), which works with no script (WCAG 2.2.2); with reduced motion the still shows and the control is hidden. New classes only.
  - **New component: player** (`capsomer/player.css`, `capsomer/react/player`). `Video`, `Audio` and `Embed` for JSX pages, and the markup Prelum's `:::video`, `:::audio` and `::embed` are to write: `figure.cap-player[data-kind]`; a video with its poster, width, height and `preload="none"`; audio with `preload="none"` and its transcript link (`.cap-player-transcript`); an embed as `a.cap-player-facade` with the site's own poster (`img.cap-player-poster`), a play button (`.cap-player-play`) and the service and title (`.cap-player-label`), keeping `data-embed-src` and `data-embed-title`, and the player in its place on a click. Spotify and SoundCloud (`data-service`) are a strip, not 16:9. Prose blocks keep their `.media` and `.embed-link` rules for HTML already stored. New classes only.
- **Site: headers from site-runtime's registry, and integrity on every script** (area 2). The Worker is `staticSite({ site: capsomer })` with the TDMRep header, and `npm run site` writes `site-dist/_headers` with `site-runtime-headers --site capsomer`, so `scripts/security-headers.ts` is gone. The registry adds COEP (Report-Only) with `report-to`, `Integrity-Policy-Report-Only`, and a Report-Only policy with Trusted Types and `default-src 'none'`. Every script and modulepreload the site build writes, `theme.js` included, carries a sha384 `integrity` and `crossorigin` (site-runtime's `subresourceIntegrity()` Vite plugin). Site only: the package is unchanged.

## 0.56.0

The accessibility release (area 9 of the completeness plan, 2026-10-31).

- **The sweep (`test/sweep.spec.ts`), blocking in CI.** Every states page and page-level specimen is checked for reflow at 320px, right-to-left spill, WCAG 1.4.12 text spacing, touch targets on a coarse pointer, forced colors (axe and a visible focus outline, both themes) and React wrapper parity with the HTML contract; the per-spec sideways-scroll checks it covers are gone, and the forced-colors screenshots are now taken by the sweep (`playwright.review.config.ts` removed). `expectNoAxeViolations` takes `disableRules`. `capsomer report` runs from an installed package without devDependencies, no longer counts Capsomer's own classes, markup or imports (`cap-btn`, `cap-chip`, `cap-field`, `app-*`, a site that renders ErrorPage) as own copies, reads double-quoted `replaces` patterns correctly and skips `*.min.js`; the `replaces` patterns in 21 doc pages are tightened (whole class names; dead or noisy ones fixed or dropped). Forced colors: `.cap-range` and the no-script menu's items show an outline on focus (their rings were a box-shadow or a tint).
- **The sweep's findings, fixed in the components.** Reflow: the breadcrumb and the tree no longer widen a grid or flex parent to their uncut names (`min-inline-size: 0`), the media inspector's pane goes at once instead of sliding past the window as it closes, and the people page's history keeps its status words inside a phone's width. Right to left, the collapsed shell rail's tooltip slides from the rail by its inline-start offset, not a transform under `:dir(rtl)` (the site's build lowers `:dir()` to `:lang()`, which missed a page that sets only `dir`). Text spacing (WCAG 1.4.12): a cut name takes a second line before it is cut (breadcrumb link and page, row title and detail, so the history list too, media tile name, admin menu label); a listbox option's label, the slash menu's address, a tree label, the moderation queue's and mentions list's excerpt and the posts list's excerpt wrap and are never cut; the site header's logo is held to 160px only by its mark, and grows taller rather than clipping; `.cap-dialog-body` holds what is positioned in it, so a `.cap-sr-only` text can no longer give a dialog a scroll of its own. Targets on touch: the table's example and the permission matrix's view switch use the contract's markup (`label.cap-check`; `.cap-seg-options` inside `fieldset.cap-seg`, which the permission matrix's HTML, React and doc page lacked); an interactive badge's hit area is no longer clipped by the badge (`overflow: visible` on `a.cap-pill`, `button.cap-pill`), and where the target is taller than the badge (a coarse pointer) a block margin keeps wrapped lines of them a whole target apart; the system map's item link, the manual's cue name link, the admin shell's trail links and the media inspector's "used in" links are `--target` tall; the site header's links are at least `--target` tall; a link in the moderation queue's open-row facts reaches `--target` with block padding. Forced colors: the media grid's active tile and the moderation queue's selected row mark themselves with their border, so the focus outline still shows on them; the posts list's selected row takes the table's Highlight edge.
- **Changed HTML contract.** The admin shell's menu link puts its `.cap-admin-tip` before `.cap-admin-count` (collapsed, the visible words read in the order of the name, WCAG 2.5.3). PermissionMatrix renders `.cap-seg-options` in its view switch. The posts list's update view writes its withheld reason as `ul.cap-posts-withheld` with one `li`, as the doc page has it. React `SearchField` names its `role="search"` landmark with its label when `landmark` is not given. The doc pages now show what is rendered: the tree's enhanced branch (`role="group"` on `.cap-tree-group`, APG) and the site header's search link (`a.cap-site-header-icon.cap-site-header-search-button`). Prose blocks follow Prelum 0.25.0: `div.table-scroll` and every `pre` are named, focusable regions (`tabindex="0"`, `role="region"`, `aria-label="Table: …"` or `"Code: …"`).
- **The sweep's React check covers every wrapper.** A wrapper with no `examples.tsx` is rendered from one small fixture on the server and compared with its HTML contract by class, role and name (44 wrappers; `link` and `content` have no states page to compare with). The admin shell's tip test reads its corners inside the 6px radius (it missed at some sub-pixel positions).
- **Links are semibold, not underlined.** `a` has `text-decoration: none`, and a text link (an `a` with no `cap-` class) is `font-weight: 600` in `--accent-text` (`css/base.css`); the links in a banner's, a dialog description's and an empty state's words, `.cap-link-btn`, `.cap-catalog-clear`, `.cap-drop-browse` and the email's links are semibold too. Every underline on a link is gone, on hover and focus included (attention list, breadcrumb, button's link variant, error summary, family footer, feed, flag list, manual, row list, status link, table open link, the site's links); hover keeps its color change, and the focus ring is unchanged. The sweep checks that every text link is semibold and no link is underlined.
- **The link system** (Dustin, 2026-10-31; components/link/link.md, now the link system's page with a states page). Every text link (no `cap-` class, or any `a[data-link]`, outside a `nav`) keeps the link color and semibold and gains a hover tint (`--accent-soft` with a 2px shadow of it, 3px radius, cloned onto wrapped lines), a pressed tint (`--accent-5`, `:active`) and a visited color; in forced colors it underlines on hover. New token `--accent-visited` in every family, light and dark and under `prefers-contrast: more` (the link's hue turned 90 degrees toward violet, 30 degrees clear of the error red, Lc 75 on every ground, at least 7 from `--accent-text` and 10 from `--text` in OKLab; `apart()` in `tokens/palette-core.mjs`, covered by the palette check and report), and new checked pairs: link under the pointer and pressed, pressed tag, visited tag. New stylesheet `capsomer/link.css`. New contract `data-link="away|family|file|jump|email|phone|lang|footnote|xref|address|next|title|tag"` on an `a`, with `data-site="<id>"` for a family site (ids in the new `FAMILY_LINK_SITES`, `components/family-footer/family-footer.ts`; icons drawn in link.css for dustinedwards, germomics, julieedwards, foxing, foxhound, txasm, bsw, carrel, capsid, capsomer and prelum; enarratio has none yet and takes the family arrow turned out). New classes: `.cap-link-meta` (a file's format and size or a language's name, a sibling after the link), `.cap-link-icon` (an outside site's fetched icon, the link's first child, replacing ↗) and `.cap-footnote-ref`; GFM's `data-footnote-ref` and `data-footnote-backref` are styled as they are. Without the attribute CSS finds away (`rel~="external"`, `target="_blank"`), file (`download` and common extensions), jump (`href^="#"` in running text), email and phone (`mailto:`, `tel:`), footnote (`sup > a`) and title (`:is(h1, h2, h3, h4) > a`); marks are hidden from assistive technology (`content: "↗" / ""`), and the words are in the contract (`<span class="cap-sr-only"> (opens another site)</span>`). `data-force="visited"` and `data-force="focus"` join hover and pressed as specimen hooks on a link. The next and title arrows are the family arrow (`css/utilities.css`). Changed: the catalog's active-filter chip is the tag pill (its look moved from catalog.css to link.css, which a catalog page now loads: ring edge, `--accent-soft` fill, accent text); the breadcrumb's links and its folded list, the anchor bar's links and the family footer's links now take the `--accent-soft` hover tint. The sweep checks every text link's base cues, each kind's mark, that marks are hidden, that visited and pressed differ from rest, and that marks show in forced colors. The site's doc pages write the away contract on links to other sites.
- **Charts after the move to Enarratio 0.4.** In `.cap-chart`, a data table wider than the frame scrolls inside it instead of the page (the frame is one `minmax(0, 1fr)` column), an open range picker takes its own row, the CSV link is a full target, and Enarratio's buttons (legend, controls, range) reach `--target` tall, 44px on a touch screen (`--enarratio-padding-button`).

The form components the accessibility audit found missing (area 9, rows B6, B7, B10, B11, B16): an error summary, a password with Show, a character count, a one-time code, a number with steppers, a search field, a date input and a date picker.

- **Error summary (new component, `capsomer/error-summary.css`, `capsomer/behavior/error-summary`, `capsomer/react/error-summary`).** GOV.UK's pattern: a titled list of links to every field in error, at the top of the form, taking focus; the page title starts "Error: " until the form is sent. New classes: `.cap-error-summary`, `.cap-error-summary-title`, `.cap-error-summary-list`; new contract `[data-cap="error-summary"]` with `role="alert"`, `tabindex="-1"` and `aria-labelledby` its title (`autofocus` when the server renders it). Functions: `enhance`, `wireSummary`, `summaryFor`, `showSummary`, `hideSummary`, `setTitleError`. React: `ErrorSummary({ errors, title })`.
- **Changed: Field's submit check.** A `form[data-cap="field"]` whose submit finds two or more fields in error now writes the error summary (the form's own `.cap-error-summary`, or a new one as its first child) and moves focus to it, and the messages beside the fields are no longer announced (`role="alert"`) as well. One field in error behaves as before: focus moves to it and its message is announced. Either way the page title takes "Error: ". A field whose part is fixed while another part is still wrong keeps that part's message (`clearError`).
- **New Field parts** (in `field.css`, `field.ts`, `field.react.tsx`; documented on the Field page, with a section on autocomplete tokens for personal data, WCAG 1.3.5):
  - Password: an input group with `button[data-cap-part="reveal"][aria-controls]`, `hidden` until script shows it; Show and Hide change the type, the words and the name ("Show password", "Hide password") and are said in the status region; a sent form hides it again. React `PasswordInput`.
  - Character count: `data-maxlength` or `data-maxwords` on the control and new class `.cap-field-count` (`data-over="true"` over the limit, with the error's icon); the status region says the count a second after typing stops; over the limit is wrong on submit (`data-error-too-long`). React `CharacterCount`.
  - One-time code: `.cap-input[data-variant="code"]` with `autocomplete="one-time-code"` and `inputmode="numeric"`, one input, paste allowed.
  - Number: `button[data-cap-part="step-down"]` and `button[data-cap-part="step-up"]` in a number input's group, named, `tabindex="-1"` and `hidden` until script; Page Up, Page Down, Home and End (APG spinbutton). React `NumberField`.
  - Search: new class `.cap-search` (`role="search"`, a form or a div) with `button[data-cap-part="clear"]`; Esc clears. React `SearchField`.
  - Date input: new classes `.cap-date-input` (`data-error-date`) and `.cap-date-input-part`, inputs with `data-cap-part="day|month|year"`; per-part errors and a real-date check. React `DateInput`.
  - New exports in `capsomer/behavior/field`: `dateFrom`, `checkDate`, `setRevealed`, `revealedText`, `countText`, `limitOf`, `stepValue`, `keyValue`, `setValue`. React `Field` takes a function as its child and `after`.
- **Date picker (new component, `capsomer/date-picker.css`, `capsomer/behavior/date-picker`, `capsomer/react/date-picker`).** The APG date picker dialog on Capsomer's Dialog, enhancing a text input that takes 2026-11-14, so it works with no script. New contract: `.cap-date-picker[data-cap="date-picker"]` around an input group with `button[data-cap-part="open"]` (`hidden` until script), `data-min`, `data-max` and `data-error-date` on the input. New classes, built by script: `.cap-date-picker-dialog`, `.cap-date-picker-head`, `.cap-date-picker-grid`. Functions: `enhance`, `wirePicker`, `parseDate`, `isoDate`, `addMonths`, `moveDate`. React `DatePicker`.

The foundation's accessibility gaps from the area 9 audit (2026-10-30).

- **One switch for every single-key shortcut (WCAG 2.1.4).** The shortcuts sheet's switch (`setSingleKeys`) now sets `data-cap-single-keys` on `<html>`, and the stored choice is put there again when any component with single keys loads. Every component's own keys obey it: row list `j`/`k` (and so the attention list, flag list and publish gate), media `x`, draft compare `n`/`p`, history list, moderation queue, mentions list, message `z`, site header `T` (`themeKey`) and admin shell `G` and a digit (`enhance()` and the React `jumpKeys`). `singleKeysOff()` now lives in `shortcuts.ts` (row-list re-exports it). `data-cap-undo-key="off"` still turns off one region's `z`. Changed contract: setting `data-cap-single-keys="off"` on `<html>` yourself also turns off the registry's own single keys.
- **Font sizes in rem** (WCAG 1.4.4): `--fs-h3`, `--fs-h2`, `--fs-prose`, `--fs-prose-h2`, `--fs-page`, `--fs-label`, `--fs-detail`, `--fs-body`, `--fs-lead`, the Foxhound and Portal site layers' sizes, the Make page's presets and `.cap-mono` are rem, the same size at the default 16px. `--fs-input-coarse` is `max(1rem, 16px)`. `tokens.json` writes rem as `{ unit: "rem" }`. No token renamed.
- **Focus is never hidden under a bottom bar (WCAG 2.4.11).** `scroll-padding-bottom` on the page while the shell's phone tab bar, the site header's tab bar or a bottom bulk bar is shown.
- **New: `.cap-sr-only-focusable`**, hidden like `.cap-sr-only` until it or something in it has focus. **New: `.cap-skip`**, the skip link for a page with neither shell nor site header. `.cap-shell-skip` and `.cap-site-header-skip` are now drawn by `utilities.css` (one rule, same look); their markup is unchanged.
- **Forced colors** blocks in shortcuts (key caps), family footer (the current site), share preview (the image's place) and table (a selected row, which was a tint and a shadow).
- **Right to left.** Logical properties replace physical ones that misplaced things: admin shell and site header badges, attention list and panel source lines, dialog side sheets (their edge and their slide), the disclosure and row list chevrons (mirrored), feed times, people roles, permission matrix and stat tile headings, the shell's labels, tips, counts and badges, and the switch's thumb. `--cap-dialog-dir` is new on `.cap-dialog`.
- **Right to left survives a site's build.** Vite 8 lowers `:dir(rtl)` to `:lang(ar, he, ...)` for its default browser targets, so a page that sets only `dir="rtl"` lost the mirrored chevrons, sheet, switch thumb and arrows. Each `:dir(rtl)` rule (dialog, pagination, switch, select, menu, meter, breadcrumb, disclosure, row-list) also matches `:where([dir="rtl"])`.
- **Reduced motion:** the letterhead's scroll-linked fade is off under `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce`.
- **A 16px checkbox's hit area is `--target`** (44px on a touch screen), drawn the same size, for a row's box with no visible label.
- **Disclosure:** an accordion's titles may be headings (`<summary><h3>...</h3></summary>`), documented and in the specimen.
- **New: toolbar** (`components/toolbar`: `.cap-toolbar`, `.cap-toolbar-sep`, `attachToolbar`, `rovingIndex`), the APG toolbar with one tab stop and a pressed look for toggle buttons. The markdown editor's toolbar uses it; `rovingIndex` moved there (still exported by the markdown editor) and now swaps Left and Right right to left.
- **New: tag group** (`components/tag-group`: `.cap-tags`, `.cap-tags-label`, `.cap-tags-list`, `.cap-tags-empty`, `.cap-tag`, `.cap-tag-name`, `.cap-tag-remove`), removable tags with arrows, Delete or Backspace, focus kept and Undo.
- **New: summary list** (`components/summary-list`: `.cap-summary-list`, `.cap-summary-actions`, `.cap-summary-missing`), GOV.UK's summary list with Change links. **Changed contract:** the detail panel's `.cap-detail-record` is drawn by `summary-list.css` now, so load it with `detail-panel.css`.
- **New: step indicator** (`components/step-indicator`: `.cap-steps`, `.cap-steps-list`, `.cap-step`, `.cap-step-name`, `.cap-steps-heading`, `.cap-steps-counter`), USWDS's, with `aria-current="step"`.
- **Docs:** the link page (`components/link/link.md`), the back link (on the breadcrumb page), and a Refused patterns section in DEFAULTS.md (carousel, feed, window splitter, menubar, context menu, grid, tree grid, two-thumb range slider, phase banner; the cookie notice is open). The bulk bar, history list and mentions list pages no longer claim a switch that did nothing.

- **`capsomer/behavior/chart-enhance` resolves.** The `./behavior/*` pattern looks for `components/chart-enhance/`, but the module lives in `components/chart-frame/`, so the import the charts entry documents failed in a site's build. An explicit export now points at it. No other public entry lives outside its own folder.
- **Site only: deployed by devkit's shared deploy** (no package change). CI's new `deploy` job (worker-deploy.yml v0.20.18) replaces Cloudflare Workers Builds, so the site gets the D1 sites' live gate: it deploys with `DEPLOY_SHA`, waits for `/health` (site-runtime 0.60.0's `staticSite`) to report the commit, checks the home page and rolls back on a refusal. IndexNow moves from its own workflow to a job after the deploy. A push to `main` no longer cancels the run before it. Dormant until the `CF_DEPLOY` variable is set (DEPLOY.md).
- **Charts: one theming path, enhanced by default, text-safe series** (the charts checklist, area 10; the verifier's gaps of 2026-10-31, sections 1, 4, 5 and 6). Needs Enarratio 0.5.
  - **`capsomer/enarratio.css` is now the whole chart stylesheet**, generated by `tokens/palette.mjs`: it imports Enarratio's `enarratio/base.css` (from the site's installed Enarratio) and the token mapping, and adds gridlines, each series' ink and the color-pair rules. A site imports it after `tokens.css` and drops its own `enarratio/base.css` import, `stylesheet(capsomerTheme)`, hand-written `[data-enarratio-colors*=...]` rules, `--enarratio-series-N` overrides and gridline CSS. The bare `@import "enarratio/base.css"` needs a bundler (Vite resolves it). Renamed file: the token mapping that `tokens.css` imports moved from `css/enarratio.css` to `css/enarratio-tokens.css` (`tokens.css` still loads it; no export changes).
  - **Gridlines are an attribute**: `data-gridlines="none | x | y | both"` on the frame or any wrapper (`gridlines` on `ChartFrame` and `frameHtml`). The default is capsomerTheme's `y`, so a page that loaded only `enarratio/base.css` with the mapping loses its vertical (x) gridlines: a visible change.
  - **Color pairs**: `colors: { A: "var(--crit)" }` with any of `--series-1` to `--series-8`, `--ok`, `--warn`, `--crit`, `--info`, `--nodata` or `--accent` is themed by `capsomer/enarratio.css` (the rule `colorStyles()` would write, with the label's ink). Other colors still need `colorStyles(html)`.
  - **New tokens `--series-ink-1` to `--series-ink-8`**, every family, both themes and "increase contrast": each series' hue moved only as far as text needs (4.5:1 and APCA Lc 60 on the surface, ground and raised surface). Words Enarratio writes in a series color (direct labels, curve labels) take it, so #cc4f0c (4.42:1 light) and #8c5fd2 (3.99:1 dark) no longer reach text; marks and swatches keep the series colors. Forced colors keep Enarratio's system colors.
  - **The ramp starts at 3:1**: `--ramp-1` to `--ramp-5` are re-spread in every family so the least step is 3:1 against the surface, ground and raised surface (it was 1.2 to 1.3:1), each step still further from the surface than the last. Heat strips and heatmaps are darker in light and lighter in dark: a visible change. `npm run check` now checks the least step, every ink and the no-data color (3:1).
  - **`ChartFrame` enhances by default**: it loads `enarratio/enhance` after mount and runs it on its figure after each draw. New props `enhance` (`true`, `false`, or Enarratio's `EnhanceOptions`) and `gridlines`. A site that passed `onDraw` to call `enhance` (Foxing's `enhanceChart`) deletes it. HTML contract: the frame carries `data-enhance="self"` or `"off"`, `data-gridlines` when set, and `data-state="refused"` when Enarratio refuses the chart.
  - **New module `capsomer/behavior/chart-enhance`** for pages drawn on the server: import it once and every `.cap-chart` without `data-enhance` is enhanced, including frames added later. Exports `enhanceCharts(root, options)` and `watchCharts(options)`. Listed in `sideEffects`, so a bundler keeps the bare import.
  - **Server frames redraw to their width** (needs Enarratio 0.5): `frameHtml(chart, { redraw: { chart: "bar" | "line" | "area", options } })` writes Enarratio's `data-enarratio-redraw` spec on the frame, and `capsomer/behavior/chart-enhance` draws such a frame again at its column's width with `enarratio/redraw` (the code Prelum shares), so a 440 drawing in a 320 px column keeps its 12 px text. `enhanceCharts` returns those charts too. `components/chart-frame/chart-enhance.ts` joins `sideEffects`: the showcase imports the source, and its bare import was being dropped, so no server frame on the site was enhanced. The peer range is `enarratio ^0.5.0`; an `overrides` entry gives Prelum v0.24.0 (which still names ^0.4.0 as an optional peer) the same 0.5.0 until a Prelum release names ^0.5.0.
  - **Inline primitives** (components/README.md): a sparkline in a stat tile and an uptime strip in a table row are `role="img"` named by their numbers and need no frame. Stat tile: `.cap-tile-chart` is no longer `aria-hidden`, the `.cap-sr-only` summary and the tile's `aria-describedby` are gone, and `chartSummary` now names the slot (`role="img"`, `aria-label`). Renamed class: the tile with its table, `.cap-chart[data-variant="tile"]`, is now `.cap-data`. Usage meter: its table sits in `.cap-data`, not `.cap-chart` (a meter is not a chart). `.cap-data` is new: numbers behind "Show data" for something that is not a chart.
  - **A figure's links take the family's link style**: `capsomer/enarratio.css` sets the source link and "Download CSV" (and any link in a note or caption) semibold in `--accent-text` with no underline, over Enarratio's `color: inherit`. Tick labels are drawn in proportional figures: the interface face's tabular set gave the comma and the 1 a full figure's width ("1 , 600"). Both rules are unlayered, as Enarratio's base rules are: a visible change.
  - **A figure's links are in the link system**: the source link, "Download CSV" and Enarratio 0.5's "Download SVG" and "Download PNG" buttons take every link's states (the `--accent-soft` hover tint, the `--accent-5` pressed tint, `--accent-visited` once followed, links only, and the shared focus ring), from `css/base.css`: the unlayered rule in `capsomer/enarratio.css` now sets `revert-layer` on the properties Enarratio sets, instead of repeating the values. A visible change. A data table under a stat tile or meter (`.cap-data`) scrolls inside its box, as a frame's does.
  - **`ChartFrame` fills its column**: `drawWidth` returns the measured whole-pixel width (never under `minWidth`) instead of 40 px steps, which left up to 39 px empty beside the chart. A visible change.
  - **Showcase**: the "Builds per day" React frame and the refusal are drawn when the page is built, so they read with scripts off; the timeline specimen is drawn at 440 px, so its text stays 12 px; the inline uptime strips are named with the percent shown beside them; the family specimens carry a source and CSV link, load the families' scopes (they all showed purple's surface and ramp) and show each family's ramp under its eight series, which are the same in every family; the pages load `capsomer/enarratio.css` and `chart-enhance` as a site does.
- **Numbers no longer spread their commas** ("61 , 400", "96 . 1%"). Schibsted Grotesk's tabular figures give the comma, period and colon a full figure's width, and no feature or alternate narrows them. New tokens: `--figures` (`proportional-nums`), for a number on its own, which every component that set `tabular-nums` now takes (stat tile, meter, usage meter, uptime strip, status, chips, counts, pagination, time and the rest); and `--figures-column` (`tabular-nums`), for a column whose digits must line up: the table's `data-num` cells, prose tables and `.cap-num`. A site whose face has narrow tabular punctuation may set `--figures: tabular-nums`. Chart tick labels take `--figures`. A visible change; no class or HTML contract changes.
- **Site only: the Worker is site-runtime's `staticSite`** (no package change). site-runtime v0.52.0's shared static-site Worker replaces `worker/index.ts`'s own code and now runs first for every page (`run_worker_first: ["/*", "!/assets/*"]`), so the hashed files stay with the asset server and keep their immutable cache. Every page carries `Speculation-Rules: "/speculation-rules.json"` (the site's own prefetch rules, in place of Cloudflare's generic ones), `Server-Timing`, and a preload `Link` header for the home page's stylesheet and its Schibsted Grotesk and Source Serif 4 faces, which Early Hints answers with a 103. The build reads their hashed names from `site-dist` into `worker/preload.generated.json` (`scripts/preload.ts`). The 404 page and any page asked for with a cookie are `private, no-store`, where the 404 was `public, max-age=0, must-revalidate`. The pages look the same: full-page screenshots of the home page before and after differ only in a test count the page reads at runtime from the results branch, which CI rewrote between the two.
- **New component: prose blocks** (`capsomer/prose-blocks.css`). Every class Prelum's renderer writes is now styled inside `<article data-context="prose">`, in both themes, every family and at a phone's width: headings with their own links, task lists, scrolling tables, code with a title and marked lines, the five callouts, figures with credits and wide or full widths, galleries, video, audio and transcripts, embeds that load nothing until a click, button links, bookmarks, details, FAQs, footnotes, the editorial pack's side note, pull quote and swatch, and `render/enhance`'s reading progress, Copy, footnote preview, image viewer and selection link. Links are left to Capsomer's link system. A site imports the file and deletes its own copy. `.sr-only` (GFM's footnotes heading) is hidden inside the prose context, as `.cap-sr-only` is. New classes only; nothing is removed or renamed.
- **Showcase site: Prelum v0.23.0** (no package change). The site's `@drdustinedwards/site-helpers` pin moves from v0.22.0 to v0.23.0, where Prelum wraps `:::chart` output in this frame (`div.cap-chart > div.cap-chart-figure`) and sets `RENDER_VERSION` to 9. The site uses Prelum only for search, head tags and icons, so no page changes.
- **Samples and docs use neutral content instead of one site's subject matter** (no HTML contract change). The specimens, specs and doc examples of admin-shell, approval-sheet, attention-list, avatar, banner, breadcrumb, bulk-bar, button, catalog, chart-frame, chips, combobox, command-menu, confirm-dialog, detail-panel, dialog, disclosure, draft-compare, drop-zone, email, error-page, feed, field, flag-list, history-list and listbox drop phage, lab, podcast, book, payment-recovery and family-site names for example.com domains, made-up people (Alex Rivera) and neutral topics. The catalog's specimen is an article library (`fixture.ts` exports `ARTICLES` and `articleCatalog`) in place of a protocol library.

## 0.55.0

The catalog's filter tray link reaches its results (writing and rendering, area 4).

- **Showcase site: the family icon set** (no package change). Prelum v0.22.0's icons, drawn from the letterhead's hexagon on the magenta family's `#752c6a` (white on it for the Apple touch and maskable icons), are in `site/public`; every page links them with `iconLinks()` and `site.webmanifest` comes from `webManifest()`. The inline data-URL favicon is gone.
- **Catalog: the results region has its id** (`{id}-results`). The filter tray's "Show N" link pointed at `#{id}-results`, which nothing carried, so on a phone it closed the tray without moving to the results; Prelum's live check flagged it on all eight dustinedwards.info research pages. HTML contract: the results region gains `id="{id}-results"`.

## 0.54.0
