Recova's calculator slider.
- **New: solid filter chips** (`data-variant="solid"` on `.cap-chips`): the pressed chip fills with the primary color without the tick, and an unpressed chip's edge turns to the accent under the pointer. Recova's product filter.
- **Site `foxhound`** names Foxhound's metric-matched fallback faces (`"DM Sans Fallback"`, `"DM Mono Fallback"`) after DM Sans and DM Mono, and takes Recova's 12px card corners (`--radius-l`), its card shadow (a lit top edge in dark), a 4px lift and an amber edge on linked cards, and the amber sweep on the default button.

## 0.18.0

**Foxhound back to amber** (Dustin, 2026-10-25: Foxhound keeps Recova's amber and its old sizes; teal goes to capsid.dustinedwards.info).

- **New: the `amber` family.** `data-family="amber"` switches an element's subtree to Recova's amber `#f0b429`, in both themes; `capsomer/families.css` now imports it, or take it alone as `capsomer/family-amber.css`. Dark is its default theme, where step 9 is `#f0b429` itself (10:1 on the dark ground). The seed is under 3:1 on the light surfaces, so in the light theme step 9 is the same hue and chroma darkened to 3:1 (`#b68508`), from the new `lightStep9()`; every other family keeps its seed, unchanged. White cannot sit on amber, so the family's primary button carries the dark ground as its label in both themes (new family option `label: "dark"`), as Recova's did, and fills with step 9 (Recova's `#f0b429` in dark) and hovers to step 10. Its accent text is Recova's: `#8a5c06` in light (the step 11 anchor) and `#f0b429` itself in dark (10:1), not the paler step 11 the other families use there. Every pair passes WCAG 2.2 in both themes. `EmailFamily` gains `"amber"`. The teal family is unchanged; its note now names capsid.dustinedwards.info.
- **Site `foxhound` moves to the amber family and Recova's sizes:** `--display` DM Sans (its page titles fell back to a system face, since Foxhound loads no Schibsted Grotesk), `--fs-detail` 13px, `--fs-body` 14px, `--control` and `--control-h` 38px, `--pad-x` 14px, `--pad-y` 8px, `--pad-card` 20px, `--row-h` 44px, with 44px controls kept under a coarse pointer. Foxhound puts `data-family="amber"` where it had `"teal"`.
- **Compact density, one step roomier** (Dustin, 2026-10-25: "revisit Capsomer's spacing... now we are just adjusting to get things just right"). The default compact set changes on every site that does not set its own: `--fs-body` 13px to 14px, `--fs-detail` 12px to 13px, `--control` and `--control-h` 32px to 34px, `--pad-x` 11px to 12px, `--pad-y` 6px to 7px, `--pad-card` 16px to 20px, `--gap` 12px to 14px, `--row-h` 36px to 40px, `--leading` 1.45 to 1.5. Comfortable is unchanged. No token or class is renamed.
- **New site option: `coarse`**, values applied under `(pointer: coarse)`, so a site that sets its own control size keeps the 44px touch size.
- The site header's Foxhound specimens are amber.
- **Site only:** the showcase site serves `robots.txt` (allow all, pointing to the sitemap), `sitemap.xml` and `llms.txt` (what Capsomer is and where its documentation is), so search engines and AI assistants can find it. The package is unchanged.

## 0.17.0

**Foxing's asks** (apps/web on Capsomer v0.16.0, which kept its own copy of each).

- **New site: `foxing`** in `SITES`, exported as `capsomer/site-foxing.css` and in `capsomer/sites.css`, on `<html>` with `data-cap-site="foxing"` under the fox family: Foxing's cream and charcoal neutrals, DM Sans for the UI, DM Mono, Fraunces as its display face and the sky-blue on state of its Rule 05. Its `app/styles/site-foxing.css` can go. Rule 05's `#43a1d8` is 2.7:1 on the cream ground in the light theme, under the 3:1 a checked box needs, so the light theme takes Foxing's own hover blue `#2b87bc` (hover `#1f7fb8`); the dark theme keeps `#43a1d8` (hover `#5aafe0`). Foxing's to change.
- **New token: `--display`**, the face for a heading that wants a display face, in `tokens/scale.css` (group `type`): Schibsted Grotesk, the UI face, whose 800 weight the page head already uses, so nothing changes until a site sets its own. A site layer may set it. `.cap-page-head-title` now reads it.
- **New tokens: `--on` and `--on-hover`**, the fill of a checked checkbox or radio, an on switch and a chosen media tile's box, in every family (the primary fill and its hover, so nothing changes by default). A site layer may set them; the palette and site checks hold them to 3:1 against the surface, ground and raised surface, with the tick (`--primary-fg`) and the switch's thumb (`--surface`) at 3:1 on them. `field`, `switch` and `media` read them in place of `--primary` and `--primary-hover`.
- **Site header: the account button can show a picture.** `HeaderAccount` (and `AdminAccount`) gain `avatar`, an image address drawn over the initials; the initials show when there is none or it fails to load. New class `cap-site-header-avatar-image`, an `img` inside `.cap-site-header-initials`.
- **Command menu: live results.** The React `CommandMenu` takes `search(query, signal)` and `searchDelay` (180 ms): called after the last key, never for an empty field, the call before aborted; its commands follow the matching ones in their own groups. While it runs the list is `aria-busy` and an empty list says "Searching…" (new export `SEARCHING`). New specimen `live`. React only.
- **Releases ship the built package.** The release workflow now tags a release commit on top of the merged version bump: main's tree plus `dist/`, without the `prepare` script. An install of `github:DrDustinEdwards/capsomer#vX.Y.Z` from the next release on builds nothing, so it installs none of Capsomer's devDependencies (two are git dependencies, which npm 12 refuses without allow-git) and runs no tsc under the store. Apps can drop `NPM_CONFIG_ALLOW_GIT` and pnpm's build allowance for Capsomer once they pin it.
- Already done: the return types on `Pagination` and the skeleton exports shipped in 0.15.1.

**Fix: a visible label and count are words of the control's name** (axe-core 4.14, label-content-name-mismatch, WCAG 2.5.3; 4.14 counts `aria-hidden` text as visible). Changed HTML contracts, no visual change:

- A space now separates an entry's label from its count in the shell (`.cap-shell-label` and `.cap-shell-count`, the phone tab's `.cap-shell-badge`, the More sheet's count), the admin shell's menu (`.cap-admin-label` and `.cap-admin-count`, the sheet's app rows) and the site header's panels, sheet and bottom tabs (`.cap-site-header-count`, `.cap-site-header-badge`), so "Sites2" reads "Sites 2" inside "Sites, 2 down or degraded".
- Past 99 a count's name starts with what the bubble shows: "Inbox 99+, 120 need you" (was "Inbox, 120 need you"); `countName` changes accordingly.
- The business tile's letter and the avatar's initials are drawn from new attributes, `data-tile` on `.cap-site-header-switch-tile` and `data-initials` on `.cap-site-header-initials`, and those spans are now empty, so a decorative letter is not read as part of "Business: Acme Supply Co." or "Your account".
- The media inspector's copy buttons are named "Copy address" (was "Copy the address") and "Copy the HTML tag" (was "Copy the HTML image tag").

**Development: axe-core 4.14** (`@axe-core/playwright` ^4.14.0). Dev dependency only.

**Site only: the showcase site's `Reporting-Endpoints` header comes from site-runtime v0.12.0's `reportingEndpoints`** in place of a hand-written string (no package change). The header and the Content-Security-Policy are unchanged. site-runtime is a dev dependency at v0.12.0.

**Development: site-runtime v0.12.1** (loopback report endpoints, `nonceStyles` and `baseUriSelf`). Dev dependency only; no class, token or HTML contract changes.

## 0.16.0

**New: inside any system, in one tap** (Dustin, 2026-10-24: the Capsid explorer and dustinedwards.info should draw the map with the same code). dustinedwards.info drew every system's wiring with its own engine, beside Capsomer's map; that engine is now Capsomer's, and both sites use it. `capsomer/system-map-circles` exports `circleLayers(data, { summary?, saved? })`, which draws each system's circle at build time (its parts inside, what it works with outside and every system it connects to round the rim, searched for from fixed seeds so the same data draws the same way), with `sceneOf`, `layoutScene`, `layoutKey` and `faultsOf`. `saved` keeps the circles found, by system id under a key of what drew each (its boxes, its wires, its width and the search's own code), so a build searches again only for a circle that changed. Drawn from dustinedwards.info's model, all 23 circles are byte for byte the ones its own engine drew, the class names aside.

`systemMapHtml` takes them as the new option `circles: { layers?, src?, chosen? }`: the core's inside is its circle (flows play on it), one tap on any capsomer zooms into that system's circle, a system on the rim zooms there, and Back to all systems, Escape or a tap outside returns. `src` is every circle as one JSON object, which the script fetches once the page has loaded; `chosen` delivers that system zoomed into, its lines on. New: `modelMapData(model, href?)`, Capsid's public model as the map's data with each family's word and group, the same on every host; the `ModelSystem` and `SystemModel` types; the controller's `enter(id?)`; the event `cap:system-map-leave` (`{ id }`), sent whenever the map steps out of an inside; the root's `data-circles`, `data-insides`, `data-zoom` and `data-insides-ready`; an inside layer's `data-system` and `data-shown`; a wire's `data-loose` and `data-whole`; `button[data-cap-part="out"]`; and the classes `cap-system-map-rim`, `cap-system-map-rim-name`, `cap-system-map-inside-name` and `cap-system-map-inside-text`. Without `circles`, the map is as before. New option, exports, attributes and classes only; nothing is removed or renamed.

## 0.15.1

**Fix: a pnpm install of Capsomer builds wherever the store sits.** `Pagination`, `Spinner`, `Skeleton` and `SkeletonBlock` now state their return type (`ReactElement`, and `| null` for `Pagination`). Without it, the install-time build failed with TS2883 when the package was built under a `node_modules` path, which is where pnpm's store sits by default, so Foxing's CI had to move its store. No class, token or HTML contract changes.

## 0.15.0

**System map: groups** (Dustin, 2026-10-23: like goes with like, and a stranger should read the map without a key). A system people use can carry a `group` (Websites, Apps). When any does, each group is drawn together, its systems chained in the order given, apart from every other group, and named beside it in a new `text.cap-system-map-group-label` on a new `svg.cap-system-map-layer[data-layer="labels"]`, hidden inside a system. The gaps between groups are not drawn as empty shell, and the list below is sectioned by group. `Layout` gains `labels` and `grouped`. A map with no groups is drawn as before. New field, class and layer only; nothing is removed or renamed.

**Fix: system map tile names match what the tile shows** (axe-core 4.14, label-content-name-mismatch, WCAG 2.5.3). The tag line's `<text>` now starts with a space, so a tile's text reads "Capsomer shared" and not "Capsomershared"; SVG does not draw the space. A tile with a `short` label is named by that label, then its full name: "Site MCP, tool (Site connector)" where it was "Site connector, tool". No visual change.

**Development: a smaller browser suite.** Behavior, keyboard and geometry tests run in the light theme only (`grepInvert` in `playwright.config.ts`); accessibility tests still run in both, so the suite is about 1,810 runs instead of 2,610. Six tests that pinned the look rather than a promise are gone: the showcase's card alignment, menu row heights and color-view family list, the admin shell's collapsed icon rhythm and rail tint step, and the site header's 56px height and 24px logo inset. The canvas contrast ratio that five specs each wrote out is one in-page helper, `window.capRatio`, installed by `visitStates`. No token, class or HTML contract changes.

**Development: the family packages at their latest.** devkit and site-runtime v0.11.0 (and the devkit workflows at v0.11.0), and Enarratio 0.3.1 from npm in place of a 0.2.0-era git commit. Dev dependencies only; no class, token or HTML contract changes.

## 0.14.0

**New: per-site hover touches** (Dustin, 2026-10-23: each site keeps its own differences in Capsomer's components). Six new tokens in `tokens/scale.css`, group `touch`, each off by default so no site changes unless its `SITES` entry sets them: `--link-rule` (the site header's section links grow an underline in under the pointer; `0px`), `--icon-radius` (the header's icon controls and the theme button; `6px`, as `--radius-m` was), `--icon-border` (their outline's width; `0px`), `--theme-turn` (the theme button turns under the pointer; `0deg`), and `--arrow-rest` and `--arrow-shift` (`1` and `0px`) for the new `.cap-arrow` class: a trailing arrow icon in a link or button that a site can hide until the pointer or focus arrives, when it slides in. The `julieedwards` site turns them all on, as its old site had them: a 1px underline, round outlined icon buttons, a 20 degree turn and arrows that slide in. New tokens and class only; nothing is removed or renamed.

## 0.13.1

**New site: `julieedwards`** (julieedwards.info onto Capsomer, 2026-10-23), exported as `capsomer/site-julieedwards.css` and in `capsomer/sites.css`, on `<html>` with `data-cap-site="julieedwards"` under the forest family: `--ui` is Open Sans (reading) and `--font-ui` Montserrat (the header and the site's headings), both of which the site self-hosts.

**Site header: on a phone, the tools keep to the window's edge when the section links sit on the right.** With `data-links="right"` the hidden links' rule still set the tools' start margin to 12px, so the theme and menu buttons sat beside the logo instead of at the right. A visual fix at phone width only; the phone test now measures the menu button's edge. No class, token or HTML contract changes.

## 0.13.0

**New: the `forest` family** (Dustin, 2026-10-23: Julie's green must go with the other families). `data-family="forest"` switches an element's subtree to julieedwards.info's green, in both themes; `capsomer/families.css` now imports it, or take it alone as `capsomer/family-forest.css`. The Bluff Dale ISD green `#15532E` is the light theme's step 11 anchor (links, headings, accent text), as navy's `#002868` is. Step 9 is `#06954f`, its hue at the lightness purple, fox and navy share (oklch 0.587 0.15 153); green leaves sRGB above chroma 0.15 at that lightness, so it takes the strongest green there is. Light is its default theme. Every pair passes WCAG 2.2 in both themes, and the chart checks pass. `EmailFamily` gains `"forest"`; the site's overview counts five families. New attribute value and new exports only; nothing is removed or renamed, and no existing family changes.

## 0.12.0

**New: the `navy` family, and the `txasm` site** (the txasm-onto-Capsomer plan of 2026-10-23). `data-family="navy"` switches an element's subtree to txasm's navy, in both themes; `capsomer/families.css` now imports it, or take it alone as `capsomer/family-navy.css`. The brand navy `#002868` is too dark to be step 9 (as the focus ring it is under 3:1 on the dark surface), so it is the light theme's step 11 anchor (links, headings, accent text, as purple anchors `#4F2D7F`), and step 9 is `#3978e0`, the same hue at the lightness and strength purple and fox share (oklch 0.587 0.172 260; Dustin, 2026-10-23: the navy must go with the others). Light is its default theme. Every pair passes WCAG 2.2 in both themes, and the chart checks pass. The anchor is Dustin's to change. New site `txasm` in `SITES` (`capsomer/site-txasm.css`, `data-cap-site="txasm"` on `<html>`): the UI face stays Inter, which txasm loads. txasm's red `#a30e28` stays txasm's own, for its logo. `EmailFamily` gains `"navy"`; the site's overview counts four families. New attribute value and new exports only; no class, token or HTML contract is removed or renamed, and no existing family or site changes.

**Smoother motion, family-wide** (Dustin, 2026-10-23: "more modern/smoother feeling ui/ux"; the study is in the PR). A visual change only; every value is a token or sits in a component's CSS, so every app that takes the release changes together. Nothing new moves under reduced motion.

- **Tokens.** `--ease-out` is now `cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1)` (was `cubic-bezier(0, 0, 0.38, 0.9)`): a faster start and a longer soft stop, Radix Themes' and Arc's curve. `--ease-in-out` is now `cubic-bezier(0.65, 0, 0.35, 1)` (was `cubic-bezier(0.2, 0, 0.38, 0.9)`). New `--ease-spring`, a damped spring as `linear()` (95% at 30% of the time, 4% past), for a release or a thumb, with `--dur-slow`. Durations unchanged.
- **Exits are quicker than entries.** Dialog: rises 8 px and grows from 96% over 200 ms, leaves in 100 ms to 98% (was 95% both ways at 100 ms); sheets slide in over 200 ms and out in 100 ms over half the distance; the backdrop fades in 200 ms, out 100 ms. Popover and Base UI popups (select, combobox, menu): grow from 96% and slide 4 px over 200 ms, leave in 100 ms (was 95% and 2 px, 100 ms both ways). Tooltips stay 100 ms. The top palette (command menu) only fades in, and goes at once.
- **Press.** `.cap-btn` gives to 97% at once and springs back on release (was a 1 px nudge down); stat tiles give to 98.5%. Tiles lift on `--shadow-s` under a mouse.
- **Theme switch.** A person's choice crossfades the page in 200 ms through a view transition; a restored or synced choice paints at once. `effectiveTheme()` answers the new theme straight away.
- **Pages.** `base.css` opts same-origin page loads into a 200 ms crossfade (`@view-transition { navigation: auto; types: cap-page }`); in a transition typed `cap-page` the `main` element rises 6 px into place while the rest stays. A client-side router starts one with `document.startViewTransition({ update, types: ["cap-page"] })`, as the showcase site now does. An app that wants none writes `@view-transition { navigation: none; }`.
- **Messages** fade in out of a 4 px blur instead of a plain fade; still no slide. Links ease their color on hover.
- **Segmented control and tabs: the chosen highlight slides** and resizes to the new choice over 200 ms (uiarc). It is one mark, the tray's or list's `::before`, tethered to the chosen segment or tab by CSS anchor positioning, so it needs no script and the React wrappers and client-side routers get it as they are. Where `anchor-scope` is missing, under reduced motion and in forced colors the chosen item draws its own highlight, as before. The line variant's rule slides the same way.
- **Morphing button labels.** New behavior module `capsomer/behavior/button`: `morphLabel(button, change)` eases the width to the new label's and crossfades the words out of a 4 px blur (Save draft, Saving…, Saved); the leaving copy is `aria-hidden`. Opt in: the words in `.cap-btn-label`. React: `<Button morph>`.
- **Sheets drag away** (Vaul). A bottom, right or left sheet's header is its handle: it follows the pointer toward the edge while the backdrop thins, closes past a third of its size or on a flick (as Close does, `returnValue` `"drag"`, focus back to the opener) and springs back otherwise. A bottom sheet shows a grab bar once wired. Not on a locked or alert dialog, while busy, or under reduced motion. The sheet header takes `touch-action: none` and `user-select: none`.
- **Testing.** `paintedContrast` and `expectContrast` read a pseudo-element when the selector ends in `::before` or `::after`.

No class or HTML contract changes; one token added (`--ease-spring`), two token values changed. New: the `capsomer/behavior/button` module, the React `Button` `morph` prop, and the `data-leaving` and `data-cap-dragging` attributes these set while they run.

## 0.11.2

**Three more sites: `foxhound`, `germomics` and `capsid`** (the site-settings plan of 2026-10-23), each exported as `capsomer/site-<name>.css` and in `capsomer/sites.css`.

- `site-foxhound.css`, on each `.fh-cap` wrapper (never `<html>`, where Foxhound's unlayered `:root` would beat it, and never the admin): Foxhound's own neutrals (`--ground`, `--surface`, `--raised`, `--sunken`, `--text`, `--muted`, `--dim`, `--line`, `--line-strong`) in both themes, and DM Sans and DM Mono (`--ui`, `--font-ui`, `--mono`), under the teal family's accents. The header band stays in Foxhound, because the admin shares it and `.cap-site-header` sets its own band tokens.
- `site-germomics.css`, on the admin's `[data-cap-theme]` element: `--mono` is the site's JetBrains Mono.
- `site-capsid.css`, on the Portal's `<html>`: `--fs-label` and `--fs-detail` are 13px on a phone (the Portal UX audit's m4), under the Portal's own 820px breakpoint, since moving it to Capsomer's 768px would shrink the labels between 769px and 820px.

**`tokens/sites.mjs`** gains three fields. `defaultTheme: "dark"` writes a site's dark values as the plain block and its light values only where light is pinned (`:root[data-theme="light"]`, `[data-cap-theme="light"]`), as Foxhound draws its own pages; the dark values are also written under the family's dark selectors, so they tie them. `phone` holds values for a phone, under Capsomer's 767.98px or the site's own `phoneMax`. `waive` records a WCAG pair a site's own colors already fail where it renders today, with the reason; the check refuses a waiver that no longer matches a failure. Foxhound's dark `--line-strong` (`#4a4c44`, legacy's value) is the one waiver: 2.02:1 on `--surface`, under the 3:1 a control border needs. No class, token or other HTML contract changes.

## 0.11.1

**Per-site settings win over `tokens.css` whatever order they load in.** A bundler can put the site file before `tokens.css` (dustinedwards-info's Vite build did), and with equal specificity the later `:root` defaults won, so the site's chart sizes did not apply. Each site selector now repeats its attribute (`[data-cap-site="x"][data-cap-site]`). No class, token or HTML contract changes.

## 0.11.0

**New: per-site settings** (`data-cap-site="<site>"`; the site-settings plan of 2026-10-23). `SITES` in `tokens/sites.mjs` holds what a site differs on from the family defaults (faces, neutrals, type sizes, space and radius, `--enarratio-*`), as literal values; a brand color stays a family. It generates `tokens/site-<name>.css` in the `cap.tokens` layer and the barrel `css/sites.css`, exported as `capsomer/sites.css` and `capsomer/site-<name>.css`; import after `tokens.css` and `families.css`. The selectors repeat the family files' shapes (plain, system dark, pinned dark), so dark theme and density cannot override a site value, and each also matches a `[data-family]` or `[data-density]` wrapper inside the site. `npm run tokens` writes the files; `npm run check` refuses a hand edit, a missing export, a `var()` value or a brand token, and checks the site's colors against WCAG 2.2 in both themes. The first site is `dustinedwards`, on `<html>`: its charts keep their look from before Enarratio 0.3 (title `1rem`, small text `0.875em`, and Enarratio's own spacing, shape and timing literals). New attribute `data-cap-site`; no class, token or other HTML contract changes.

## 0.10.1

**`capsomer/email`: `checkUrl` refuses a link with a user or password before the host** (`https://trusted.example@evil.example/` reads as the trusted name in a mail client but opens the other host). Every link the email renderers take goes through it, so a caller passing such a URL now gets a thrown `email:` error. No class, token or HTML contract changes.

**Tooling:** a `LICENSE` file for the MIT license `package.json` names; vite 8.3.5 and wrangler 4.151.3 (dev dependencies, off advised ranges); CI fails on a high advisory in the production install.

## 0.10.0

**New: `capsomer/publish-transition`**, the publish gate's DOM-free half, so carrel and dustinedwards-info keep one copy of the post editor's transitions in place of their own `publish-transition.mjs`. `transitionsFor(state, everPublished)`, `draftForIntent(intent)` (fails closed: an unknown or missing intent is a draft), `saveInPlaceIntent(state)`, `stateOf({ draft, publishAt }, now)` (an unparseable `publish_at` is published, not scheduled), `DRAFT_BY_INTENT` (no prototype: the apps' copies answered `draftForIntent("constructor")` with `Object`, not a draft), `PUBLISH_CONFIRMED_INTENT` and `FIRST_PUBLICATION_NOTE`, with the types `PostState` and `Transition`. Pure, no DOM, safe in a server action. `capsomer/behavior/publish-gate` still exports `FIRST_PUBLICATION_NOTE`, now from this module, and its confirmed button posts `PUBLISH_CONFIRMED_INTENT`. No class, token or HTML contract changes.

**Tooling: site-runtime v0.7.0 and devkit v0.6.0** (dev dependencies; no package change). The `ai-author` and `no-bloat` workflows pin the commit devkit v0.6.0 points at. Knip's unused exports in `bin/states.mjs` and `site/data.ts` are no longer exported, and the site's Worker deploys minified with source maps.

**Site only: the showcase site's security headers come from `@dustinedwards/site-runtime` v0.4.0** (no package change). `scripts/security-headers.ts` holds the site's list (`data:` images, test results from raw.githubusercontent.com, the Cloudflare Web Analytics beacon, styles from its own files only, its own pages framed with `frame-ancestors 'self'` and `X-Frame-Options: sameorigin`, the clipboard's write side for the Copy specimens) and the site build writes `site-dist/_headers` from it, in place of the hand-written `site/public/_headers`. The site now sends the shared standard set (HSTS, COOP, CORP, `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer`, the full Permissions-Policy) and `form-action 'self'`. Violations are reported to `/csp-report`, answered by a minimal Worker (`worker/index.ts`) that runs for that path only and logs to Workers Logs. `test/unit/security-headers.test.mjs` runs site-runtime's check on the generated `_headers` and on the report response.

## 0.9.1

**Site header and AdminShell: nothing runs past a 320px window** (WCAG 1.4.10, reflow; the Capsid Portal UX audit's M8, 2026-10-20; a visual change at narrow widths only). At 320px every AdminShell page was 335px wide and the menu button was clipped, because the logo never gave way. Now the logo (`.cap-site-header-logo`) shrinks before the controls do, and under 360px a logo with a mark shows the mark alone; the link's label keeps its name for screen readers. Under 360px the phone tab bar's count badges sit flush on the glyph's edge with 2px padding, so the last tab's "99+" stays clear of its glyph and inside the window. At 360px and wider nothing changes. Tested at 320px in both themes. No class, token or HTML contract changes.

## 0.9.0

**New: Site header** (`header.cap-site-header`, `data-cap="site-header"`; `capsomer/react/site-header`, `capsomer/behavior/site-header`; docs/design/design-site-header.md and Dustin's ten answers of 2026-10-13; a visual change that waits for Dustin's sign-off). One header across the top of every public, app and admin page in the family. Capsomer fixes the frame: 56px at every width (new token `--header-h`, 3.5rem), sticky, with `scroll-padding-top` so a focused control never lands under it, full width with the logo 24px from the window's edge (16px on a phone). The order is fixed: the logo, the switcher, the section links, then the site's extras, the theme button, and the account (the avatar menu, or Sign in) last. The section links sit where the site says: `data-links="center"`, or `"right"` before the tools after a thin divider. Each site fills its slots: the logo, its color family, the band (`--header-bg`, `--header-fg`, `--header-fg-muted`, `--header-line`, or `data-band="dark"` for a band dark in both themes, and `data-translucent`), up to two icon extras and one button, and what each panel holds. The switcher, a section with children and the account open panels: disclosure buttons (`aria-expanded`, `aria-controls`) over lists of links, not ARIA menus. A panel opens at once on hover and on click; Enter or Space opens it and focuses its first item; Up, Down, Home and End move; Esc closes it and returns focus to its button; Tab past the last item closes it. On a phone (under 768px) the header is one row: the logo, up to two of the site's icons, the theme, the avatar or Sign in, and the menu button last. The menu button opens one bottom sheet (the shared dialog) with everything else, the account rows pinned under the list so Sign out is always in view, focus on its heading; there is no second row of links. `SiteTabs` (`nav.cap-site-tabs`) is the bottom bar of a signed-in area on a phone: up to five tabs and no More. Specimens for a teal site with a dark band (Foxhound's look) and a fox site with a translucent band (Foxing's), public, signed in, a dashboard and your admin, in both themes, at desktop and phone, and a header written by hand. New HTML contract: `header.cap-site-header[data-links][data-band][data-translucent][data-sticky]` holding `a.cap-site-header-skip`, `a.cap-site-header-logo`, `div.cap-site-header-switcher` (`button.cap-site-header-switch` with `.cap-site-header-switch-tile` and `svg.cap-site-header-chev`, `span.cap-site-header-sep`), `nav.cap-site-header-sections` of `.cap-site-header-link` (`aria-current`, or `data-current` on a section button), `div.cap-site-header-tools` (`div.cap-site-header-extras` of `.cap-site-header-icon` with `.cap-site-header-badge` and `.cap-site-header-tip`, and `a.cap-site-header-cta`; the theme button; `div.cap-site-header-account` with `button.cap-site-header-avatar` and `.cap-site-header-initials`, or `a.cap-site-header-signin` with `.cap-site-header-signin-text`; `button.cap-site-header-menu`), each panel `div.cap-site-header-panel[popover="manual"][role="group"][data-align]` (`.cap-site-header-who`, `.cap-site-header-panel-label`, `ul.cap-site-header-panel-list`, `.cap-site-header-mark`, `.cap-site-header-count`, `[data-panel-close]`), and the sheet `dialog.cap-dialog.cap-site-header-sheet[data-placement="bottom"]` (`.cap-site-header-sheet-title`, `.cap-site-header-sheet-label`, `ul.cap-site-header-sheet-list`, `.cap-site-header-sheet-icon`, `.cap-site-header-sheet-name`, `a.cap-site-header-sheet-cta`, `nav.cap-site-header-sheet-account`). Behavior exports: `enhance`, `wirePanel`, `placePanel`, `closePanels`, `panelItems`, `openSheet`, `closeSheet`, `stepIndex`, `badgeText`, `HOVER_GRACE_MS`, `PHONE_QUERY`. React exports: `SiteHeader`, `SiteTabs`, `SheetGroup`, `HeaderTip`, `countName` and the types `HeaderLink`, `HeaderGroup`, `HeaderLogo`, `HeaderSwitcher`, `HeaderSection`, `HeaderExtra`, `HeaderAccount`, `HeaderAccountLink`, `SheetPart`, `SiteHeaderProps`, `LinkProps`. Tested: order, the 56px height, sticky, the logo's x, link placement, every panel's keys, the theme button, the phone row, the sheet, the tabs, and contrast on both bands in both themes.

**Theme switch: the button form** (Dustin's answer 2, 2026-10-13). New `button.cap-theme-button[data-cap="theme-switch"][data-variant="button"]`: one icon button, a moon while the page is light and a sun while it is dark (`svg.cap-theme-icon[data-show="light|dark"]`), named for what it does ("Switch to dark theme", "Switch to light theme"), Light and Dark only, with its tooltip `span.cap-theme-tip` (`.cap-theme-tip-text`, optional `kbd`). The same remembering, `t` key and system following as the switch. New exports `themeButtonLabel`, `syncThemeButton` and, in React, `ThemeButton`. The switch stays for a settings page.

**AdminShell sits under the site header, in two variants** (the same answers; a visual change that waits for Dustin's sign-off). The shell now draws the site header across the window, and under it:

- **`variant="owner"`, your admin** (the default): a 56px strip of the family's app tiles and their badges only, then the 232px menu. Search and the inbox move to the header's right; Help moves into the avatar's menu; there is no switcher in the header.
- **`variant="user"`, a customer's dashboard or a reader's area**: no strip and a 288px menu (`--admin-menu-open` is `--rail-w` + `--space-6` + `--rail-w-collapsed`), with the business and product switchers in the header. The content starts at the same x in both.
- **The theme is the header's icon button.** AdminShell's top bar no longer holds the Light and Dark switch (this replaces the 0.8.0 ruling from PR #13 for headers); the bar ends with Refresh.
- **The collapse control is the menu's first row** ("Collapse menu", its key `[` as a key cap), in the same place in both variants and both states; collapsed, its words hide and its tooltip names it. It is no longer in the strip.
- **The menu has no title**; the header's logo names the app.
- **The account menu is the header's**: who you are, this app's rows (under `appLabel` or "This app"), View site and Help and shortcuts, Sign out last. The Elsewhere group is gone; the strip lists the other apps.
- **On a phone** (now under 768px, was 820px) the header's row and its sheet replace the shell's own bar and sheet: the sheet lists the apps (your admin) or the switchers (a dashboard), then every page grouped; the bottom bar (`SiteTabs`) holds up to five pages and no More tab.
- **Foxing joins `FAMILY_APPS`**, seventh, before Capsomer, with a placeholder fox mark until its own is drawn; `FamilyAppId` gains `"foxing"`.
- The 0.8.0 guarantees hold and stay tested: no icon moves when the menu collapses, every name tip is in the top layer, one bubble for every badge and count.

Changed React contract: new `variant`, `logo`, `band`, `translucent`, `switchers`, `extras`, `viewSite`, `themeKey`, `account.appLabel`; `tabs` now defaults to the first five of `nav`; **removed `more`** (and the More tab); `onSearch`, `inbox` and `onHelp` now draw into the header and the avatar's menu, not the strip. `HeaderExtra`, `HeaderLogo` and `HeaderSwitcher` are exported from `capsomer/react/admin-shell`. Changed behavior: **`openSheet` and `closeSheet` are removed from `capsomer/behavior/admin-shell`** (the sheet is the header's: `capsomer/behavior/site-header`); `badgeText` is re-exported from site-header; `enhance` also enhances the header. Changed HTML contract: `div.cap-admin[data-variant="owner|user"]` now starts with `header.cap-site-header` (spanning every column) and ends with `nav.cap-site-tabs`; the strip is `nav.cap-admin-strip[aria-label="Apps"]` (was "Apps and tools") holding one list of `a.cap-admin-tile` and no buttons, and is absent in the user variant; the menu starts with `button.cap-admin-collapse[data-cap-part="menu-toggle"]` (an icon, `.cap-admin-label`, `.cap-keys kbd` and its tip); `.cap-admin-actions` no longer holds the theme switch's `fieldset`; with transitions on the header is named `cap-admin-header`. **Removed classes**: `.cap-admin-skip` (now `.cap-site-header-skip`), `.cap-admin-title`, `.cap-admin-title-mark`, `.cap-admin-title-text`, `.cap-admin-avatar`, `.cap-admin-account`, `.cap-admin-who`, `.cap-admin-account-section`, `.cap-admin-app-mark`, `.cap-admin-bar-app`, `.cap-admin-tabs` (now `.cap-site-tabs`), `.cap-admin-sheet`, `.cap-admin-sheet-title`, `.cap-admin-sheet-label`, `.cap-admin-sheet-list`, `.cap-admin-sheet-name`, `.cap-admin-sheet-icon` and `.cap-admin-sheet-account` (now the `.cap-site-header-sheet-*` classes); the ids `cap-admin-sheet` and `cap-admin-sheet-title` go with them. New class: `.cap-admin-collapse`. New token: `--header-h`. No token is removed or renamed. An app that styles or queries a removed class, passes `more`, or places its own theme control in `actions` changes with this release.

**Tests: contrast reads color-mix() colors.** `paintedContrast` (test/helpers.ts, `capsomer/testing`) reads a color computed as `color(srgb …)`, which is what `color-mix()` gives; it read such a color as transparent before.

## 0.8.1

**System map: parts outside the core, and flows** (capsid job_b6494c838e31, slice 6 of capsid/research/design-system-explorer.md; Dustin, 2026-10-11: Agents and GitHub as external boxes outside the dashed boundary; a visual change that waits for Dustin's sign-off). A container of kind `external` is no longer drawn inside the core: it sits outside the circle, in the stage's top corners two to a band and the bottom corners for a third and fourth (four at most), on the page's ground with a plain edge, and the inside detail counts it apart ("6 parts inside, 2 outside it."). Data gains `flows`: ordered steps, each riding a line between the core's parts toward one end, with a sentence and the parts it lights. Inside the core, Play starts a flow: the step's line is lit, a token travels it from just off one box to just off the other, the part it moves toward has a heavy edge and the parts it touches a tint and a heavy edge; the detail line reads "Step n of N." and the sentence with Back, Next and Stop; the left and right arrows and the number keys step (with no modifier, so the browser keeps its shortcuts), Escape stops. Under reduced motion the token jumps. A host's `playFlow` and `step` leave focus where it is, and every way a flow ends (Stop, Escape, stepping out, choosing, the plain view, `destroy`) sends `cap:system-map-flow` with `{ id: null, step: 0 }`. The plain view opens the steps with the systems' list. Only four parts fit outside; a flow needing a part not drawn is not offered. **Inside the core, redrawn** (seat rulings on this PR; a line under a box, or a bend or crossing beside another line, reads as a false connection, Dunne and Shneiderman 2009). The boundary stays a dashed circle, now as large as the stage allows (it reaches the drawing's margin or 16 units from a part outside), named at its foot; the parts sit in rows along its chords, and the drawing is made larger when they would not fit, so no box is cut (Dustin, 2026-10-13: keep the circle, a little bigger, and the hexagons behind it). The rim stays behind it as faded shapes with no names. Every box sits inside or outside the circle with clear space. Lines leave a box at their own ports, spread along the side facing their far ends; no line runs under a box it does not end at or under the boundary's name, each keeps 8 units (over 5 px on a 320 px phone) from every other box, crosses no line near its ends nor anywhere along a short one, runs nearly parallel to none within 10 units, and bends nowhere within 12 units of another line. Capsid's own core keeps every rule; on 100 seeded random cores, far more crowded, no line crosses a box or the name and each softer rule holds for at least 19 lines in 20. A flow's token follows the line's bends. The panel's headings use the page's face (`--face`; they were `--prose`, a serif the page did not load), the fixed detail box is tighter (7.5rem, 12rem at 40rem and narrower; was 8.5rem and 13rem), and in forced colors every capsomer and part is Canvas with a CanvasText edge (stores kept their tint before) and the lines CanvasText. A flow that names a line the data lacks, goes toward neither end, or lights a part outside the core is not offered. Each flow is also an ordered list of its steps, open with no script. Changed HTML contract, additive except the wire's element (`circle.cap-system-map-boundary` is unchanged; `.cap-system-map-inside-title` now sits at the circle's foot): `g.cap-system-map-box` gains `data-id`, `data-x`, `data-y` and `data-external` (and `data-lit`, `data-at` while a flow plays); `.cap-system-map-wire` is now a `path` (was a `line`) with `data-from`, `data-to` and `data-route`, its corners (and `data-on`); new `circle.cap-system-map-token`, `details.cap-system-map-flows` of `section.cap-system-map-flow[data-flow]` (`.cap-system-map-flow-name`, `.cap-system-map-flow-text`, `ol > li[data-from][data-to][data-toward][data-lights]`); `data-flow` and `data-step` on the root; event `cap:system-map-flow`; controller `playFlow`, `step`, `stopFlow`. New pure functions `insideScene` (the circle, its name, the boxes and every routed line), `rectInset`, `rectOutset`, `portsOf`, `playableFlows`, `stepText`, `segmentHitsBox`, `segmentHitsRect`, `segmentFaults`, `segmentCross`, `segDist`, `segAngle`, `sideBySide`, `pointSegDist`, `tokenRoute`, `routeFrames`, and the constants `ROUTE_CLEAR`, `LINE_SEP`, `JUNCTION_END`, `SHORT_LINK` and `BEND_CLEAR`. **Removed: the function `insideLayout`**, replaced by `insideScene`. No class or token is removed or renamed.

## 0.8.0

**AdminShell: one shell on every admin page** (capsid job_5ebb352157e5, Dustin's review of the six admin pages, 2026-10-11; a visual change that waits for Dustin's sign-off). Every family admin app (Capsid Portal, Carrel, dustinedwards.info, germomics, Foxhound) and Capsomer's own site now get the same strip, menu and top bar, built once here.

- **The strip is configured once, in Capsomer.** New `FAMILY_APPS` (`components/admin-shell/apps.tsx`, exported from `capsomer/react/admin-shell`) lists the five admin apps and Capsomer in one order with each product's own mark, redrawn in `currentColor` from its favicon or brand file (the Portal's hexagon, Carrel's book, dustinedwards.info's microscope, germomics' microphone, Capsomer's hexagon), never a letter. Foxhound keeps the x from its favicon, which reads as close or delete, until Dustin picks; `FOXHOUND_MARKS` offers two alternatives (a paw print, a fox's head) and none is shipped. An app passes `app` (`"portal"`, `"carrel"`, `"info"`, `"germomics"`, `"foxhound"`, `"capsomer"`) and other apps' badges in `badges`; the shell marks it and takes the menu's title from it.
- **One menu header: the app's name only**, in one style. The logo is the marked tile in the strip, so the current app's mark is never shown twice (Dustin's answer, 2026-10-11). Collapsed, the column shows the app's mark at its top, so it keeps the app's identity.
- **Nothing moves when the menu opens or closes.** Every menu icon keeps its x and y, and the current row its left edge and height, open and collapsed: one row inset (`--admin-row-pad`) both ways puts each icon in the middle of the collapsed column. A browser test measures every icon, logo and the current row in both states.
- **Collapsed, the groups keep one even rhythm.** A group heading is a short band open and the same band holding a thin divider collapsed, so there are no holes where headings were.
- **One tooltip everywhere, above everything.** Every shown name (a strip control's, open or collapsed; a collapsed menu icon's; Refresh's) is Capsomer's tooltip, the shared popover's tooltip variant, in the top layer: above every panel, tile and badge, and fully opaque. A strip tooltip starts past the strip's edge, so it covers neither its control, its badge nor the controls above and below. Keys are key caps (`G` `2`). Tests check every corner of every name, its opacity, one look for all of them, and that it covers none of its neighbors.
- **Tooltip: its own surface** (`components/popover`, the tooltip variant, so every tooltip in Capsomer): a raised surface with an edge in `--line-strong` and a shadow, `--text` on it, instead of the inverted `--text` fill, which looked like the selected menu item (they measured 1.00:1 in light). Key caps have their own edge. A test measures the tooltip against the selected item (3:1 or more) and its edge against the page.
- **The strip and the menu are two rails.** The strip is a visible step apart from the menu in both themes (`--admin-strip-bg`).
- **One bubble for every count and badge**, one size, shape and type, in the strip, the menu and the sheet, open and collapsed, and the same number in each: `99+` past 99 (collapsed showed a dot for 120). A plain count is a gray bubble (`--muted`) at 3:1 against the menu (it was a bare gray number); violet needs you; red and amber are status colors. On an icon every bubble sits in one place relative to its glyph, 2px past its right edge and centered on its top edge, so neither it nor its ring covers the glyph. Collapsed, the menu is 80px (`--admin-menu-collapsed`), so a count never crosses into the content. One rule for unread markers: always a number; `dot` is removed (a state with nothing to count is 1).
- **Hover is visible in both themes**: a fill a clear step off the menu (`--admin-hover`) and an edge at 3:1 (`--admin-hover-edge`); in dark the old `--sunken` fill was all but the menu's own color.
- **One top bar on every app.** Left: the trail, only below the top level (`trail`), so the bar never repeats the page's heading; then one freshness line on every site, "Updated 6 minutes ago" (`updated: { at, auto }`, "· refreshes itself" when `auto`, the time from the Time component); then `status`. Right: the page's own controls (`actions`), Refresh where the page refreshes (`onRefresh`, `refreshing`, `refreshShortcut`), then the theme switch, always last. The theme control is the Light and Dark switch, not an icon button: it names both choices in words, and it is the control Dustin ruled for (PR #13). `themePrefKey` passes the switch's storage key.
- **The tab title is "<page> · <app>"**, set by the shell.
- **One avatar:** the account defaults to the owner, "DE" for Dustin Edwards (new `OWNER`); `account.name` and `account.initials` are now optional.
- **The first page is "Overview"** (new `FIRST_PAGE`) in every app: it names what the page is and holds whether or not anything needs you. Apps rename Needs you, Home and Dashboard to it.
- **Content width:** the page fills the space to the menu's right from the same left edge, open and collapsed; an app drops its own centered column.

Changed React contract: `mark` is removed (the header has no logo); `dot` is removed from apps and badges; `title` and `apps` are optional and only for a site outside the family; new `app`, `badges`, `page`, `trail`, `updated`, `onRefresh`, `refreshing`, `refreshShortcut`, `themePrefKey`, and exports `FAMILY_APPS`, `FOXHOUND_MARKS`, `OWNER`, `FIRST_PAGE`, `AdminBadge`, `AdminCrumb`, `FamilyAppId`; `badgeText` from `capsomer/behavior/admin-shell`; `hideShownNames` and `showNamesAgain` are removed (the tooltip hides itself on Esc). An app passing `mark`, its own `apps`, a theme control in `actions`, or `initials` changes with this release. Changed HTML contract: the menu title is `div.cap-admin-title` holding `span.cap-admin-title-mark` (shown collapsed) and `span.cap-admin-title-text`; a tile's mark is `svg.cap-admin-logo`; every name tip is `span.cap-admin-tip.cap-popover[data-variant="tooltip"][popover="manual"][role="tooltip"]` with `data-side` and `data-offset`, holding `.cap-admin-tip-text` and `.cap-keys` of `kbd`, a child of its control, and every menu link has one; the top bar is always drawn (`data-empty` on `.cap-admin-bar` is gone) and holds `nav.cap-admin-trail`, `span.cap-admin-fresh`, `div.cap-admin-bar-status`, `button.cap-admin-btn[data-cap-part="refresh"]` and the theme switch's `fieldset` in `.cap-admin-actions`. Removed: `data-dot` and `data-wide` on bubbles, `data-tip="hidden"`. A badge's placement moves from `right` to `left` (beside the glyph). No class or token is removed or renamed.

**Theme switch: one marker.** The chosen choice is the selected-item fill (`--accent-12`, its word in `--ground`), over 3:1 against the page, and the accent bar under it is gone. Tested.

**Status: no data is an empty circle with a bar**, not a dashed ring, which read as a spinner that had stopped. Changed in every component that draws it (status, stat tile, session row, feed, flag list, moderation queue, media and their examples); the history list's queued mark is a plain empty circle. Changed HTML contract: the no-data glyph is `<circle cx="8" cy="8" r="6.2" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6"/><path d="M5 8h6" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-linecap="round"/>`.

**Row list: one layout at every width, a flush critical edge, visible sizes.** In a narrow column an icon or a picture stays beside the title, and the rows stay a subgrid of the list, so every row's text starts in one column with or without media (row 1's title no longer starts where the others' icons do). The critical edge is an inset shadow, flush with the row's edge and clipped to its rounded corners (it was inset and square); in forced colors it is a border. `data-size="sm"` is tighter with a 28px picture beside the text (it was the tallest row, picture stacked above), and `"xs"` tighter again with its title a size smaller. The demo's captions are prose, not `data-size` attributes. Tested.

**Row list: an icon stays beside its title in a narrow column** (from the same review: Carrel's cards put their icons above the titles when the menu was open and beside them when it was collapsed). Under 30rem the `cap-rows` container query moved every `.cap-row-media` above the title; a row whose media is `data-variant="icon"` now keeps it in the first column beside the title and detail, so a list has one layout at every width. A picture (`data-variant="image"`) and a status still move above. CSS only; no class, token or HTML contract changes. A browser test compares the icon and title placement wide and narrow.

**Capsomer's site uses AdminShell**, so the reference matches what the apps get: the family's app strip with the collapse control at its top, the menu in two groups with the dark selected pill, the top bar with the page, test freshness and the theme switch, and `[` to collapse. The usage example shows `AdminShell`. The site marks Capsomer as the app you are in. The Overview's cards lay out from the top, so labels and numbers share a line across the row, open and collapsed; the contrast card puts its two facts on two lines ("3 color families in 2 themes", then "None failing"), and no card line wraps; the Tests card says its number once ("All passed"). A component's page shows its trail in the top bar, and the page's own breadcrumb goes. American spelling in the site's own words: "Color and contrast" (now `#/color`; `#/color` still opens it), "The color families", "Behavior".

## 0.7.1

**Fixed: the system map's detail line is a fixed box** (capsid job_5c81a4bd1baf, found by dustinedwards-info's browser checks). Choosing a system grew the detail line (88 px to 119 px on desktop, up to 191 px on a phone), so the page below moved. `.cap-system-map-detail` now has a fixed block size (8.5rem, 13rem at 40rem and narrower) and scrolls inside it when a detail is longer; the size is `!important` inside the layer, so a host's ordinary unlayered CSS cannot undo it. A browser test measures the line and the list below it through choosing, going inside and stepping out, at desktop and phone widths, with a host override injected. CSS only: `min-height: 5.5rem` is replaced by the fixed size, and no class, token or HTML contract changes. A visual change that waits for Dustin's sign-off.

## 0.7.0

**New: `capsomer/testing`, the axe and contrast helpers** (capsid job_5e26713fd175, step 3 of capsid/research/design-shared-tests.md). `test/helpers.ts` is now a public entry: `capsomer/testing` exports `eachTheme`, `visitStates`, `expectNoAxeViolations` (WCAG 2.2 A and AA, with the Base UI focus-guard and iframe exclusion), `paintedContrast` (text, border or outline as painted, SVG text by its fill over its shape), `expectContrast`, `focused`, `nextPost` and `THEMES`, built to `dist/test/helpers.js` with types, so an app stops copying them. `@playwright/test` and `@axe-core/playwright` are optional peer dependencies, needed only by an app that imports it. The helpers are now tested on their own (`test/helpers.spec.ts` in the browser suite, `test/unit/testing-export.test.mjs` for the export). No token or class is removed or renamed and no HTML contract changes.

**Tooling: devkit v0.5.0's shared workflows.** `.github/workflows/no-bloat.yml` calls devkit's `no-bloat` report (Knip and jscpd against the new `.no-bloat.json`, warn-only as before) and keeps capsomer's page weight and line counts as a job of its own; the new `.github/workflows/ai-author.yml` calls devkit's gate that refuses an AI attribution trailer or author on new commits. Both pin the commit v0.5.0 points at, and `@dustinedwards/devkit` is a dev dependency at that tag. Not shipped in the package.

**New: System map** (`figure.cap-system-map`, `data-cap="system-map"`; `capsomer/behavior/system-map`; Capsid job_b6494c838e31, slice 3 of capsid/research/design-system-explorer.md, answer 9 of capsid/rulings/system-explorer-2026-10-10.md; a visual change that waits for Dustin's sign-off). A family of systems drawn as a capsid, the systems as its capsomers: the core in the middle, shared parts ringing it, what people use on the outside, in flat-top hexagons whose geometry is computed in `systemMapHtml`, so a server or a static build delivers the whole contract. At rest there are no lines; choosing a capsomer draws its connections (solid where it installs or publishes, dashed where it is coordinated or watched) along the seams between capsomers and beneath them, so no line crosses a label, outlines each system at the far end, and fills the detail line; choosing the core again goes inside it, folding the inner rings into the middle and fading the rim to shapes only while the core's containers appear within a dashed boundary. The same data is always a list grouped by ring: open with no script, behind "The same map as a list" with it, and shown as columns in the plain view. One tab stop with the arrow keys moving to the nearest capsomer that way, Home and End, Enter or Space to choose and to go inside, Escape to step out or clear. The core, the inner rings and the rim are separate drawings over one viewBox, so going inside moves whole layers by transform and opacity; nothing moves under reduced motion. Private is a dashed edge and the word; chosen is a heavier edge and a tint. New HTML contract: `figure.cap-system-map[data-view]` holding `figcaption.cap-system-map-head` (`.cap-system-map-title`, `.cap-system-map-caption`, `.cap-system-map-views`), `div.cap-system-map-stage` of `svg.cap-system-map-layer[data-layer="edges|core|inner|rim|inside"]` with `g.cap-system-map-tile[data-id][data-x][data-y][data-ring]` (`data-core`, `data-shared`, `data-private`, `data-empty`, `data-linked`) and `path.cap-system-map-edge[data-from][data-to][data-kind]` (`data-loose`, `data-on`), `div.cap-system-map-detail`, and `details.cap-system-map-list` of `section.cap-system-map-group`; `data-selected` and `data-entered` on the root; events `cap:system-map-select` and `cap:system-map-enter`. Pure functions `systemMapHtml`, `layoutMap`, `ringCells`, `seamRoute`, `spatialNext`, `linksOf`, `insideLayout`, `connectionText`. No React wrapper yet: a React page renders `systemMapHtml` and calls `enhance`. No class or token is removed or renamed.

**Tests: contrast reads SVG text as painted.** `paintedContrast` (test/helpers.ts) reads an SVG `text` element's `fill`, over the fill of the shape drawn before it in its group, instead of its CSS `color` over the page; it read the wrong colors for SVG text before.

**New: People and roles** (`section.cap-people`, `data-cap="people-roles"`; `capsomer/react/people-roles`, `capsomer/behavior/people-roles`; capsid job_d0fabaadecb1, Dustin's people-and-roles rulings of 2026-10-09 and capsid docs/design/design-identity-roles.md; a visual change that waits for Dustin's sign-off). The one members page every app with staff uses (the lab first, then txasm, Foxing and Foxing Edu): each person's title, when their access ends, and whether Cloudflare's allow list matches yet ("Ready: they can sign in", "Waiting for Cloudflare" with Retry, "Cloudflare refused" with Cloudflare's reason and Retry). One action inline per row (Retry, else Renew to the date the server worked out) and the rest in the shared form menu (Change title, History, Remove). Remove asks first in the confirm dialog, saying what follows, and posts `confirmed=yes`; with no script the server answers with the confirm page. Change title opens as its own row by the address (`?change=`) and needs a one-line reason. The term review is a Keep or Remove checklist with a live tally; anyone not answered loses access on the end date. Within three weeks of the end date a row says so in bold beside the warning shape. Removed and expired people stay in the list, quieter. The viewer's own row is marked You and offers only its history. The add form's end date follows the chosen title and cannot pass its default; a title that does not expire has no end date. Every action is a form post with an `intent` (`add`, `change_title`, `renew`, `remove`, `retry`, `review`), so the page works with no script; React's `onIntent` runs them in place. The rules stay on the server, in site-runtime's `./members` (v0.3.0). New HTML contract: `section.cap-people` holding `.cap-people-head` (`.cap-people-count`), `.cap-people-lead`, `p.cap-people-result[data-tone]`, `form.cap-people-card.cap-people-review[data-variant="review"]` (`fieldset.cap-people-review-item`, `.cap-people-review-who`, `.cap-people-review-foot`, `.cap-people-tally[data-cap-part="tally"]`), the table `table.cap-people-table` whose rows are `tr[data-status][data-email][data-name]` with `th.cap-people-who` (`.cap-people-name`, `.cap-people-you`, `.cap-people-email`), `.cap-people-end[data-soon]`, `td.cap-people-sync` (`.cap-people-sync-error`) and `td.cap-people-actions` (`.cap-people-acts`), the change row `tr.cap-people-edit` with `form.cap-people-change` (`.cap-people-change-actions`), `section.cap-people-history` (`.cap-people-history-head`, `ol.cap-people-history-list` of `.cap-people-history-item` with `.cap-people-history-when`, `.cap-people-history-what`, `.cap-people-history-by`, `.cap-people-history-reason`), and `form.cap-people-card.cap-people-add` (`.cap-people-add-fields`, `.cap-people-add-foot`, `[data-cap-part="end-help"]`). Pure functions: `rowActions`, `accessText`, `endsSoon`, `daysLeft`, `syncText`, `actionText`, `cloudflareText`, `endHelp`, `tallyText`, `removeBody`, `removeTarget`, `dayText`, `timeText`; `confirmRemove`, `followTitle`, `updateTally`. No class or token is removed or renamed. Swap: the lab's `/lab/members` first (its own job), then Carrel's `/people` when it suits.

**Field: a radio group with nothing chosen no longer paints every radio as chosen** (found building People and roles). A radio in a group with no checked radio matches `:indeterminate`, and the checked look (the primary fill and edge) was also given to `:indeterminate`, so an unanswered group showed every radio filled. The indeterminate look is now for checkboxes only (`.cap-check input[type="checkbox"]:indeterminate`), in the normal, disabled and forced-colors rules. CSS only; a checkbox's mixed state is unchanged.

**New: Tree** (`ul.cap-tree`, `data-cap="tree"`; `capsomer/react/tree`, `capsomer/behavior/tree`; job_3af6c3949bf8, Carrel's writing-tools ruling of 2026-10-10, point 7; a visual change that waits for Dustin's sign-off). Nested items a person opens and closes, chooses and moves, after the WAI-ARIA tree pattern: Carrel's binder, and any outline of folders, sections or series. One tab stop with the arrow keys inside (Down, Up, Right opens or goes in, Left closes or goes out, Home, End, `*`, typeahead), Enter follows a link row, Space chooses (`data-selection="none|single|multi"`). With `data-movable` (React `onMove`), Alt with an arrow moves a row up, down, into the branch above or out of its branch, and a row drags with a mouse or pen, its landing shown as a line before or after a row or a ring inside a branch. Every move is a cancelable `cap:tree-move` (`{ id, from, to }`) and is said in a polite live region; the tree stores nothing, and `reverseMove` gives the host its Undo. Delivered with no script it is nested lists with every level shown and every link working; `enhance` adds the roles (`tree`, `treeitem` with `aria-level`, `aria-posinset`, `aria-setsize`, `aria-expanded`, `aria-owns`; `group`; `none` on the list items), closes the branches marked `data-collapsed`, and adds the chevrons. New HTML contract: `ul.cap-tree` of `li.cap-tree-item[data-value]`, each holding `.cap-tree-row` (an `a` or a `span`, with `.cap-tree-icon`, `.cap-tree-label`, `.cap-tree-meta`) and, for a branch, `ul.cap-tree-group`; `data-branch`, `data-collapsed`, `data-selected`, `data-variant="surface"`; the script's `span.cap-tree-toggle`, `data-drop` and `data-dragging`; events `cap:tree-select`, `cap:tree-toggle`, `cap:tree-move`. Pure functions for a host that draws its own: `visibleRows`, `treeKey`, `planMove`, `dropMove`, `applyMove`, `reverseMove`, `movedText`, `findNode`, `findPlace`, `branchIds`. No class or token is removed or renamed. Swap: Carrel's binder sidebar (step 2 of the ruling's build order).

**New: Sortable list** (`ol.cap-sortable`, `data-cap="sortable-list"`; `capsomer/react/sortable-list`, `capsomer/behavior/sortable-list`; job_3af6c3949bf8, the same ruling; a visual change that waits for Dustin's sign-off). Rows, or cards in a wrapping grid (`data-layout="cards"`), a person puts in order three ways: drag the grip; press Space or Enter on the grip, move with the arrow keys, Home and End, drop with Space or Enter, and Escape (or leaving the list) puts it back; or Move up and Move down, the one-pointer way WCAG 2.5.7 asks for. The item moves as you go with no floating copy, focus stays on the grip or button pressed, and each step is said in a polite live region. The list owns no storage: a finished move is a cancelable `cap:sortable-change` (`{ order, id, from, to, by }`), and React calls `onReorder(order, move)` and shows the caller's `items` again. Inside a form it works with no script: Move up and Move down are submit buttons (`up=<id>`, `down=<id>`) and each item posts its id (`order`). New HTML contract: `ol.cap-sortable` of `li.cap-sortable-item[data-value]` holding `button.cap-sortable-handle` (`aria-pressed` while up, described by one help line), `.cap-sortable-body` (`.cap-sortable-label`, `.cap-sortable-detail`) and `.cap-sortable-steps` (`button.cap-btn[data-cap-part="up|down"]`), with an optional `input[type=hidden]`; `data-lifted`, `data-dragging`. Pure functions `moveId`, `keyTarget`, `indexAtPoint`, `sortText`, and `startPointerDrag`, the pointer drag the tree shares. No class or token is removed or renamed. Swap: Carrel's corkboard and a series' order (step 2 of the ruling's build order).

## 0.6.0 (2026-10-10)

**Moderation queue: the contract's states** (C3 of docs/design/design-content-components.md, job_a6d19ef929fc; Dustin's ruling, 2026-10-09; a changed HTML contract and a visual change that waits for Dustin's sign-off). The queue's states are site-api's `MentionStatus`, in these words: Waiting (`pending`), Approved, Rejected, Not yet checked (`unverified`) and Source not found (`failed`). Spam, Bin and Source gone go. Changed HTML contract: a row's `data-state` is `unverified`, `pending`, `approved`, `rejected` or `failed` (was `waiting`, `approved`, `spam` or `bin`); the root's `data-view`, the filter's radio values and `data-cap-count` are `pending`, `failed`, `approved`, `rejected` and `all` (were `waiting`, `approved`, `spam`, `bin` and `gone`); the filter's radios are named `view`, so the no-script form sends `?view=`; a row's `data-view`, `data-gone` and `data-gone-at` are removed; a row the site's next sweep removes carries `data-expiring`, and the sweep removes those (the queue no longer counts retention days itself); `data-cap-reset` on the root says the site offers Back to waiting. Changed actions: `data-cap-action` is `approve`, `reject`, `reset` or `delete` (was `approve`, `spam`, `bin`, `restore` or `delete`); keys `a` approve, `r` reject (was `s` spam; `r` was back to waiting), `d` delete after a confirm with the count to type (was `d` bin); Back to waiting has no key and shows only with `data-cap-reset`; Delete is on every row (was Delete permanently, in the bin only), and Delete permanently is now Delete. A mention not yet checked or whose source was not found has no decision, only Delete; a bulk decision leaves it and says so. Undo between approved and rejected is the opposite decision; from waiting only with `data-cap-reset`, and without it the message says there is no Undo. The confirm before approving a gone source goes. Changed words: the statuses and filters above; "Approved 2 mentions." and "Rejected the mention from X." (was "Marked X as spam.", "Moved X to the bin."); the empty states "No missing sources", "Nothing rejected", "No mentions yet" (were "No spam", "The bin is empty"); the live line "3 waiting, 1 not yet checked" (was "3 waiting, 2 with a source gone"). Removed exports: `ModView` value `gone`, `viewOf`, `countViews` (now `countStates`), `needsConfirm`, `RETENTION_DAYS`, `expiredIds`, `retentionNote`, `actionLabel` (now `ACTION_LABEL`); new `STATE_LABEL`, `ACTION_LABEL`, `DELETE_KEY`, `bulkActionsFor`, `inView`, `undoAction`, `sweepLines`, `NO_UNDO_TEXT`. **Removed: the React `ModerationQueue`** (`capsomer/react/moderation-queue`), which no app used; its React form is the mentions list. An app that matches the old words, states or keys changes them with this release.

**New: Mentions list for a site** (`.cap-mq.cap-mentions`, `data-cap="mentions-list"`; `capsomer/react/mentions-list`, `capsomer/behavior/mentions-list`; C3, job_a6d19ef929fc; a visual change that waits for Dustin's sign-off). One page of one site's mentions, the same component in Carrel and in every site admin: the moderation queue on the contract's data, drawing the queue's rows. Tabs as links with the site's counts (Waiting, Source not found, Approved, Rejected, All), opening on Waiting, or on All when nothing waits; "N not yet checked" as a pill linking to All; each row's decisions as a form (`ids`, `version`, `intent` approve, reject or reset), offered only on waiting, approved and rejected mentions; the bulk bar in form mode (approve, reject, Back to waiting, delete) with each mention's outcome; a sender's name opens the row through the address (`?open=`), in place with script; cursor paging; delete with the count to type and the retention sweep with the contract's `expiring` counts, each behind `result.confirm` (the confirm dialog, or the confirm page with no script). Every action is a form post, so it works with no script; with `submit` a decision runs in place, the message offers Undo where the site can reverse it, and the keys decide (`j`, `k`, `a`, `r`, `d`, `x`, Esc). An action is offered only when the site supports it (`offers.reset`, `offers.delete`, `offers.sweep`) and the person may decide mentions (`can.decideMentions`); a withheld one is named in words. New HTML contract: `section.cap-mq.cap-mentions[data-view]` holding `nav.cap-mentions-tabs`, `.cap-mq-head` (`.cap-mq-summary`, a `.cap-pill`, `.cap-mentions-count`), `ul.cap-mentions-withheld`, `div.cap-content-result`, `form.cap-mentions-bulk-form` and the bulk bar, `ul.cap-mq-list` whose rows hold `form.cap-row-actions.cap-mentions-act` and a title link with `aria-expanded`, `nav.cap-mentions-pages`, and `form.cap-mq-retention`. Swap: Carrel's mentions screen (K3) and dustinedwards-info's admin mentions (D3), each through site-api's content kit.

**`capsomer/content`: mention types** (C3). New `MentionStatus`, `MentionCounts`, `MentionsQuery`, `MentionOffers` and `MentionsData`, whose rows are the moderation queue's `Mention` (which gains `failureReason`, `version` and `expiring`, and loses `gone` and `goneAt`). Defined from the design, to be reconciled with S1's types.

**Media: Trash, not bin** (Dustin, 2026-10-10, on C2). The media component and the media library say Trash, the site-api contract's word, wherever they said bin. Changed words: the flag "In the bin" is "In Trash"; the button "Move to the bin" is "Move to Trash" (inspector and bulk bar); the view "Bin" is "Trash" (media's segmented view and the library's tab); "The bin is empty" is "Trash is empty"; "A file you move to the bin keeps its address" is "A file you move to Trash keeps its address"; the count "N in the bin" is "N in Trash" (the library's "N files in the bin" likewise); the messages "Moved X to the bin." and "Moved {n} files to the bin." say "to Trash."; "Put it back in the bin." and "Put {n} file{s} back in the bin." say "in Trash."; the library's list name "Files in the bin" is "Files in Trash"; its tabs are named "library or Trash"; the bulk action's `data-cap-label` is "Trash". Code names do not change: the state `binned`, `data-cap-part="bin"`, the view value `bin`, `data-cap-count="bin"`, `data-cap-empty="bin"`, `bulkHandlers().bin` and `onBin`. An app that matches these words in its own tests or copy changes them with this release. The message and empty-state pages' sample sentences say Trash too (specimens only: "Move to Trash", "Moved the mention from fieldnotes.example to Trash.", "In Trash", "No mentions in Trash").

**New: Media library for a site** (`.cap-media-lib`, `data-cap="media-library"`; `capsomer/react/media-library`, `capsomer/behavior/media-library`; C2 of docs/design/design-content-components.md, job_a6d19ef929fc; a visual change that waits for Dustin's sign-off). One page of one site's files, the same component in Carrel and in every site admin, composed from `media` (grid, tiles, inspector), `drop-zone`, `chips`, `tabs`, `bulk-bar` and the shared intent loop: search as a GET form; tags and a host's lenses as link chips; the library and Trash as tabs; grid or list in the address; upload as a multipart form checked against the site's own limits, with alt text asked and a suggestion offered, never applied; the bulk bar in form mode (add and remove a tag, move to Trash, restore, delete for good) with each file's outcome, a refused delete listing where the file is used; the inspector opened by the address (`?inspect=`), reading where the file is used, in form mode (the default: one form per field with its own Save, which works with no script) or autosave mode (with `submit`); Undo for Trash and tags; and cursor paging. `mode="picker"` is the same grid, images only, with a required alt step: Insert, or Insert as decorative; with script `onPick` gets `{ id, url, alt }`, with no script the form posts `intent=pick`. It replaces Carrel's `library-insert.tsx`, dustinedwards.info's `media-picker.tsx` and germomics' `window.prompt` calls." The words say Trash, as the contract does (`view=trash`, `intent=trash`). New HTML contract: `section.cap-media-lib` holding `nav.cap-media-lib-tabs`, `form.cap-media-lib-filters`, `nav.cap-media-lib-chips` (lists of `a.cap-chip`), `div.cap-media-lib-bar` (`.cap-media-lib-count`, `.cap-media-lib-tools`, `nav.cap-media-lib-layout`, `form.cap-media-lib-extra`), `ul.cap-media-lib-withheld`, `form.cap-media-lib-upload`, `form.cap-media-lib-bulk-form`, the media grid and inspector, `form.cap-media-pick[data-cap-part="pick"]` (`.cap-media-pick-title`, `.cap-media-pick-preview`, `.cap-media-pick-actions`) and `nav.cap-media-lib-pages`. Swap: Carrel's media screen and editor insert (K2), dustinedwards-info's admin media (D2), germomics' upload field (G2).

**`capsomer/content`: media types and the shared intent loop.** New types `MediaData`, `MediaQuery`, `MediaOffers` and `PickedMedia`. `intentForm` and `outcomesOf` move here from the posts list (still exported from `capsomer/behavior/posts-list`), so `capsomer/content` is no longer types only. New `capsomer/react/content`: `useIntents`, the loop every content component runs (a post goes through `submit` or the browser; the result is said with Undo; each item's outcome goes to the bulk bar; a `result.confirm` is the confirm dialog or the confirm page), and `ResultBox`. Changed HTML contract (unreleased, from C1): the list's own result box is `div.cap-content-result`, with Undo as `form.cap-content-undo`, in the new `content.css`, in place of `.cap-posts-result` and `.cap-posts-undo`.

**Media: form mode, and a site's own fields** (C2). `MediaInspector` takes `show` (which fields), `saving="form"` with `action` and `Form` (one `form.cap-media-field-form` per group of fields with its own Save, posting `save-alt`, `save-details` or `save-tags` with `ids` and `version`), `closeHref` (Close as a link) and `can.delete`; with `action`, Move to Trash, Restore and Delete for good are each a `form.cap-media-act`. `MediaGrid` takes `hrefFor`, `selectForm` (the boxes post as `ids`) and `selectable`. `MediaRecord` takes `version` and `usedUnknown` (no Unattached flag where a list did not read where a file is used). Changed: a binned tile's Restore is drawn only when `onRestore` is given. Without the new props nothing else changes. No class or token is removed or renamed.

**Media: the inspector's preview no longer slides under its fields** (a fix found in C2). In a pane shorter than its content, the inspector's rows shrank to fit, and the preview, sized only by its aspect ratio, shrank to nothing while its picture still painted, under the alt text. `.cap-media-form` sets `grid-auto-rows: max-content`, so each row keeps its content's height and the body scrolls. CSS only.

**Chips: link chips** (C2). `a.cap-chip[aria-current]` wears the pressed look (the tick, the accent edge and tint), for a filter kept in the address, and `ul.cap-chips` drops the list's marks. A button chip is unchanged.

**New: Posts list** (`.cap-posts`, `data-cap="posts-list"`; `capsomer/react/posts-list`, `capsomer/behavior/posts-list`; C1 of docs/design/design-content-components.md, job_a6d19ef929fc; a visual change that waits for Dustin's sign-off). One page of one site's posts, the same component in Carrel and in every site admin: status tabs with counts, search, kind, tag and sort as a GET form (the view is in the address), a table whose title links to the editor, a row menu (Edit, View on the site, Unpublish, Duplicate, Delete), the bulk bar in form mode (add and remove a tag, duplicate, unpublish, delete), a host's own columns and toolbar actions as data, and "Next page" and "First page" by cursor. An action is offered only when the site supports it (`data.offers`) and the person may run it (`data.can`); a withheld one is named in words. Every action is a form post, so it works with no script and the page comes back with `result`; with `submit` the same posts run in place, the message region offers Undo (`z`), and a one-way intent the kit answers with `result.confirm` opens the confirm dialog (the confirm page with no script), with the count to type. `contentStatus(status, publishAt, now)` is the one mapping from a post's status to its word, shape and "in 3 days". New HTML contract: `section.cap-posts` holding `nav.cap-posts-tabs`, `form.cap-posts-filters`, `div.cap-posts-bar` (`.cap-posts-count`, `.cap-posts-tools`, `.cap-posts-extra`), `ul.cap-posts-withheld`, `div.cap-content-result` (Undo is `form.cap-content-undo`; shared with the media library, in `content.css`), `form.cap-posts-bulk-form`, and `table.cap-posts-table` with `.cap-posts-check`, `.cap-posts-title` (`.cap-posts-meta`, `.cap-posts-path`, `.cap-posts-kind`, `.cap-posts-notes`), `.cap-posts-status` (`.cap-posts-when`), `.cap-posts-updated` and `.cap-posts-actions`; `nav.cap-posts-pages`. The design's "Show more" default is "Next page" here, because a cursor page replaces the one before it. Swap: Carrel's posts list (K1), then dustinedwards-info's (D1) and germomics' (G1), each through site-api's content kit.

**New: `capsomer/content`** (types only, no runtime). The view data the shared content components take and what their forms get back: `PostsData`, `PostRow`, `PostsQuery`, `PostOffers`, `ContentCan`, `RowNote`, `ExtraColumn`, `ExtraAction`, `PageInfo`, `IntentResult`, `IntentOutcome`, `ConfirmRequest`, `SubmitIntent`, `MediaUse`, `ContentStatus`. Defined from the design because site-api's content kit (S1) has not shipped; to be reconciled with S1's types when it does. Additions to the design's shapes: `PostsData.newHref`, `PageInfo.sortedOnPage`, `IntentResult.confirm`, and notes on the row (`PostRow.notes`) rather than in `extra`.

**Bulk bar: each item's outcome** (C1). After an action that acted on several items, the bar lists what happened to each: `div.cap-bulk-results[data-cap-part="results"]` (a group named by `p.cap-bulk-results-summary`, "2 done, 1 not done."), `ul.cap-bulk-results-list` with one `li[data-ok]` per item, refusals first (a Done or Not done status, `.cap-bulk-result-label`, `.cap-bulk-result-message`, and `ul.cap-bulk-result-uses` for where a refused file is used), and `button[data-cap-part="dismiss-results"]`, rendered `hidden` by a server and shown by the script. While the list is there the bar stays in view with nothing selected (its count says "Nothing selected") and does not stick. New: `BulkOutcome`, `outcomeSummary`, `orderOutcomes`, `showOutcomes(bar, outcomes)`; React `outcomes`, `onDismissOutcomes` and `prompt` (form mode: the count's words before script runs). Changed HTML contract: the outcome list is new; a bar without one is unchanged. No class or token is removed or renamed. Swap: it replaces Carrel's three Result panels (`project.tsx`, `media.tsx`, `mentions.tsx`) as K1 to K3 land.

**Form menu: link items.** `FormMenuItem.href` makes an item a link, `a.cap-option.cap-menu-item[href]`, with the same look ("Edit", "View on the site"); a danger item is never a link. The arrow keys move through buttons and links alike. Changed HTML contract: a form menu may hold `a.cap-menu-item` beside its submit buttons. No class or token is removed or renamed.

**Confirm page: a list value posts one field per item.** `ConfirmPage`'s `hidden` takes `string | string[]`; a list is one hidden input per item under one name (the ticked `ids`). A string is unchanged.

**Message: `useOptionalMessage()`** (`capsomer/react/message`): the message API, or `null` when no `MessageProvider` is above, for a component that also works without a region. Nothing else changes.

## 0.5.0 (2026-10-08)

**Admin shell: the phone layout, eight fixes** (job_c29ddc144d09; from the review in capsomer/research/adminshell-phone-review.md, all eight picked by Dustin 2026-10-08; a visual change that waits for his sign-off). (1) The More sheet pins the account rows (settings, shortcuts, Sign out) under the scrolling list, so Sign out is in view on a 360 x 740 phone. (2) Every sheet row starts its label in one column: each row holds a 24px `span.cap-admin-sheet-icon`, empty when the row has no icon, and the sheet's rows share one side inset (the dialog's own link rule gave a link 11px and a button 12px). (3) Opening the sheet focuses its heading (`h2#cap-admin-sheet-title`, `tabindex="-1"`), not the first row; Tab goes on from there and no ring shows after a tap. (4) The current tab has an accent pill (`--accent-4`) behind its icon, with its color and `aria-current`. (5) A tab badge reads `99+` past 99 (the accessible name keeps the real count) and sits clear of the glyph, ringed in the bar's color. (6) Tab labels are 12px, and all five fit at 360px. (7) On a phone the thin bar names the current app at the left, before the status line. (8) The sheet's bottom safe-area inset was already applied by the shared dialog (`padding-block-end: env(safe-area-inset-bottom)` on a bottom placement); nothing was added, so it is not doubled. It still needs a check on a phone with a home indicator, and the page needs `viewport-fit=cover` for the inset to be non-zero.
