Changed HTML contract: `div.cap-admin-bar` is always rendered (`data-empty` when there is no status or actions; it is not displayed on a wide screen, and on a phone it shows the app name alone) and holds `div.cap-admin-bar-text` (`strong.cap-admin-bar-app`, then the status) beside `div.cap-admin-actions`; the sheet's account rows move out of the body into `nav.cap-admin-sheet-account[aria-label="Account"]`; the sheet's title is a plain `h2.cap-dialog-title` rather than `DialogTitle`. New CSS classes `.cap-admin-bar-text`, `.cap-admin-bar-app`, `.cap-admin-sheet-icon`, `.cap-admin-sheet-account`. No class or token is removed or renamed. Swap: the Portal takes it with the next release.

**Reduced motion: no transition starts at all** (a fix found by #43). The catch-all in `css/base.css` set `transition-duration: 0.01ms`, so under reduced motion every color or weight change (a link becoming current) still began a real, if tiny, `CSSTransition`, and `document.getAnimations()` listed it until the next frame. It now sets `transition-duration: 0s` and `transition-delay: 0s`: no transition runs. Animations keep `0.01ms` so `animationend` still fires. No class or token is removed or renamed.

**Admin shell: page transitions** (item 22 of capsomer/research/admin-shell-findings.md, job_e4a050b1e6e4; a visual change, waits for Dustin's sign-off before release). An option, off by default: `transition` on React `AdminShell`, `data-transition="page"` on `.cap-admin`. The content area cross-fades between pages (`--dur-fast` out, `--dur-base` in) while the strip, menu and phone bar stay put, using the View Transitions API. New `startPageTransition(update, shell?)` and `pageTransitionsOn` (`capsomer/behavior/admin-shell`; `startPageTransition` also from `capsomer/react/admin-shell`): wrap the page change; with no API, with `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce`, or without the option the update runs at once, with no animation and no error. Cross-document use is the app's own `@view-transition { navigation: auto; }`. New HTML contract (only with the option): `data-transition="page"` on the shell. New CSS: `view-transition-name` `cap-admin-page`, `cap-admin-strip`, `cap-admin-menu`, `cap-admin-tabs` and the `::view-transition-*` rules, all inside `prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference`. New specimen `transition`. No class or token is removed or renamed. Swap: the Portal's page changes take it when it adopts the option.

**New: Email templates** (`capsomer/email`, D7.2, job_a1cd3ed31049; design approved by Dustin 2026-10-06, a visual change that waits for his sign-off and his check in a real mail client). `renderEmail(template, data)` returns `{ subject, html, text }` from one definition, so the HTML and the text cannot disagree; it is pure (no dependency, no filesystem, no fetch) and runs in a Worker, in Node and in a test. Five templates: `magicLink`, `verification`, `reset`, `alert` (`crit`, `warn`, `info`, the level a word in the subject and the first line) and `notice`; a product defines more with the same block types (`Template<D>`). Every value is escaped, a link must be `https:` or `mailto:` with no space or control character or `renderEmail` throws, single-line values are folded to one line, and a token in a link is passed through untouched and shown only in that link. A product keeps its own wording through `copy` (`subject`, `heading`, `intro`, `button`, `ignore`) and its own footer lines through `footer` (Foxhound's unsubscribe and CAN-SPAM lines stay Foxhound's). Look: one centered column of 600px at most in nested tables with inline styles, system fonts, the product's name in type (no logo image), light as the base and dark best effort through `color-scheme`. Colors are the family's literal hexes in the new generated `components/email/colors.ts` (`tokens/palette.mjs` writes it, `npm run check` refuses a file that differs); the button fills with step 10 and carries `primary-fg` (rule 18, not step 9 as the design note said). New: export `capsomer/email`, generated file `components/email/colors.ts`, doc page and states page `components/email`. No class or token is removed or renamed, and no HTML contract of an existing component changes. Swap: Carrel's alert mails (`health.server.ts`, `ai.server.ts`) adopt first, then dustinedwards-info's watchdog mail, Foxing's and TXASM's `email.server.ts`; Foxhound's customer mail last, after its rebuild. The existing senders were not readable by this driver and stay unverified; the sending helper goes to site-runtime.

**Dialog: a centered dialog fits its content** (job_af2f0a09c587; a fix, look approved by Dustin 2026-10-07). `.cap-dialog` set `height: auto`, which on a modal overrides the browser's `fit-content` and stretches the box between its insets: a short centered dialog was 688px high in a 720px window. It is now `height: fit-content`, still capped by `max-height`, so the body scrolls past it as before. Centered, alert and top-placed dialogs (confirm dialog, command menu, rename and delete dialogs) shrink to their content; the side sheets (`height: 100%`) and the bottom sheet are unchanged. No class, token or HTML contract changes.

**Combobox: a tag field** (gap 4 of dustinedwards-info PR #320, job_510a2aa10d7e). `ComboboxMultiple` takes `creatable` (and `createLabel`): typed text that is not an item is offered as a first row, `Create “text”`, highlighted so Enter adds exactly what was typed, and becomes a chip like a listed value; text that matches an item or a tag already on the field (ignoring case and extra spaces) makes nothing new. `values` stay `string[]`, a made value is its text, and values not in `items` show as chips. Chips plus the combobox compose the field, so no new component. New markup only with `creatable`: the create row is one more `.cap-option` at the top of the list. Without it nothing changes. No class or token is removed or renamed. Swap: dustinedwards-info's `bulk-tag-controls` and post tag field, and Carrel's media tags job (job_b4acbaabf747), take it.

**History list: load into editor** (gap 7 of dustinedwards-info PR #320). `data-load-href` on the root (React `loadHref`, `onLoad`; `BuildOptions.loadHref`) shows "Load into editor" on each earlier line in place of Restore: a link to that address plus the version's id, so it works without script, with a cancelable `cap:history-load` (detail `{ entry }`) for an app that loads the text in place. Nothing is added to the history; the editor's save makes the version. New HTML contract (only when the attribute is set): `a.cap-btn[data-cap-part="load"][data-version]` in `.cap-row-actions` where `button[data-cap-part="restore"]` was. Without the attribute nothing changes. No class or token is removed or renamed. Swap: dustinedwards-info's revision row action "Load into editor" takes it; Carrel's history job (job_c30fd19cc41b) can choose either action.

**Bulk bar: form mode** (gap 6 of dustinedwards-info PR #320). A bar over a plain form: its actions are `<button type="submit" name="intent" value="...">`, the browser posts the ticked `name="ids"` boxes and the intent, and the server confirms a destructive one with its own page, so the bar works with no script. With script it counts, hides while nothing is ticked and refuses a press with nothing ticked; it does not run, preview or offer Undo for a submit button. React: `form` on `BulkBar` and `submit: true` on an action (`BulkBarAction.run` is optional). New HTML contract, only for a bar with submit buttons: the bar is not `hidden` in the delivered page (the script hides it). The button mode is unchanged. No class or token is removed or renamed. Swap: dustinedwards-info's `bulk-action-bar` forms and Carrel's media and mention bulk jobs (job_b4acbaabf747, job_43166f1bbe3a) take it.

**Menu: a form menu** (gap 9 of dustinedwards-info PR #320, job_510a2aa10d7e). `details.cap-menu-form[data-cap="menu-form"]` holding a `summary.cap-btn.cap-menu-trigger` and a `form.cap-popover.cap-menu` of `button[type=submit].cap-option.cap-menu-item` (`name="intent"`, `value`), a row-actions menu that works with no script: the Base UI menu has nothing in the delivered HTML. Same surface and item look, danger last after a separator, a disabled item a real `disabled` button with its reason in plain text; not a `role="menu"`. New behavior module `capsomer/behavior/menu` (`enhance`, `stepIndex`): Esc, arrow keys, ArrowDown on the closed summary, closing on an outside press. React `FormMenu` (`label`, `action`, `method`, `items`, `name`, `hidden`). The Base UI `Menu` is unchanged. No class or token is removed or renamed. Swap: dustinedwards-info's row action menus of form submits take it.

**Confirm dialog: a confirm page** (gap 1 of dustinedwards-info PR #320, job_510a2aa10d7e). `form.cap-confirm-page[data-cap="confirm-page"]` (React `ConfirmPage`): the confirmation as a form in the page, for a server that answers a destructive request with its own page, with no script. The title, lead, list of changes, reason and typed word are the dialog's, the action is a danger submit button, Cancel is a link, and the typed word and reason are the browser's own `required` and `pattern` checks (`wordPattern(word)`, new in `capsomer/behavior/confirm-dialog`, writes the case-insensitive pattern); the server must check them again and, when it refuses, renders the page again with the message in the `role="alert"` box. New HTML contract (new element, the dialog is unchanged): `form.cap-confirm-page` with `.cap-dialog-header`, `.cap-dialog-body`, `.cap-confirm-reason`, `.cap-confirm-typed`, `.cap-confirm-error` and `.cap-dialog-footer`. New CSS class `.cap-confirm-page` only. No class or token is removed or renamed. Swap: dustinedwards-info's server-rendered confirm pages (posts, media) take it, and it is the page the form-mode bulk bar (#28) and form menu (#30) send a destructive intent to.

**Dialog: in the page until script opens it** (gap 2 of dustinedwards-info PR #320, job_510a2aa10d7e). `data-cap-inline` on a `<dialog open>`: with no script it flows in the page as a card (new CSS `dialog.cap-dialog[data-cap-inline]:not(:modal)`: static position, no shadow) and its form works; `enhance()` removes `open` on load so a trigger (`data-cap-dialog-open`, a link to `#id` is fine) opens it as the modal. A `data-cap-part="cancel"` or `close` that is a link now closes the modal and stays on the page (`preventDefault`), and is a plain link with no script. New attribute only; a dialog without `data-cap-inline` is unchanged. No class or token is removed or renamed. Swap: dustinedwards-info's edit and rename dialogs that are shown in the page before script runs take it.

**Markdown editor: a scrolling editor is reachable by keyboard** (gap 12 of dustinedwards-info PR #320, job_510a2aa10d7e; a fix). A long text overflowed its scroller (`.cm-scroller`, CodeMirror's `tabindex="-1"`) and axe's `scrollable-region-focusable` failed in every state: editable, read-only and disabled (a disabled editor's text cannot take focus, so a keyboard could not scroll it at all). While the text overflows the scroller now takes `tabindex="0"` and gives CodeMirror's `-1` back when it does not; arrow keys and Page Down scroll it, and Tab then moves into the text, so a long editable editor has one extra tab stop. No HTML contract of the editor changes beyond that attribute, and no class or token is removed or renamed. Swap: dustinedwards-info's editor, which fails the rule, takes the fix with the next release.

**Tabs: a link slot for router links** (gap 10 of dustinedwards-info PR #320, job_510a2aa10d7e). React `TabsNav` takes `renderLink` (and `TabLink` its own, which wins): a function given a plain `<a>`'s props with `href` set, `cap-tab` and `aria-current="page"` on it and the count inside, returning the app's router link, so a tab link navigates in the app with no page load and keeps its look and state. New exports `RenderTabLink`, `TabLinkProps`; `TabLink`'s `href` is now required (it was optional in the type). Without `renderLink` nothing changes in the markup. No class or token is removed or renamed. Swap: dustinedwards-info's tab links that are router links take it.

**Shell: a site logo slot in the brand area** (the shell logo slot of job_510a2aa10d7e). React `Shell` takes `logo` (an `<img alt="">` or `<svg>`) and `brandLabel`: the logo is drawn before the name as `span.cap-shell-logo[aria-hidden="true"]` inside the brand link, held to the bar's height and in proportion; the link is named by the brand text, or by `brandLabel` when `brand` is null. New HTML contract (only when a logo is given): `a.cap-shell-brand > span.cap-shell-logo`, and `aria-label` on the link for a logo-only brand. New CSS class `.cap-shell-logo`. A shell without `logo` is unchanged. No class or token is removed or renamed. Swap: dustinedwards-info's and Carrel's top-bar logos take it.

**Detail panel: driven by the address** (gap 8 of dustinedwards-info PR #320, job_510a2aa10d7e). `data-cap-detail-param="inspect"` on the panel: it is open while the address carries `?inspect=<id>`, other parts of the address kept. A row link is `href="?inspect=<id>"` (works with no script when the server renders the panel `open`); with script `enhance()` opens it as the modal on load and on a plain click, closing takes the address back (a pushed entry is taken back, an address that began open is rewritten with no new entry), and Back and Forward move the panel. The panel holds plain forms that post to the server. New pure helpers `detailUrl` and `detailInUrl`. New attribute only; the hash mode (`data-cap-history`) is unchanged and the two are not combined. No class or token is removed or renamed. Swap: dustinedwards-info's and Carrel's URL-driven inspectors of plain forms take it.

**New: Page head** (`.cap-page-head`, `capsomer/react/page-head`; gap 11 of dustinedwards-info PR #320, job_510a2aa10d7e). The top of a view inside the shell: an optional breadcrumb (the shared Breadcrumb, with `renderLink` for a router), the page's one `h1`, one line saying what the page is for, and the page's actions at the right, wrapping below on a narrow space. Extracted from Carrel's `PageHead` and the site admin's copy (`.app-head`). The page frame already existed (`.cap-shell-page`, `.cap-admin-page`), so only the head is new. New HTML contract: `header.cap-page-head` holding `div.cap-page-head-text` (`nav.cap-breadcrumb`, `h1.cap-page-head-title`, `p.cap-page-head-lead`) and `div.cap-page-head-actions`. No class or token is removed or renamed. Swap: Carrel's and dustinedwards-info's `PageHead`, `.app-head` and `.app-actions` take it. Their layout helpers (`.app-form`, `.app-fields`, `.app-filters`, `.app-facts`, `.app-pills`) are not part of it.

**New: Share preview** (`.cap-serp`, `.cap-og-card`, `capsomer/react/share-preview`; gap 5 of dustinedwards-info PR #320, job_510a2aa10d7e). How a page appears where it is shared, before it goes out: a search result (address, title, description, an optional note) and a social card (an image box at the real 1200 x 630 shape, host, title, optional description and note). Read-only: nothing in them takes focus, each is a named group, and the app passes the text already cut to its limits. Extracted from the site admin's `SerpPreview` and `OgPreview` (`.serp-*`, `.og-preview-*`); nothing similar existed in Capsomer. New HTML contract: `div.cap-serp` (`.cap-serp-url`, `-title`, `-description`, `-note`) and `div.cap-og-card` (`.cap-og-card-image`, `-body`, `-host`, `-title`, `-description`, `-note`). No class or token is removed or renamed. Swap: dustinedwards-info's `SerpPreview`, `OgPreview`, `.serp-*` and `.og-*` take it.

**Three gaps from Carrel's history and bulk build** (job_b7fca0656ce5, from Carrel PR #25).

- **Table: a row checkbox is above the row link.** `.cap-check` joins links and buttons above the stretched `.cap-table-open` area, so a checkbox in a row that opens a detail ticks and the row stays shut. CSS only; no markup changes. Carrel's three-line workaround in `app/app.css` can be deleted when it takes this release.
- **History list: read-only, and `words` optional.** `data-readonly` on the root (React `readOnly`; `BuildOptions.readOnly`) leaves an earlier line with no action, neither Restore nor Load; the current line still says Current. `HistoryEntry.words` is optional: a line with none shows no size change, as an empty `span.cap-hist-delta[data-sign="none"][aria-hidden="true"]` that keeps the columns, with no `data-words` attribute and its id left out of the title link's `aria-describedby`; a run of autosaves holding such a line has none either. `deltaOf` now returns `number | undefined` and `Run.delta` is optional. No class or token is removed or renamed. Carrel's revision table can use it in place of the table and checkbox classes it built.
- **Draft compare, patch mode: a revision label is not a rename.** A file whose old and new paths are one name with different `@` suffixes (`id@v1`, `id@v2`) is headed by the name once, with "v1 to v2" in the existing `.cap-patch-kind` beside it, instead of "id@v1 → id@v2". Changed HTML contract: for such a pair `.cap-patch-path code` holds the name only and `.cap-patch-kind` holds the revisions, and the table caption names the file once. `revisionPair(old, new)` is new, pure and exported. Any other pair of paths reads as before.

## 0.4.0 (2026-10-07)

**New: Admin shell** (`.cap-admin`, `data-cap="admin-shell"`; `capsomer/behavior/admin-shell`, `capsomer/react/admin-shell`; from Dustin's pick of 2026-10-06, job_f60172264c51). The one structure every admin area, dashboard and site shares: a strip of apps and tools (always shown, `--rail-w-collapsed` wide), a grouped menu for the current app (collapsible, `--rail-w` wide; collapsed it shrinks to an icon column at `--rail-w-collapsed` beside the strip, same order and places, labels and group headings hidden, a tooltip with the label on hover and focus, the count or a dot kept on the icon, the current page's dark fill kept), then the content. The current app and the current page are dark filled (`--accent-12`); a badge sits only on another app; a plain number counts and a violet pill needs you; tools (Search, Inbox, Help) and the account panel (the Popover API: this app, elsewhere, sign out) sit at the strip's bottom; G then a number jumps between apps; on a phone the strip and the menu live in one sheet behind the tab bar's More. All links and content are in the server-rendered HTML. New HTML contract: `div.cap-admin` with `nav.cap-admin-strip`, `nav.cap-admin-menu` (a link holds an icon, `span.cap-admin-label` and `span.cap-admin-count`; collapsed state is `data-menu="collapsed"` on the root, and the menu is never `hidden`), `main.cap-admin-main` (`.cap-admin-bar`, `.cap-admin-page`), `nav.cap-admin-tabs` and `dialog.cap-admin-sheet`; see `components/admin-shell/admin-shell.md`. The older `Shell` is unchanged apart from `LinkProps["aria-current"]`, which now also allows `"true"`.

**Changed token: `--ground` in light** is near-white (OKLCH lightness 0.985, was 0.965), changed in the generator (`tokens/palette.mjs`) and regenerated: `tokens/color.css`, `family-fox.css`, `family-teal.css`, `themes.css`, `tokens.json` and the Enarratio theme. Every contrast pair still passes. Dark keeps its ground. No token or class is removed or renamed.

**New: Catalog** (`.cap-catalog`, `data-cap="catalog"`; `capsomer/behavior/catalog`, `capsomer/react/catalog`; from the publications list and phage table of dustinedwards.info). A searchable, filterable, sortable collection: search with ranking, combinable facets with counts (a facet's counts hold the other filters and not its own), removable filter chips that are links, a sort menu, a table that becomes cards on a phone (the table's opt-in reflow), a filter tray over the results on a narrow catalog, pagination, and the empty states. A collection declares its fields once (`defineCatalog`); `parseCatalogParams` and `queryCatalog` are pure, so a server renders the same result a browser would. The state is in the address in one fixed order (`catalogHref`, `catalogRedirect`), it is a GET form that works with no script, and the behavior swaps regions in place with `fetch`, falling back to navigation on any failure. See `components/catalog/catalog.md`.

**Draft compare: a patch mode** (from the site admin's `DiffBlock`, dustinedwards PR #320 gap 3). `renderPatch(patch, { id, title })` draws a unified git patch by line: a heading and counts for each file, old and new line numbers, a literal plus or minus on each changed line with "added" or "removed" for a screen reader, hunk headers, a note for a binary file, and a status for an empty patch; each file scrolls in a focusable, named region. `parsePatch`, `patchCounts` and `patchCountsText` are pure. React `DraftPatch`. New HTML contract: `section.cap-compare.cap-patch[data-mode="patch"]` with `.cap-patch-file`, `.cap-patch-path`, `.cap-patch-scroll`, `.cap-patch-table` and `tr.cap-patch-line[data-op]`; the existing `.cap-compare-*` key, counts and bar are reused. No class or token is removed or renamed, and the word compare's markup is unchanged. Swap: dustinedwards-info's `DiffBlock` `<pre>`, and Carrel's history job (job_c30fd19cc41b), take `DraftPatch`.

**Catalog: headings over the rows.** A definition may declare `group: { field, value?, label? }`. In the default order with nothing typed, the shown rows come back as `result.groups` (and render as one `tbody` per group, each opening with a row-group header, `tr.cap-catalog-group > th[scope=rowgroup]`); in any other order, or with a search typed, `groups` is `null` and the rows are one flat table. A column may set `hideWhenGrouped`, so a Year column is left out under year headings and shows in every flat view. `defineCatalog` throws unless the group field is a sortable field the default sort orders by. New HTML contract: `tbody` repeats, and `tr.cap-catalog-group` is the heading row. No class or token is removed or renamed. A facet with no options is no longer drawn (its fieldset, legend and "Any" are omitted).

## 0.3.0 (2026-10-02)

Two parts. Part 1 extracts the writing and admin pieces of dustinedwards.info's admin (`app/components/admin/*`, `app/routes/admin.*`, in the `dustinedwards-info` repository) into Capsomer, rebuilt in Capsomer's own CSS from the shared dialog, listbox, popover and meter, with their craft matched to the nearest shadcn/ui pattern (the pin in 0.2.0 is unchanged). Part 2 connects Capsomer to Enarratio (0.2.0, from GitHub): a chart palette, a stylesheet section that maps Enarratio's `--enarratio-*` properties onto Capsomer's tokens, a generated `capsomerTheme`, and a chart frame. Enarratio never imports Capsomer, and Capsomer never imports Enarratio at run time.

**New: basics the admin pieces needed** (each matched to its shadcn/ui counterpart)

- **Avatar** (`.cap-avatar`, `data-cap="avatar"`; `capsomer/behavior/avatar`, `capsomer/react/avatar`): initials in the HTML with the picture over them (a failed picture gives way to the initials), sizes `sm|lg`, `data-shape="square"`, `data-kind="ai"`, a corner badge, `.cap-avatar-group` with `.cap-avatar-count`.
- **Tabs**: panels form `.cap-tabs[data-cap="tabs"]` with `.cap-tabs-list[role=tablist]`, `button.cap-tab[role=tab]`, `.cap-tabs-panel[role=tabpanel]` (inactive panels `hidden`, all in the HTML); links form `nav.cap-tabs-list` with `a.cap-tab[aria-current=page]` (the better default for admin filters); `.cap-tab-count`; `data-orientation="vertical"`, `data-activation="manual"`, `data-sync="hash"`, list `data-variant="line"`, `data-size`, `data-fill`. Behavior `enhance, select, nextTab`; event `cap:tabs-change`. React `Tabs, TabsList, TabsTrigger, TabsContent, TabsNav, TabLink`.
- **Breadcrumb**: `nav.cap-breadcrumb` > `ol.cap-breadcrumb-list` > `li.cap-breadcrumb-item`, CSS separators, the current page `aria-current="page"`, middle levels folded into a popover of links (`data-max-items`). Behavior `enhance, collapse, foldedLevels`. React `Breadcrumb`.
- **Pagination**: `nav.cap-pagination` with `.cap-pagination-status` and a list of `.cap-page` links or buttons (`data-kind="prev|next"`), seven slots with gaps, disabled ends `aria-disabled`, a compact "Page 3 of 16" under 34rem of container width; button form `data-cap="pagination"`. Behavior `enhance, setPage, render, pageItems, showingRange, statusText, pageCountOf`; event `cap:page-change`. React `Pagination`.

**New: writing and admin components** (extracted from dustinedwards.info; where, below)

- **Draft compare** (`.cap-compare`, `data-cap="draft-compare"`; from `revision-list.tsx` `DiffBlock` and the history route). Side by side by default, with inline and changes-only views (all three in the HTML, chosen by CSS, remembered in `localStorage`); differences by sentence and then by word (Myers, bounded); unchanged passages collapsed with one sentence of context; a "No differences" state; `ins.cap-ins` and `del.cap-del` carry a plus or minus mark and hidden "insertion start/end" text; an always-visible legend with counts; jump markers (`n` and `p`, a live "Change 2 of 5"). Pure functions `diffWords, diffSentences, analyze, splitSentences, commonSubsequence, countWords, countsText, tokenize, renderCompare, esc` in `capsomer/behavior/draft-compare` (renders on a server as a string); `enhance, setView, toggleAll`; React `DraftCompare` (optional `runs` shows authorship per sentence).
- **Authorship marks** (`.cap-authorship`, `.cap-run[data-kind="you|ai|quoted"]`): three declared kinds, shown or hidden by one switch (remembered; works without script), each run marked by a margin rule in a different pattern (solid, dashed, dotted) and a text label, plus a summary of shares by words. `summarize, summaryText, summaryHtml`, `enhance, refresh, setShown, isShown, collectRuns`; React `Authorship, Run`.
- **Flag list** (`.cap-flags`, `.cap-flag`; from the checks and feedback code, `check-copy.mjs`, `publish-policy.mjs`): blocking and advisory flags, open and resolved (the record stays, with who and when), a flag anchored to text whose text has gone ("Anchor lost", last-known quote kept), a suggestion inert until the owner applies it, one at a time (Undo) or in a batch (a preview first). Built on the row list; the filter is CSS over real radios, so every flag is in the HTML. `groupFlags, countFlags, summaryLine, anchorStatus, applySuggestion, revertSuggestion, appliable`, `attachFlagList`; event `cap:flag-change`; React `FlagList, Flag`.
- **Publish gate** (`.cap-gate`; from `publish-actions.tsx`, `publish-policy.mjs`, `publish-transition.mjs`): one labeled region beside the publish button, failing checks first, required apart from advisory, each row naming its cause and linking to it, states blocked, ready, held and published, one row per destination site. A blocked or held button is `aria-disabled` and described by the summary. `gateState, sortChecks, siteState, worstState, describeGate`, `attachPublishGate`; event `cap:gate-state`; React `PublishGate, PublishButton, publishWithPreview`. A first publication goes through the confirm preview; the server must still refuse an unconfirmed one.
- **History list** (`.cap-hist`; from `revision-list.tsx` `RevisionList` and `RevisionMeta`): one line per change (who, when, signed size change in words, a one-line summary), autosave runs condensed and expandable, named versions and publishes in full, pick any two to compare (`?from=&to=`, older first), restoring makes a new version and offers Undo. `condense, wordDelta, pickTwo, compareHref`, `enhance, restoreVersion, appendEntries`; events `cap:history-restored|-unrestored|-older`; React `HistoryList`.
- **Moderation queue** (`.cap-mq`; from `routes/admin.mentions.tsx`): built on the row list; `j`, `k`, `a` approve, `s` spam, `d` bin, `z` undo (walks back through earlier decisions), `x` select, `r` back to waiting; acts first, then offers Undo; states waiting, approved, spam, bin and source gone (the site's rejected became spam, its failed became source gone; bin is new; a gone source asks before it is approved); Delete permanently only in the bin, with a confirm. `decide, actionsFor, countViews, undoStack`, `enhance, decideRows`; events `cap:mod-decided|-undone|-deleted`; React `ModerationQueue`.
- **Media library** (`.cap-media`, tile, inspector; from `media-grid.tsx`, `media-tile.tsx`, `media-inspector*.tsx`, `media-keyboard.tsx`, `copy-button.tsx`): a grid or list of tiles (arrow keys in two dimensions, Enter, Space or `x`), a details pane that saves by itself (800 ms after typing and on blur, with a live "Saving", "Saved" or "Could not save" and Retry), "used in" links, an unattached filter, copy address, search, sort, and bulk tags and bin. Tile states: uploading (progress), failed (reason and Retry), missing alt text, marked decorative (its own state, not the same as missing) and in the bin. `attachMedia, createAutosave, altStateOf, nextTile, applyBulkTag`; React `MediaGrid, MediaTile, MediaInspector`.
- **Drop zone** (`.cap-drop`; from `media-drop-anywhere.tsx`, `use-image-upload.ts`): a real file input drawn as a drop surface; idle, file over ("Drop to upload"), and rejected with each file's reason; `data-scope="page"` for drop anywhere. `validateFiles, matchesAccept, formatBytes`, `attachDropZone`; events `cap-drop-accepted|-rejected`; React `DropZone`.
- **Bulk action bar** (`.cap-bulk`; from `media-bulk-bar.tsx`, `bulk-tag-controls.tsx`): the selected count as a live status, the actions, Clear; a reversible bulk action runs at once and offers Undo, a destructive one previews every item in the confirm dialog (focus on Cancel); Esc clears; acts only on items in view. `previewItems, performBulk, selectedItems`, `attachBulkBar`; events `cap-bulk-change|-action|-clear`; React `BulkBar`.
- **Markdown editor** (`.cap-md`, `data-cap="markdown-editor"`; from `markdown-editor.tsx`, `md-editor-commands.ts`, `md-editor-toolbar.tsx`, `use-link-palette.ts`, `use-image-upload.ts`): the site's CodeMirror editor as one shared component. The page loads with a real, labeled `<textarea>` holding the text; `enhance()` upgrades it in place and keeps it in step, so a form posts the markdown. Toolbar (one tab stop), Mod-b, i, k and e, heading cycle, footnote, a link palette on the shared popover and listbox, a slash menu for scaffolds (app-supplied data; the site's chart, diagram and figure blocks are the example), paste or drop an image with a mandatory alt-text step (`onUpload` is the app's), word count and reading time, `readOnly`, `disabled`, `required`, `maxLength`, CSP nonce. `enhance, mountMarkdownEditor, getMarkdownEditor` and pure edit helpers; React `MarkdownEditor` (CodeMirror is loaded with `import()`, its own chunk). New **optional peer dependencies** (and dev dependencies): `@codemirror/state`, `view`, `commands`, `language`, `lang-markdown` and `@lezer/highlight` (MIT, the versions the site uses); an app that never mounts an editor installs and bundles none of it.

**New: chart pieces**

- **Series palette and ramp** (`tokens/chart.mjs`, generated into every color file): `--series-1` to `--series-8` (purple #8c5fd2, fox #cc4f0c and teal #008489 first, then sage, indigo, pine, orchid and rose), `--series-text-N` and `--ramp-text-N` (the label that reads best on each), and a five-step sequential ramp `--ramp-1` to `--ramp-5` in the family's hue (lightest to darkest in light, darkest to lightest in dark). `npm run check` verifies, for every family and both themes: each series at least 3:1 against the surface, ground and raised surface; every pair at least 8 CIEDE2000 apart with typical vision and under protanopia, deuteranopia and tritanopia; every companion at least 6 from the status colors; the ramp ordered and its last step at 3:1; every label at 4.5:1. `node tokens/palette.mjs --chart` prints the closest pairs.
- **`css/enarratio.css`** (imported by `tokens.css`; also `capsomer/enarratio.css`): maps every `--enarratio-*` property onto Capsomer's tokens (series, ramp, text, axes, grid, fonts, sizes, spacing, radii, shadow, focus, timings), declared on `:root`, `[data-family]` and `[data-density]` so a wrapper re-resolves it. A test fails if Enarratio's base rules read a property this file does not define.
- **`capsomerTheme`** (`capsomer/enarratio-theme`, `tokens/enarratio-theme.mjs` with `.d.mts`): the same values as hex for Enarratio's `stylesheet()`. Generated by `tokens/palette.mjs` and checked by `npm run check`; CI runs Enarratio's own `checkTheme` on it (`test/unit/enarratio-theme.test.mjs`): no errors; pairs between 8 and 10 apart are its warnings.
- **Chart frame** (`.cap-chart`; `capsomer/behavior/chart-frame` for `frameHtml`, `tableHtml`; React `ChartFrame`, `ChartData`): a title, one sentence that says what the chart shows, the chart, and its numbers as a table behind "Show data" (closed unless `open`), all in the HTML. The stat tile takes `chartTable` (React) or the `data-variant="tile"` frame; the uptime strip is built on the frame.
- **`enarratio` is a dev dependency** (`git+https://github.com/DrDustinEdwards/enarratio.git#1de8bee`, the 0.2.0 commit; not on npm yet) used by the theme check only. CI now installs with scripts so npm builds it.

**Breaking changes** (what an app on 0.2.0 must change)

- _Uptime strip_: it is a chart frame now. The head is `div.cap-chart-head` holding `h3.cap-chart-title.cap-uptime-title` and `span.cap-chart-meta.cap-uptime-figure` (was `figcaption.cap-uptime-head`); the summary is `p.cap-chart-summary.cap-uptime-summary` and comes before the strip (was after the legend); the strip slot is `div.cap-chart-body.cap-uptime-strip`; the numbers are `details.cap-disclosure.cap-chart-data[data-variant="accordion"]` labeled "Show data" (was "The numbers, by day"). Add `class="cap-chart cap-uptime"` and `aria-labelledby` and `aria-describedby` to the figure, and load `chart-frame.css` with `uptime-strip.css`. The `.cap-uptime-head` rule is gone. Tests that find the disclosure by its old name change.
- _Stat tile_: nothing changes unless you pass `chartTable`; the tile then sits in a `.cap-chart[data-variant="tile"]` wrapper and "Show data" is one more tab stop after it.
- _Tokens_: new `--series-*`, `--series-text-*`, `--ramp-*`, `--ramp-text-*` in every color file and `--enarratio-*` on `:root` from `tokens.css`. An app that defined its own `--enarratio-*` properties in an unlayered rule keeps them (unlayered wins).
- _Packaging_: new exports `capsomer/enarratio.css` and `capsomer/enarratio-theme`; `tokens/chart.mjs` is shipped (palette.mjs imports it).
- Nothing else in 0.2.0 changes.

**Defaults changed** (each is on the component's page, and the first two are in `DEFAULTS.md`)

- **Every chart is a sentence, a picture and a table**, the table behind "Show data" (shadcn's Chart has no table: charts are images there).
- **Chart colors are tokens**, with a floor of 8 CIEDE2000 between series, not Enarratio's default 10: eight colors of one weight cannot all reach 10 under the three deficiencies without dull or status-like colors (searched; the best set had a closest pair of 8.8). The pairs between 8 and 10 are Enarratio's warnings, never errors.
- **Tabs activate on arrow keys** (the APG advice when panels are already loaded; `data-activation="manual"`), and the links form is the default for filters. The chosen tab is a fill, an accent edge and weight, because shadcn's fill alone fails 3:1.
- **Pagination shows seven slots with gaps** so its width does not move; a disabled end is dashed and readable, where shadcn removes it. **Breadcrumb folds into a popover of links**, not a dropdown menu, because they are navigation.
- **Autosave runs in the history list are a button with `aria-expanded`**, not `<details>`, so `j` and `k` land on them. A third compare pick is refused, not swapped in.
- **Approving a mention whose source is gone asks first**, although approving is reversible. **The media bin is reversible** (Undo); only Delete for good previews. **The media tile grid is a list with one tab stop per tile, not `role="grid"`** (the column count follows the container). **An uploading tile is `role="progressbar"`**, not `role="meter"`.
- **Authorship is shown by default.** **Draft compare is side by side by default.**
- **The markdown editor's selection is the accent at 28% over the surface**, not `--sel` (about 1.1:1, it would not show where the selection is). **Footnotes are numbered one past the highest**, where the site counted matches.
- **Resolve, dismiss and reopen on a flag are open to anyone who can edit; only Apply is the owner's.** A closed flag keeps its record and is not re-anchored.

**Maintainers**

- `PROGRESS.md` is not part of the release. Tests that only pinned a design value: none were added; none were removed.

## 0.2.0 (2026-10-02)

Every in-scope component is rebuilt to the craft of shadcn/ui's Base UI flavor: its sizes, padding, radii, edges, rings, shadows, every state and its open and close motion. The reference is pinned: shadcn-ui/ui `main` at d75a96ab787f (2026-10-01, `shadcn` 4.21.1), the nova style, with comfortable density taken from vega. Nothing is copied: no shadcn code, no Tailwind, no CSS-in-JS. Each component is plain CSS in `cap.components`, tokens only, state from the platform, with the behavior and the React wrapper rendering the same HTML contract. Where an accessibility rule needs more than shadcn gives (an opaque focus ring, a dashed disabled edge instead of half opacity, a word beside every status), the component's doc page says so under "Deliberately different". Class names, markup and React props stay as they were wherever that cost no quality; every exception is listed under Breaking changes.

**New**

- **Shared building blocks.** `dialog` (native `<dialog>`: modal, backdrop, centered, side sheet, bottom sheet and top palette placements, enter and exit motion, focus return, backdrop click, busy, history entry), `listbox` (options, groups, active descendant, filter, typeahead, selection), `popover` (Popover API with anchor positioning, flip and shift, arrow, delay groups; the tooltip variant) and `meter` (one bar with value, max, tone, threshold and projection). Each has CSS at `capsomer/<name>.css`, a behavior module and a React wrapper. The confirm dialog, detail panel, command menu, shortcuts sheet, shell More sheet and approval sheet stand on the dialog; the command menu, select, combobox and menu on the listbox; the tooltip, select popup, combobox, menu and message on the popover surface; the meter, usage meter and permission matrix bars on the meter. The dialog locks page scroll while any `.cap-dialog` is modal.
- **Density.** `data-density="compact"` (the default, the Portal's values) or `"comfortable"` (vega's spacing) on the root or any wrapper. The shell remembers the choice (`readDensity`, `applyDensity`, `applyStoredDensity`, `DENSITY_PREF`, `data-density` on `<html>` under `cap-density`).
- **Families fox and teal.** `data-family="fox"` (#cc4f0c) or `"teal"` (#008489) on the root or any element; import `capsomer/families.css`, or `capsomer/family-fox.css` or `capsomer/family-teal.css` after `tokens.css`. Both themes. Purple stays the default.
- **Generated states pages.** A component supplies `examples.html` (and `examples.tsx` when React renders it); `node bin/capsomer.mjs states` builds `states.html` from `site/states-template.html` and the doc page's `title` and `summary`. The files are gitignored and the URLs are unchanged. `capsomer size` also writes `test-results/size.json`.
- **Skeleton folder.** `components/skeleton` (`capsomer/skeleton.css`, `capsomer/react/skeleton`) holds the skeleton and spinner moved out of Empty, plus `.cap-skeleton-block` (`data-shape="circle|card"`), spinner `data-size="sm|lg"` and the page spinner (`data-layout="page"`, React `<Spinner layout="page" />`, `role="status"`).
- **Panel.** `.cap-panel-pad` (a padded block inside a flush body), `.cap-panel-desc`, `.cap-panel-action`, `.cap-panel-foot`, `data-size="sm"`; React `Panel` takes `ref`, `headingId` (wires `aria-labelledby`, making the section a named region), `description`, `footer`, `size`.
- **Badge.** `.cap-badge` (alias of `.cap-pill`) with `data-variant="default|secondary|outline|destructive|ghost|link"`; a badge may be a link or a button. React `Pill` takes `tone`, `variant`, `href`, `onClick`; `Badge` is the same.
- **Button.** `data-variant="secondary|link"`, `data-size="xs|sm|lg"`, `.cap-btn-group` and `.cap-btn-group-text`, `data-icon="start|end"`; React `size`, `ButtonGroup`, `ButtonGroupText`.
- **Field.** `.cap-input-group` with `.cap-input-addon` and `.cap-input-group-text`, `.cap-label`, `.cap-field-group`, `-content`, `-title`, `-description`, `-separator`, `data-orientation`, `.cap-check[data-variant="card"]`, file input styling, a textarea that grows with its text. React `Field` `orientation`; `Check`, `InputGroup`, `InputGroupAddon`.
- **Select.** An enhanced popup (`data-cap="select"`, `enhance()` from `capsomer/behavior/select`), `data-size="sm"`, `data-native`, a React `Select`.
- **Combobox.** `clearable`, `ComboboxOption.group`, `ComboboxMultiple` (values as chips).
- **Menu.** `MenuAction.type` and `icon`; entries `checkbox`, `radio-group`, `group` and `submenu`.
- **Banner and alert.** `data-tone="neutral|info|ok"`, a body with an optional title, `.cap-alert-action`; React `tone` (five) and `title`.
- **Others.** Segmented `data-size`, `data-orientation="vertical"`, `data-fill`, `invalid`; chips `data-size`, `.cap-chip-count`, per-option `disabled`, arrow, Home and End between chips; switch `data-size="sm"` and `aria-invalid`; disclosure `data-variant="accordion"` and a height animation; row-list `data-variant="outline|muted"`, `data-size="sm|xs"`, `data-gap`, `.cap-row-media`; table `tfoot`, selected rows, `data-caption="bottom"`, `data-sticky`, `.cap-table-empty`; empty `.cap-empty-header`, `-media`, `-content`, `icon` and `flush`; shell `.cap-shell-group` and `group` on `ShellEntry`; command menu Home, End, Page Up and Page Down and a spoken result count; meter `threshold`, `projected`, `size`, `measuring`, `data-state="indeterminate"`; listbox helpers `filterCommands` ranking hook and a wrapping `step`; shortcuts `.cap-kbd` and `.cap-kbd-group`; `--radius-xl`, shadows, ring tokens (below).

**Changed tokens**

- `--accent` is step 9 of the family scale (purple #8c5fd2 in both themes), not the Portal's #4F2D7F and #a386d7. It is for fills, the focus ring, large shapes and icons at 3:1. New `--accent-text` (step 11, 4.5:1 on every surface) carries text. New `--accent-1` to `--accent-12`; `--accent-soft`, `--accent-line` and `--sel` keep their names.
- New `--primary` (step 10), `--primary-hover` (step 11), `--primary-fg` (`--surface` in light, `--ground` in dark). `--accent-hover` is now step 12, the hover of accent text.
- New `--ring` (= `--accent`). `--focus-color` is removed. New `--ring-width` (3 px), `--ring-soft`, `--shadow-ring`.
- Every neutral, status and soft color is regenerated from the purple family's hue (299.1): values move a few hex steps, names stay.
- New density tokens `--control` (32 / 36 px, 44 on touch), `--target` (24 / 28), `--pad-x` (11 / 12), `--pad-y` (6 / 8), `--pad-card` (16 / 24), `--gap` (12 / 16), `--row-h` (36 / 44). `--fs-label`, `--fs-detail`, `--fs-body`, `--fs-lead` follow density (11, 12, 13, 15 compact; 12, 13, 14, 16 comfortable), as do `--text-size` and `--leading`. `--control-h` stays as an alias of `--control`; `--control-h-coarse` stays.
- New `--radius-xl` (14 px) and `--shadow-xs`, `--shadow-s`, `--shadow-m`, `--shadow-l`; `--shadow` is kept as an alias of `--shadow-l` and looks slightly different.
- Links are `--accent-text`, hovering to `--accent-hover`. `tokens/themes.css` pins the primary, ring and elevation tokens too.
- The `--legacy` mode of `tokens/palette.mjs` and its byte-for-byte fixture are removed; `npm run tokens` writes `color.css`, `family-fox.css` and `family-teal.css`. `npm run check` also asserts the primary label at 4.5:1, the primary fill and ring at 3:1 and accent-hover text at 4.5:1, in all three families and both themes.

**Breaking changes** (what an app on 0.1.1 must change, by component)

_Tokens_

- Text that used `--accent` as a color: use `--accent-text`. Never set text on an `--accent` fill.
- A button fill that used `--accent-hover`: use `--primary` and `--primary-hover`. The primary button label is `--primary-fg`, not `--surface`.
- `var(--focus-color)` becomes `var(--ring)`.
- Anything running `tokens/palette.mjs --legacy` or reading its fixture: gone.

_Anchor bar_

- The current link is `--accent-text` with a 2 px `--accent` bar on `::after`. Styles on `.cap-anchors-list a[aria-current]` `text-decoration` should style the bar.

_Approval sheet_

- The dialog is `class="cap-dialog cap-approval" data-cap="approval-sheet" data-size="lg" data-cap-modal-lock data-cap-destructive`, with an `id` on the description and `aria-describedby` on the dialog.
- Parts renamed: `cap-approval-head` to `cap-dialog-header`, `-title` to `cap-dialog-title`, `-lead` to `cap-dialog-description`, `-foot` to `cap-dialog-footer` with `data-align="between"`.
- The gates container is `class="cap-dialog-body cap-approval-gates" data-flush` and drops `tabindex="0"`.
- A pending sheet carries `aria-busy="true"` (`setPending()` sets it). React renders the shared `Dialog` (`data-cap="dialog"`).

_Banner and alert_

- `.cap-alert` is a bordered box on the tone's tint (was bare critical text); check the layout around an alert beside a field.

_Combobox_

- `.cap-combobox-item` to `.cap-option`, `.cap-combobox-item-label` to `.cap-option-label`, `.cap-combobox-check` to `.cap-option-indicator`. Popup `class="cap-popover cap-combobox-popup" data-flush`, list `class="cap-listbox cap-combobox-list"`, empty `class="cap-listbox-empty cap-combobox-empty"`. Tests and overrides on the old classes change.
- Load `popover.css` and `listbox.css`. The popup and list draw no border, fill or padding of their own; the selected option is no longer bold (it has a check).

_Command menu_

- Markup: `class="cap-dialog cap-cmd" data-placement="top" data-size="lg"`; search `.cap-cmd-search` holding an icon svg, the label (now `cap-sr-only`) and the input; list `class="cap-listbox cap-cmd-list" data-controlled`.
- Renamed: `.cap-cmd-group` to `.cap-listbox-group`, `.cap-cmd-group-title` to `.cap-listbox-label`, `.cap-cmd-option` to `.cap-option`, `-option-label` to `.cap-option-label`, `.cap-cmd-keys` to `.cap-option-keys`, `.cap-cmd-hint` to `.cap-option-hint`, `.cap-cmd-empty` to `.cap-listbox-empty`; `.cap-cmd-label` is gone.
- The active command is `data-active`, not `aria-selected="true"`; read `data-active` or `aria-activedescendant`. Option data attributes are `data-keywords` and `data-group` (the `data-cap-` forms are still read).
- Load `dialog.css` and `listbox.css`. React `CommandMenu` renders the shared `Dialog` and `Listbox`; its `label` is the hidden label and `placeholder` defaults to "Search commands".

_Confirm dialog_

- The dialog is `role="alertdialog" data-placement="center" data-size="md"`; tests that find it by `role=dialog` use `alertdialog`.
- Add `<div class="cap-dialog-header">` (optional `.cap-dialog-media`, `.cap-dialog-title`, lead as `<p class="cap-dialog-description">`) and put the change list, reason and typed-word fields in `<div class="cap-dialog-body">`.
- `.cap-dialog-actions` to `.cap-dialog-footer` (with `data-align="between"`); `.cap-dialog-reason` to `.cap-confirm-reason`; `.cap-dialog-typed` to `.cap-confirm-typed`; `.cap-dialog-note` to `.cap-confirm-note`; `.cap-dialog-error` to `.cap-confirm-error`; `.cap-dialog-error-lead` to `.cap-confirm-error-lead`. The error box sits after the body.
- `aria-describedby` names the description and the list (`id-desc id-list`). Load `dialog.css`; the 0.1 light panel look (480 px, `--surface`, `--line-strong`) is gone. React renders the shared `Dialog`.

_Detail panel_

- `<dialog class="cap-dialog cap-detail" data-placement="right" data-size="md">`; `.cap-detail-head` becomes `<div class="cap-dialog-header" data-divider>` holding `.cap-detail-heading`; the title is `.cap-dialog-title` inside a new `.cap-detail-titlerow` (status pill beside it); the body is `class="cap-dialog-body cap-detail-body"`.
- Close moves to the end as `<button class="cap-btn cap-dialog-close" data-variant="quiet" data-icon-only data-cap-part="close" aria-label="Close">`; the header's word "Close" is gone.
- `data-cap-history="detail"` keeps `#detail-<id>` (an empty value still works for a panel).
- Focus on open is the first control (Close only if none). Width 35rem, at most 85% of the window (was 560 px, full width on a phone). The `.cap-detail`, `-head`, `-title` rules are gone; load `dialog.css`. React renders the shared `Dialog`.

_Dialog and listbox (shared)_

- Load the dialog's CSS before the confirm dialog's only if the 0.1 look must stay; the confirm dialog no longer defines `.cap-dialog*`. Imports of `rememberOpener`, `returnFocus`, `isBackdropClick`, `errorText`, `uid`, `isBusy` from `confirm-dialog.ts` still work.
- A hand-written command menu uses the listbox classes above; `aria-selected` means chosen only.

_Empty, skeleton and spinner_

- The skeleton and spinner CSS moved: load `skeleton.css` wherever `empty.css` is loaded (every spinner and the long wait need it). Names and markup are unchanged; `capsomer/react/empty` still exports `Skeleton` and `Spinner`.
- `.cap-empty` is centered with a dashed edge and `--pad-card` padding: add `data-flush` where it sits inside a panel or table that already has an edge. React `Empty` renders the header and content parts.

_Field_

- A checkbox or radio in `.cap-check` is drawn by CSS (`appearance: none`): remove `accent-color` or size overrides on `.cap-check input`. A mixed checkbox is `input.indeterminate = true`.
- `.cap-input` on a `<select>` becomes `.cap-select` (see Select). `.cap-field` takes `data-invalid="true"` (set by `showError` and `clearError`), which turns its label red.

_Menu_

- `.cap-menu-item` becomes `.cap-option.cap-menu-item`, `.cap-menu-label` becomes `.cap-option-label`, `.cap-menu-separator` becomes `.cap-listbox-separator.cap-menu-separator`; the popup is `class="cap-popover cap-menu"`; a shortcut is `.cap-option-keys` holding `kbd.cap-menu-kbd`. The trigger is `class="cap-btn cap-menu-trigger"`. Load `popover.css`, `listbox.css`, `button.css`.

_Message_

- Each `.cap-message-item` gets `data-kind="ok|warning|failure"` and a leading `<svg class="cap-message-glyph" aria-hidden="true">` (`say()`, `fail()` and the React provider write them; hand-rendered items add them). A failure's edge is critical, not warning.

_Meter_

- `role="meter"` and the `aria-value*` attributes move from `.cap-meter` to `.cap-meter-bar`, labeled by `aria-labelledby`. `data-value` and `data-max` on the root are gone (the module reads the bar's `aria-valuenow`, `-valuemin`, `-valuemax`).
- The status word sits between the label and the value (a `.cap-status`), not in the note. React `warnAt` and `critAt` derive tone and word; `words` sets it; a bare `tone` leaves the word to the note.
- No data is `data-tone="nodata"` with an `aria-hidden` bar and no role. `setFill` is replaced by `setBar` (`toneFor` and `update` are new; `ratio`, `enhance` stay). The default bar is 8 px (`data-size="sm|lg"` is 4 or 12 px).

_Usage meter_

- Each row is `.cap-usage-item.cap-meter[data-cap="meter"]`. `.cap-usage-top` is gone; `.cap-usage-name` becomes `.cap-meter-label`, `-value` becomes `.cap-meter-value`, `-bar` becomes `.cap-meter-bar`, `-used` becomes `.cap-meter-fill`, `-projected` becomes the bar's `data-projected`. `--used` and `--projected` become `--cap-meter-ratio` and `--cap-meter-projected` (0 to 1). Load `meter.css` with `usage-meter.css`. The bar's 1 px edge is gone.

_Panel_

- React `Panel`: `actions` is wrapped in `<span class="cap-panel-action">`; a direct-child selector on `.cap-panel-head` needs updating.

_Permission matrix_

- Cell marks are `<span class="cap-perms-check" aria-hidden="true">` (held: the tick SVG inside, `viewBox="0 0 16 16"`, no width or height; not held: empty). Keep `data-held` and the `cap-sr-only` word. The ok and dim colors on the cell are gone.

_Select_

- `select.cap-select` inside `<div class="cap-select-wrap">` (`select.cap-input` still draws the native form). For the popup add `data-cap="select"` and call `enhance()`; load popover and listbox CSS.
- The native select is hidden with id `<id>-native`; a `role="combobox"` button takes the original id. Focus the button, read the value from the native select (`select.focus()` is forwarded). After setting `select.value` in code call `selectControlFor(select).sync()` or dispatch `change`.

_Shell_

- More sheet: `<dialog class="cap-dialog cap-shell-more" data-cap="dialog" data-placement="bottom" id="cap-more" aria-labelledby="cap-more-title">`, a `.cap-dialog-header` with `<h2 class="cap-dialog-title" id="cap-more-title">`, a `.cap-dialog-body` with the nav and list, and a corner `<button class="cap-btn cap-dialog-close" data-variant="quiet" data-icon-only data-cap-part="close" aria-label="Close">`. `.cap-shell-more-card`, `-title`, `-foot` and the footer Close (`data-cap-part="more-close"`) are gone. React `<Shell>` does this itself; its props are unchanged.
- Rail items are `--control` tall with a 16 px icon (was 30 and 14); the current item is the accent tint; a count is a pill; the collapsed rail is `overflow: visible`. Overrides on `.cap-shell-rail a` sizes need a recheck.

_Shortcuts_

- The sheet is `class="cap-dialog cap-keys" data-placement="center" data-size="md"`; `.cap-keys-head` and `-title` become `<div class="cap-dialog-header" data-divider>` with `<h2 class="cap-dialog-title">`; the body is `cap-dialog-body cap-keys-body`; the switch and note sit in `<div class="cap-dialog-footer" data-align="start"><div class="cap-keys-foot">`. The header Close becomes the corner icon button (as the detail panel's). Load `dialog.css`.
- Every `<kbd>` is a small rounded cap on `--sunken` in the interface face with no outline (was mono with an outline).

_Switch_

- Thumb travel is computed from `--cap-sw-w` and `--cap-sw-thumb`: an app that overrode the 36 by 20 geometry sets those instead of widths.

_Theme switch_

- `<fieldset class="cap-theme"><div class="cap-theme-options">` replaces `cap-seg` and `cap-seg-options`; each label is `<label><input ...><svg class="cap-theme-icon" aria-hidden="true">...</svg><span class="cap-theme-text">Light</span></label>`. The 0.1 markup still works but is unstyled. Words are hidden under 820 px (still in the accessibility tree).

_Time_

- A relative time is `--muted` and an exact time `--text` (its `.cap-time-utc` part `--muted`). Add `data-tone="text"` to keep a relative time in the text color.

_Tooltip_

- The tip is `class="cap-popover" data-variant="tooltip" popover="manual" role="tooltip"` with `data-cap="tooltip"`, optional `data-side` and `<span class="cap-popover-arrow" aria-hidden="true">`; triggers (`data-cap-tip`) are unchanged. `enhance()` upgrades a 0.1 `.cap-tip`, but load `popover.css`: the `.cap-tip` rules and `--cap-tip-top` and `--cap-tip-left` are gone and `tooltip.css` is empty.
- The default side is above the trigger (was below): `data-side="bottom"` keeps the old place.

_Uptime strip_

- An app that set the SVG's height sets `--cap-uptime-h` instead.

**Look changes to check** (no markup change; tokens now drive them, compact density keeps 0.1's padding)

- Focus rings on the rebuilt controls (button, field, switch, segmented, chips) are a 3 px opaque outline; the invalid look adds a soft ring.
- Button: default gains `--shadow-xs`; hover and pressed fills are mixed from the surface; pressed moves 1 px under motion. `.cap-link-btn` is unchanged.
- Row list: rounded rows (`--radius-l`) with a transparent 1 px border; the focus ring is `--ring` (was `--accent-line`), as in the table.
- Table: padding from `--pad-y` and `--pad-x` (8 by 11 compact), every body row tints on hover, text follows `--fs-body`.
- Disclosure: consecutive sections share their edges; spaced with your own margin, recheck.
- Feed, session row, stat tile, attention list: padding and radius follow `--pad-*` and `--radius-l` or `-xl`; an app that overrode `.cap-feed-row` padding, the tile's padding, or `.cap-attention-head` padding sets the matching variable (`--cap-attention-pad`) instead. The feed draws a connector between a day's rows.
- Listbox group labels are plain muted mixed-case text (were uppercase letter-spaced). The highlighted menu and combobox option gains a 3 px accent edge; a danger item is no longer bold.
- Status: the pill is 20 px and follows density; the glyph is 1.15em.
- Maintainers: `components/<name>/states.html` is generated and gitignored; `states.tsx` is now `examples.tsx`.

**Defaults changed**

- **Tooltip delay is 300 ms, not shadcn's 0, and a tip opens on keyboard focus (`:focus-visible`) only.** A pointer crossing a toolbar must not flash tips; a click must not leave one over a button; the next tip opens at once. Esc hides only the tip (WCAG 1.4.13).
- **Meter bar is 8 px, not shadcn's progress 4 px (now `data-size="sm"`).** The fill needs 3:1 and the hatched projection needs height. `role="meter"` sits on the bar, since a meter is a value in a range, not a task.
- **Disabled is dashed and muted, never half opacity** (button, field, switch, chips, combobox, select): half opacity cannot reach 4.5:1 text or 3:1 edge, and a disabled control keeps focus and says why.
- **Focus ring is opaque**, not shadcn's 50%: it must reach 3:1.
- **Chosen and highlighted states carry more than a tint**: segmented, chips, theme switch, listbox options (accent edge, tick or bar), since a pale fill fails 3:1.
- **Native inputs, not ARIA buttons**, for checkbox, radio, switch and segmented: forms, labels and keys come from the platform.
- **Dialog title keeps weight 800; sheets are at most 85% wide** (shadcn 75%), so a record's columns fit with the backdrop left to press. `top` is a palette, not a full-width sheet.
- **Confirm dialog is 30rem and keeps the action at the far end from Cancel**, with focus on Cancel.
- **Select has no scroll buttons or item-aligned popup**, and keeps the real `<select>` as the source of truth, so the content is in the HTML.
- **The message region stays in the page** (a status region) and a lasting message waits to be dismissed, instead of floating, stacking, swiping and auto-closing as a Sonner toast does (WCAG 2.2.1; the one fact the person must not miss must not be hidden).
- **Command matching stays a substring, not cmdk's fuzzy score**, so what matches is predictable; Home and End move the active command.
- **Shell tooltips are CSS labels with no delay**, and the rail changes width at once (no width animation: only transform, opacity and color move).
- **Comfortable density is vega's spacing; compact stays the Portal's.** Nova is the reference style.
- **Skeleton pulse is 1.6 s to 55 percent opacity and stops under reduced motion;** the spinner shows a word.
- **Hover Card is not built:** it is not in scope and a hover-opened popover with interactive content cannot be made keyboard-equal.

**Tests**

- Deleted, because each pinned only a design value: the button control height and squareness; the tooltip "below its trigger" placement; the combobox popup width; the detail panel's edge and phone fill; the key cap boundary contrast (a key cap is not a control); the command menu label contrast (its class is gone); the usage meter's bar edge color and bar widths as layout; the legacy palette byte-for-byte fixture tests (they pinned the Portal's old colors). The contrast-pair count is no longer pinned to 300.
- Added: keyboard, accessibility and behavior specs for each shared block (dialog, listbox, popover, meter, skeleton) and for each rebuilt interactive component, in `<name>.spec.ts` with test names starting `keyboard:`, `accessibility:` or `behavior:`, using axe, `expectContrast` (3:1 and 4.5:1, never exact values) and aria snapshots. Reduced-motion specs treat a 0.01 ms duration (base.css's rule) as no motion. `test/unit/states.test.mjs` checks that every `examples.html` makes a valid generated page.

**Not matched** (on purpose; each is on the component's page)

- Sonner's stacked deck, 4 second auto-dismiss, floating corner placement, swipe to dismiss and slide motion (message).
- Select's scroll buttons and item-aligned popup; Combobox hides its open button once a value is chosen (here both stay); Select separators and labels outside optgroups.
- A Hover Card; shadcn's three dialog components (here one `<dialog>`); shadcn's full-width top sheet; Floating UI and portals (the top layer and CSS anchor positioning replace them).
- Sidebar's animated rail width and its portaled tooltips; its left mobile sheet (the More sheet rises from the bottom).
- Skeleton's 2 s pulse that keeps going under reduced motion; the 16 px icon spinner with only an aria-label.
- Radius varied by button size (here `--radius-m`); the Toggle Group's roving tab index for chips and its `aria-pressed` buttons for segmented and the theme switch; an outline segmented variant and a spacing prop; Mode Toggle's System choice (two states, PR #13).
- A checkbox hit area bigger than its box (the label is the hit area); the half-opacity disabled look; the 8 px slide distance of popups (2 px here).
- The Progress bar's task role (a meter has a known range); cmdk's fuzzy scoring and `aria-selected` for the active command; the permission matrix's interactive checkbox (marks are read-only).
- No stat tile, uptime strip or status glyph counterpart exists in shadcn; they are Capsomer's own.

**Install.** `"capsomer": "github:DrDustinEdwards/capsomer#v0.2.0"`.

### Size (gzip, for information; nothing fails on it)

Component CSS: anchor-bar 1.5 KB; approval-sheet 1.1 KB; attention-list 2.0 KB; banner 1.5 KB; button 3.0 KB; chips 1.2 KB; combobox 2.2 KB; command-menu 1.2 KB; confirm-dialog 1.0 KB; detail-panel 1.5 KB; dialog 3.0 KB; disclosure 2.1 KB; empty 1.4 KB; feed 1.8 KB; field 3.5 KB; listbox 2.3 KB; menu 1.5 KB; message 1.6 KB; meter 1.9 KB; panel 1.6 KB; permission-matrix 1.3 KB; popover 2.8 KB; row-list 2.6 KB; segmented 1.5 KB; select 2.1 KB; session-row 1.4 KB; shell 4.2 KB; shortcuts 1.1 KB; skeleton 1.4 KB; stat-tile 1.6 KB; status 2.1 KB; switch 1.5 KB; switch-reason 0.9 KB; table 3.4 KB; theme-switch 1.4 KB; time 0.6 KB; tooltip 0.4 KB; uptime-strip 1.4 KB; usage-meter 1.0 KB. Every component's CSS together is 73.3 KB gzip and every behavior module together 72.8 KB (`npm run size`, and `test-results/size.json` in CI). Nothing was removed to save bytes; only dead code left.

## 0.1.1 (2026-10-01)

Brings every component that came from the Capsid Portal up to date with the Portal's approved redesign (capsid #214 native dialog and the eight defects, #215 attention list and tiles, #216 switches with reason and Undo, result messages, phone tab bar, Display, exact times, #221 persistent warnings, #222 stops in the command menu). The Portal's current code was read from capsid master (88bf402); each component's doc page now carries a Provenance line saying whether it was extracted from the Portal, rewritten, or both. Components the Portal does not have, or has not changed since 0.1.0 was cut, are untouched. Where the Portal and the audit disagree the audit wins.

**Changed tokens**

- `--top-h` is 44 px (was 56), the Portal's top bar.

**Changed HTML contracts and removed or renamed names**

- **attention-list** (breaking): `arrange(items, { rows, children, groups })` returns `{ problems, more, notices, noticeCount }`; `AttentionListProps` changed with it. The classes `.cap-attention-group*` and `.cap-row-main` are gone: the list uses row-list's markup (0.1.0's rows were drawn with classes no stylesheet defined).
- **stat-tile**: the figure comes before the label, inside `.cap-tile-line`, with the word on that line. The six, three and two column grid is replaced by a compact wrapping row.
- **switch-reason** (breaking): `.cap-field-label` is replaced by `.cap-switch-reason-label`; the exported `question()` is replaced by `reasonLabel()` and `verbFor()`. The reason opens inline, one row, and the app listens for `cap:switch-applied`.
- **message** (breaking): the region is empty and holds `.cap-message-item` children; `enhance()` removes 0.1.0's direct text and buttons. The region is named "Results and failures". New `say(text, { warning })` and `fail(text)`: a warning, a failed Undo and a failure stay until dismissed.
- **theme-switch** (breaking): two choices, Light and Dark, with no System shown (Dustin, PR #13). The first visit follows the operating system and writes nothing; the first choice sets `data-theme` and is remembered. `ThemeChoice` is `"light" | "dark"` and `themeChoice()` is gone (use `effectiveTheme()`); a `system` remembered by 0.1.0 reads as no choice. The markup has two radios inside a `.cap-seg-options` wrapper (0.1.0 omitted the wrapper, so the segmented styles never applied).
- **shell**: the phone tab bar takes buttons; a More button opens a native-dialog More sheet (`more`, `moreLabel`, `moreIcon` props; `tabs` is at most four). New `.cap-shell-settings`, `.cap-shell-page`, rail hints. Top bar and rail sit on the ground color without rules.
- **confirm-dialog**: optional required `reason` field; `perform` receives it.
- **command-menu**: `Command.hint`, `STOP_GROUP`, `stopCommand()`; the group name is searched.
- **shortcuts**: `Shortcut.about` line; a registered Ctrl or Cmd shortcut pressed in the open sheet closes it and runs.
- **detail-panel**: `cap-detail-diff` before and after list; slides out on close.
- **row-list, disclosure, panel, table, anchor-bar, status, uptime-strip, chips, feed, session-row, switch, time**: additions only (chevron and wrapping titles; group button in a heading; panel count and more link; optional drop columns; anchor bar re-measures and builds from sections; brief no-data cell; inline uptime cell; an address value no chip carries stays visible; critical edge on failed sessions; pending switch; exact time parts and an unreadable time).

**Untouched** (the Portal has no equivalent, or its rules are unchanged since 0.1.0): button, select, meter, usage-meter, tooltip, empty, banner, segmented, field (one wrapping rule), approval-sheet, permission-matrix, menu. The combobox changed only in one rule (below).

**Not changed, on purpose.** The audit wins over the Portal on: the message region stays in the page (not a fixed overlay); the status word stays on a phone; wide tables scroll instead of reflowing to cards; rows are links or buttons with a stretched hit area; disabled controls keep text contrast. Not carried: the Portal's scroll-spy rule and fuzzy command matching. The combobox's highlighted option gains the command menu's 3 px accent edge (Dustin, PR #13).

- **Tests.** The release adds keyboard, contrast and accessibility cases for the new behavior; no test asserts a size or color.

## 0.1.0 (2026-10-01)

The first release: the tokens, the shell, 35 components with their tests, and the site. Nothing is removed or renamed, since nothing came before it.

- **Install.** `"capsomer": "github:DrDustinEdwards/capsomer#v0.1.0"`. npm builds `dist/` on install through `prepare`. pnpm 11 refuses a git dependency's build script until the app allows it: add `allowBuilds: { capsomer: true }` to `pnpm-workspace.yaml`.
- **Components (35).** Controls: button, field, select, switch, switch with reason, segmented control, filter chips. Feedback: status, message (an in-page region with Undo, `z` runs it), banner and alert, empty, skeleton and spinner, meter, time, tooltip. Overlays and navigation: confirm dialog, detail panel, command menu, shortcuts and the `?` sheet, theme switch, anchor bar, disclosure and group rows. Data: panel, row list, table, combobox and menu. Console: attention list, stat tile, usage meter with projection, session row, approval sheet, permission matrix, feed, uptime strip. Each has its CSS, a doc page, a states page with every state, and keyboard and accessibility tests in both themes (750 tests on the release commit).
- **Base UI** is used for the combobox and the menu only, as an optional peer dependency: 55.4 KB and 52.6 KB gzip, React excluded. Under `style-src 'self'` the client-rendered components pass; a server-rendered combobox carries one `style` attribute from Base UI, so render it on the client or allow `style-src-attr`. Own implementations are proposed for 0.2 (capsomer/research/base-ui-vs-own.md).
- **Checks.** `npm run check` and the browser tests block a merge on accessibility and correctness; the token check, the size report and the no-bloat report (Knip, jscpd, page weight) only warn.
- **Tokens.** `tokens/palette.mjs`, moved from the Capsid Portal; with `--legacy` its output is byte-identical to the Portal's `tokens.css`, and that is what `tokens/color.css` holds in this release. The family scale of rulings.md rules 14 and 15 (a step-9 seed with an optional step-11 anchor; purple, fox and teal, each checked first in its default theme) is built and checked by `npm run palette-report`: 300 pairs across the three families and both themes, zero failures, and `npm run check` blocks on it. Option 1 of rule 18: the brand color is step 9, accent text is step 11, the primary button fill is step 10. `tokens/scale.mjs` adds space, radius, type, control size, motion, layers, focus and layout.
- **Layers.** Every rule sits in `cap.reset`, `cap.tokens`, `cap.base`, `cap.components` or `cap.utilities`; an app's unlayered CSS wins.
- **Names.** Component classes start `cap-`. Wrappers import from `capsomer/react/<name>`, behavior modules from `capsomer/behavior/<name>`, CSS from `capsomer/<name>.css`.
- **The shell**, extracted from the Portal, with Settings in the top bar.
- **The site** at https://capsomer.dustinedwards.info, a static Cloudflare Worker (rule 16), inside the shared shell: the three color families with their checks, the contrast grid, type, space and motion, and each component's states and test results, read at runtime from the last run on main.
